Abstract: In this note we study binomial orders and coefficient criteria for primitive quadratic polynomials over finite fields. First, we introduce Lucas sequences associated with irreducible quadratic polynomials and prove that their first zero terms determine both the binomial order and the order of the polynomial, thereby confirming a conjecture of Vega. Next, by computing the numbers of irreducible and primitive quadratic polynomials with a fixed primitive constant term, we completely classify the finite fields for which Vega's coefficient criterion $b^2\neq2c$ is valid, answering another open problem posed by Vega. Finally, for finite fields satisfying $q+1=2^mπ$, where $π$ is an odd prime, we establish a unified coefficient criterion for primitive quadratic polynomials in terms of a recursively defined family of exceptional polynomials, extending the previously known criteria for $q+1=2π$ and $q+1=4π$.

</details>


### [167] [Murmurations of quadratic and cubic characters over function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01337)
*Matilde Lalín,Kyu-Hwan Lee,Thomas Oliver,Alexey Pozdnyakov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文计算了函数域上二次特征族和三次特征族的murmuration密度，表明该密度的期望与Frobenius类高次幂迹中的修正项一致，并给出了大亏格渐近。该修正项可用带一个Weierstrass标定点的亏格g超椭圆曲线模空间的稳定同调来描述，并被识别为单层密度中的低阶项。文中还通过替代比值猜想的方法，获得了函数域上二次L函数中心点非零的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来murmuration现象揭示了随机矩阵理论与数论L函数之间的新联系，但对其在函数域及薄族（如三次特征）中的精确机制尚缺乏系统性理解。作者旨在给出murmuration密度的精确计算，并将其与Frobenius类特征多项式迹的修正项建立严格联系，同时探索非比值猜想路线下的非零结果。

Method: 本文采用函数域算术方法，利用超椭圆曲线模空间的稳定同调来描述murmuration密度对应的修正项；通过Frobenius类的特征多项式迹的高次幂展开来推导该密度的期望，并在大亏格极限下建立渐近分析。对薄族（Kummer设置下的本原三次特征）也给出了murmuration密度的计算。非零结果则基于对单层密度中低阶项的精确识别，绕过了传统比值猜想的技术困难。

Result: (1) 计算了二次特征族的murmuration密度，证明其期望等于Frobenius类高次幂迹中的修正项，并给出了大亏格渐近。(2) 将该修正项联系到带一个Weierstrass标定点的超椭圆曲线模空间的稳定同调。(3) 将该修正识别为单层密度中的低阶项，并获得了函数域上二次L函数中心点的非零结果。(4) 对Kummer设置下的本原三次特征薄族也完成了murmuration密度的计算。

Conclusion: 本文在函数域框架下给出了murmuration密度的严格计算，揭示了它通过超椭圆曲线模空间的稳定同调与Frobenius类的高次迹修正项之间的深层联系，并展示了该修正对单层密度和非零结果的影响。这为理解murmuration现象提供了新的严格的算术几何视角，且推广到了三次特征的薄族情形。

Abstract: We compute the murmuration density for the family of quadratic characters over function fields. We show that the expectation of this density coincides with a correction term arising in the traces of high powers of the Frobenius class in this family, for which we determine large-genus asymptotics. This correction term admits a description in terms of the stable homology of the moduli space of hyperelliptic curves of genus $g$ with one marked Weierstrass point. We further identify this correction as a lower-order term in the one-level density and we obtain a non-vanishing result at the central point for quadratic $L$-functions over function fields, via an alternative approach to using the ratios conjecture. We also compute the murmuration density for the thin family of primitive cubic characters in the Kummer setting.

</details>


### [168] [Weighted averages of Bernoulli polynomials and the Gamma function over regular integers modulo $n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01399)
*Waseem Alass,Sumaia Saad Eddin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了模n正则整数上涉及Bernoulli多项式和Gamma函数的加权平均数，基于Kiuchi和Matsuoka的恒等式以及作者先前关于正则整数加权平均的变换公式，推导了一类广泛算术函数的显式渐近公式，并将其应用于恒等函数、Möbius函数、除数函数和Jordan totient函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数论中，正则整数（即与n互素的整数）上的加权平均问题是一个重要课题。已有工作（如Kiuchi和Matsuoka的恒等式以及作者之前的变换公式）为研究这类问题提供了基础，但尚未建立起适用于广泛算术函数的统一渐近框架。

Method: 本文结合Bernoulli多项式与Gamma函数，构造涉及模n正则整数上的加权平均数，将先前的变换公式与Kiuchi和Matsuoka的恒等式相结合，推导出适用于一类广泛算术函数的显式渐近公式。

Result: 获得了恒等函数、Möbius函数、除数函数以及Jordan totient函数等经典算术函数的显式渐近公式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的渐近框架，可以系统性地处理模n正则整数上多种算术函数的加权平均值问题，推广了已有工作并提供了新的应用结果。

Abstract: We investigate weighted averages involving Bernoulli polynomials and the Gamma function over regular integers modulo n. Building upon recent identities of Kiuchi and Matsuoka together with our previous transformation formulas for weighted averages over regular integers, we derive explicit asymptotic formulas for a broad class of arithmetic functions. As applications, asymptotic formulas are obtained for the identity, Möbius, divisor and Jordan totient functions.

</details>


### [169] [Families preserving isomorphisms via techniques in anabelian geometry: with an application to a generalized Neukirch-Uchida theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01417)
*Arata Minamide,Koichiro Sawada,Shota Tsujimura*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从阿贝尔几何的角度重新审视了"保持族自同构"（families preserving automorphism）这一概念，将其推广为p-adic局部域的绝对伽罗瓦群等profinite群中的"保持族同构"（families preserving isomorphism），证明了包括Henselian离散赋值域（正特征残差）和Hilbert域的绝对伽罗瓦群在内的一大类profinite群满足该性质，并以此为工具推广了Neukirch-Uchida定理到l-拟数域的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于重新审视"保持族自同构"（即保持每个pro-循环子群的共轭类的自同构）这一概念。此前Jarden和Jarden-Ritter已证明某些profinite群（如非阿贝尔自由profinite群、p-adic局部域的绝对伽罗瓦群）的每个正规自同构都是内自同构，其中"保持族自同构"在该证明中起关键作用。作者希望从阿贝尔几何的视角推广这一概念到一般闭子群之间的同构，并探索其与Neukirch-Uchida定理的深层联系。

Method: 作者首先将"保持族自同构"推广为profinite群中闭子群之间的"保持族同构"。在此基础上证明了一大类profinite群（包括Henselian离散赋值域正特征残差情形及Hilbert域的绝对伽罗瓦群）满足"保持族同构来自内自同构"的性质。此外，结合对Q的无限代数扩张域的绝对伽罗瓦群中pro-循环子群的精细分析，利用该性质推广了Neukirch-Uchida定理到l-拟数域。

Result: 本文的核心结果有二：(1) 证明了一大类profinite群满足保持族同构由内自同构诱导的性质，覆盖了Henselian离散赋值域和Hilbert域的绝对伽罗瓦群等广泛情形；(2) 作为应用，得到了l-拟数域的推广版Neukirch-Uchida定理，这一结果意外地涵盖了任意数域的最大pro-素到-l扩张域的子域类。

Conclusion: 本文建立了保持族同构与阿贝尔几何之间的桥梁，将这一概念从"验证正规自同构为内自同构"的工具提升为独立的阿贝尔几何研究对象，并通过推广Neukirch-Uchida定理展示了其在算术几何中的深刻应用价值。

Abstract: Families preserving automorphisms of profinite groups are automorphisms that preserve each of the conjugacy classes of pro-cyclic subgroups. This notion appears in the context of verification of the property that every normal automorphism [i.e., an automorphism that preserves each of the normal closed subgroups] of a certain profinite group such as a nonabelian free profinite group or the absolute Galois group of a $p$-adic local field is an inner automorphism, which was proved by Jarden/Jarden-Ritter. In the present paper, we revisit the notion of a families preserving automorphism from the viewpoint of anabelian geometry. We introduce a natural generalized version of this notion for isomorphisms between general closed subgroups of a profinite group, which we shall refer to as families preserving isomorphisms in the profinite group. With regard to this generalized version, we prove that a large class of profinite groups satisfies a property that families preserving isomorphisms of certain closed subgroups of the profinite groups arise from inner automorphisms of them. For instance, the class includes the absolute Galois groups of Henselian discrete valuation fields of positive residue characteristic or Hilbertian fields. Moreover, as an application of this property, together with delicate considerations on pro-cyclic subgroups of the absolute Galois groups of infinite algebraic extension fields of $\mathbb{Q}$, we obtain a generalized version of the Neukirch-Uchida theorem for $l$-quasi-number fields, where an $l$-quasi-number field is defined to be an algebraic extension field $K$ of $\mathbb{Q}$ whose Galois closure $L$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ satisfies that $[L : \mathbb{Q}]$ is not divisible by $l^{\infty}$. Surprisingly, this result includes the Neukirch-Uchida-type result for the class of subfields of the maximal pro-prime-to-$l$ extension fields of arbitrary number fields.

</details>


### [170] [Nine-distance theorem and growth of best-approximation denominators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01443)
*Nikita Shulga*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了平坦三维环面上Kronecker序列的"九距离定理"：前N个轨道点中至多出现九个不同的最近邻距离，证明了Haynes和Marklof的猜想，并推广到d维环面上距离数至多为2^d+1。核心工具是一个关于最佳联立逼近分母的新增长定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haynes和Marklof提出了关于平坦三维环面上Kronecker序列最近邻距离数目的猜想（最多九个数），本文旨在证明该猜想。Dettmann的示例表明九是该上界的最优值，因此确立这个精确结果具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 主要方法是证明了d维内积空间中最佳联立逼近分母q_1<q_2<...的一个新增长定理：当q_{n+2^d}定义时，要么q_{n+2^d}\u22652q_{n+1}，要么指标1,...,2^d可以分成不相交的对{j,k}使得q_{n+k}=q_n+q_{n+j}。由此得出q_{n+2^d}\u2265min{2q_{n+1}, q_n+q_{n+2^{d-1}}}\u2265q_n+q_{n+1}。该增长定理本身具有独立的研究价值。

Result: 在平坦三维环面上，前N个Kronecker轨道点中至多出现九个不同的正最近邻距离，且这个上界是最优的（Dettmann的例子表明不能减少）。更一般地，在平坦d维环面上，此类距离的数量至多为2^d+1。

Conclusion: 本文证明了Haynes和Marklof的三维环面九距离猜想，并给出了d维情形的统一推广。所建立的联立逼近分母增长定理不仅是证明的关键工具，也具有独立的数论研究价值。

Abstract: We prove a nine-distance theorem for Kronecker sequences on flat three-tori. That is, we show that among the first $N$ orbit points, at most nine distinct positive nearest-neighbour distances occur. This proves the conjecture of Haynes and Marklof. An example of Dettmann shows that nine is optimal. More generally, we prove that on a flat $d$-dimensional torus the number of such distances is at most $2^d+1$.
  The main tool is a new growth theorem for the denominators $q_1<q_2<\cdots$ of best simultaneous approximations in a $d$-dimensional inner-product space, which is of independent interest. We prove that, whenever $q_{n+2^d}$ is defined, either $q_{n+2^d}\ge2q_{n+1}$, or the indices $1,\ldots,2^d$ can be partitioned into disjoint pairs $\{j,k\}$, $j<k$, such that $q_{n+k}=q_n+q_{n+j}$. In particular, $$
  q_{n+2^d}\ge \min\{2q_{n+1},q_n+q_{n+2^{d-1}}\}\ge q_n+q_{n+1}. $$

</details>


### [171] [A Coates-Sinnott-type Theorem for First Derivatives of Artin $L$-Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01486)
*Saad El Boukhari*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Deligne-Ribet积分性定理和Coates-Sinnott猜想的一阶导数类比。作者构造了S截断Artin L-函数在s=1-n处一阶导数的秩一首项，证明其满足积分消没性质，并由此构造Z_p[G]上的分式理想，证明该理想（模去来自K_{2n-1}(O_K)的自然挠因子）消没偶K-群K_{2n-2}(O_{K,S})。证明依赖于行列式方法、Σ修正的étale复形和消去辅助Euler因子的对消论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是将Kato的等变Tamagawa数猜想（ETNC）应用于一阶导数情境，推广经典的Deligne-Ribet积分性定理和Coates-Sinnott猜想，建立Artin L-函数导数与代数K-理论之间的深层联系，特别是通过构造积分化首项来刻画偶K-群的消没性质。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 假设相关p-部分的等变Tamagawa数猜想成立；(2) 利用行列式方法构造Rank-One首项；(3) 使用Σ修正的étale复形处理S截断Artin L-函数；(4) 应用对消论证消去辅助的Euler因子，从而构造Z_p[G]上的分式理想并证明其消没偶K-群。

Result: 在ETNC的p-部分假设下，成功证明了一阶导数版本的Deligne-Ribet积分性定理和Coates-Sinnott猜想。构造的秩一首项满足积分消没性质，对应的分式理想（模去来自K_{2n-1}(O_K)的自然挠因子后）消没偶K-群K_{2n-2}(O_{K,S})。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Artin L-函数一阶导数与代数K-理论之间的新关系，在ETNC假设下推广了经典的算数几何结果。该项工作深化了对L-函数特殊值与K-理论之间对偶性的理解，为后续在无ETNC假设下的无条件结果提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Let $K/k$ be a finite abelian extension of number fields with Galois group $G$ and let $n\geq 2$. We prove, assuming the relevant $p$-part of the equivariant Tamagawa number conjecture, first-derivative analogues of the Deligne-Ribet integrality theorem and of the Coates-Sinnott conjecture. We construct a rank-one leading term from the first derivatives at $s=1-n$ of the $S$-truncated Artin $L$-functions and show that it satisfies an integral annihilation property. We then attach to this leading term a fractional ideal of $\mathbb Q_p[G]$ and prove that, up to the natural torsion factor coming from $K_{2n-1}(O_K)$, this ideal annihilates the even $K$-group $K_{2n-2}(O_{K,S})$. The proof uses determinant methods, $Σ$-modified étale complexes, and a cancellation argument which removes the auxiliary Euler factors.

</details>


### [172] [Bounds for Mertens Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01498)
*Samuel Broadbent,Andrew Fiori,Habiba Kadiri,Nathan Ng,Kirsten Wilk*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为Mertens和与积（包括∑_{p≤x} p^{-1}和∏_{p≤x}(1-1/p)）提供了新的界限，在所有范围内均优于现有的指数和对数型界限。主要创新在于对zeta函数零点加权和给出尖锐界限，利用了Fiori-Kadiri-Swidinsky（2023）的技术和Kadiri-Lumley-Ng（2018）的显式零密度估计。主要定理显著改进了Vanlalngaia（2017）的指数衰减结果，并纠正了Dusart（2018）中的错误，同时提出了一个新的精确"Riemann-Guinand显式公式"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mertens和与积在数论、密码学和组合学中有广泛应用，此前由Rosser和Schoenfeld（1962）系统研究。现有界限（如Vanlalngaia 2017的指数衰减结果）不够精确，且Dusart（2018）的工作存在缺陷，因此需要为这些加权素数求和提供更优的界限并填补文献空白。

Method: 核心方法是为zeta函数的加权零点求和推导尖锐界限，采用Fiori-Kadiri-Swidinsky（2023）的技术，该技术依赖于Kadiri-Lumley-Ng（2018）对N(σ,T)的显式零密度估计。通过这些零点加权和的界限，推导出Mertens和与积的全范围改进界限，并证明一个精确的"Riemann-Guinand显式公式"。

Result: 获得了对Mertens和与积在所有范围内均更优的指数型和对数型界限；主要定理显著改进了Vanlalngaia（2017）的指数衰减结果；纠正了Dusart（2018）的错误；证明了∑_{p≤x} p^{-1}的精确"Riemann-Guinand显式公式"；提供了大量实用数值表。

Conclusion: 本文的零点加权和界限技术具有广泛适用性，可用于其他算术应用。结果以利于未来改进的方式呈现，为Mertens型求和相关研究提供了更坚实的基础，并填补了文献中的关键空白。

Abstract: In this article we provide new bounds for the Mertens sums and products including $\sum_{p \le x} p^{-1}$ and $\prod_{p \le x} (1-\frac{1}{p})$ which provide superior exponential and log type bounds for these sums in all ranges. These weighted prime number sums and products were extensively studied by Rosser and Schoenfeld (1962) and are employed in a wide range of applications in number theory, cryptography, and combinatorics. Extensive tables are provided in this article which will be useful for these types of applications. The main new ideas in this article are sharp bounds for weighted sums of zeros of zeros of the zeta function. We make use of a novel technique of Fiori-Kadiri-Swidinsky (2023) which relies on a recent explicit zero-density estimate for $N(σ,T)$ of Kadiri-Lumley-Ng (2018). The bounds and techniques in this article for weighted zeros sums will likely be useful in many other arithmetic applications. Our main theorem significantly improves the exponential decay result of Vanlalngaia (2017) and fills a gap in the literature by correcting work of Dusart (2018). The results are also presented in a way that are amenable to future improvements. In addition, we prove an exact ``Riemann-Guinand explicit formula" for the Mertens sum $\sum_{p \le x} p^{-1}$ that appears to be new.

</details>


### [173] [Algorithmic universal étale $(\varphi,Γ)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01567)
*Zhongyipan Lin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对有限扩张F/Q_p上的连通约化群Ǧ（中心连通，满足p大于Coxeter数），显式写出约化Emerton-Gee栈的多元多项式方程，通过引入模p Weil-Deligne栈来捕捉约化族中(φ,Γ)-模的导出结构，从而算法化计算不可约分支集合，证明等维性并通过不可约分支计数建立晶体提升的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emerton-Gee栈是p进局部Langlands对应中的重要对象，但其约化版本的几何结构（特别是不可约分支和晶体提升的存在性）尚缺乏系统刻画。需要显式的多项式方程和算法化方法来解决这一问题。

Method: （1）引入模p Weil-Deligne栈来刻画约化族中(φ,Γ)-模的导出结构；（2）为Borel和扭转Borel两种版本显式写出约化Emerton-Gee栈的多项式方程，两者联合覆盖整个栈；（3）基于Gröbner基实现算法；（4）通过在刚性解析Weil-Deligne栈与模p Weil-Deligne栈之间插值来推导潜在半稳定分支的数目。

Result: 建立了约化Emerton-Gee栈的等维性；通过不可约分支总数与模p晶体（或潜在半稳定）分量数相等，证明了晶体提升的存在性。算法实现验证了Ǧ=F_4情形对所有F/Q_p成立，以及Ǧ=E_6,E_7,E_8情形对除有限多个F/Q_p之外的所有情形成立。

Conclusion: 本文为约化Emerton-Gee栈的几何结构提供了显式的多项式方程组和算法化的计算框架，在例外型群情形下建立了晶体提升的存在性定理，为该方向后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $F/\mathbb{Q}_p$ be a finite extension and let $\breve{G}$ be a connected reductive group with connected center satisfying $p>h_{\breve{G}}$ (the Coxeter number). We write down explicit polynomial equations for the reduced Emerton-Gee stacks (the Borel and the twisted Borel versions that jointly cover the entire $\mathcal{X}_{\breve{G},\mathrm{red}}^{\mathrm{EG}}$), by introducing the mod $p$ Weil-Deligne stacks capturing derived structures of $(\varphi, Γ)$-modules in reduced families.
  This allows us to algorithmically compute the set of irreducible components of $\mathcal{X}_{\breve{G},\mathrm{red}}^{\mathrm{EG}}$, thereby establishing its equidimensionality, and the existence of crystalline lifts by showing the total number of irreducible components equals the number of (mod $p$) crystalline (or potentially semistable) components. The last step of the arguments is to interpolate the rigid analytic Weil-Deligne stacks and the mod $p$ Weil-Deligne stacks to deduce the number of potentially semistable components.
  An initial implementation of the algorithms based on Gröbner basis establishes the existence of crystalline lifts in the $\breve{G}=\mathrm{F}_4$ case for all $F/\mathbb{Q}_p$, and in the remaining $\breve{G}=\mathrm{E}_6, \mathrm{E}_7$, and $\mathrm{E}_8$ cases for all but finitely many $F/\mathbb{Q}_p$.

</details>


### [174] [On the Petersson norm $\langleθ_ψ,θ_ψ\rangle$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01583)
*Wei-Lun Tsai,Dongxi Ye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究虚二次域K上的Hecke特征ψ对应的theta函数θ_ψ的Petersson范数与Chowla-Selberg周期的比值，证明了在素数p>3下该比值对素理想λ是λ-整的，且对所有h_K个底层Hecke特征取乘积后该比值是有理数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: theta函数作为权为2ℓ+1的模形式，其Petersson范数与Chowla-Selberg周期的比值在数论中具有重要的算术意义，特别是与特殊L值、p-adic性质以及Hodge-Arakelov理论相关。研究者希望确立该比值的算术性质（p-整性和有理性），为相关领域提供理论基础。

Method: 设K为判别式-d<-11的虚二次域，ψ为有限导子平凡、无限型为(2ℓ,0)的Hecke特征。作者通过对θ_ψ的Petersson范数⟨θ_ψ,θ_ψ⟩与Chowla-Selberg周期Ω_K的4ℓ次幂之比值进行p-adic（λ-adic）分析，证明其在λ上整；并通过遍历所有底层Hecke特征取乘积，利用自身与Galois共轭作用的对称性证明乘积的有理性。

Result: 对任意素数p>3及λ|p，比值⟨θ_ψ,θ_ψ⟩/Ω_K^{4ℓ}是λ-整的；对h_K个Hecke特征的全部乘积∏_{i=1}^{h_K}⟨θ_{ψ_i},θ_{ψ_i}⟩/Ω_K^{4ℓ}是有理数。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了theta函数Petersson范数相对于Chowla-Selberg周期的算术规范化性质：逐项λ-整性及整体有理乘积性，这推广并补全了此前关于虚二次域特殊周期比值算术性质的研究。

Abstract: Let $K$ be an imaginary quadratic field of discriminant~$-d<-11$, and for $\ell\geq1$, let $ψ$ be a Hecke character of $K$ with trivial finite conductor and infinite type~$(2\ell,0)$. In this work we prove that for any prime $p>3$, the quotient $$ \frac{\langleθ_ψ,θ_ψ\rangle}{Ω_{K}^{4\ell}}, $$ is $λ$-integral for a prime ideal $λ$ over $p$, where $\langleθ_ψ,θ_ψ\rangle$ is the Petersson norm of the theta function $θ_ψ=θ_ψ(τ)$ attached to $ψ$, and $Ω_{K}$ denotes the Chowla--Selberg period attached to~$K$, and the product $$ \prod_{i=1}^{h_{K}}\frac{\langleθ_{ψ_{i}},θ_{ψ_{i}}\rangle}{Ω_{K}^{4\ell}} $$ is rational, where the product is over all $h_{K}$ of the underlying Hecke characters.

</details>


### [175] [A New Characteristic-Uniform Model for Elliptic Curves -- Theory, Arithmetic, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01675)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了一类称为Cd曲线的椭圆曲线族 (u²+u)(v²+v)=d 的特征均匀理论。该曲线族在任意特征下具有统一的几何框架（光滑(2,2)-完备化、标记边界和二面体对称），在奇特征下与Edwards、Montgomery和Weierstrass模型显式对应，在特征2下保有Artin-Schreier结构。作者在该曲线族上直接推导了仿射和射影加法公式、完备加法律图集、差分加法、Kummer算术、倍点、三点倍加和标量乘法等运算，并研究了自同态、分裂多项式、同构类、有限域均值、保模型同源、Vélu构造及Tate和Weil配对。特别地，对Cd25519的详细特化显示了该模型与Curve25519的兼容性及高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线在密码学和数论中具有核心地位，但不同特征下的模型（Edwards、Montgomery、Weierstrass等）各有优缺点且缺乏统一的处理框架。本文旨在建立一个在任意特征下均有效的椭圆曲线模型，提供统一的几何和算术理论，从而简化跨特征的理论分析与密码学实现。

Method: 以Cd曲线 (u²+u)(v²+v)=d 为核心对象，利用光滑(2,2)-完备化的几何结构、标记边界和二面体对称性构建统一框架。在奇特征下建立与Edwards/Montgomery/Weierstrass模型的显式字典，在特征2下利用Artin-Schreier结构。直接在Cd曲线上推导各种算术公式（加法、倍点、差分加法、Kummer运算、halving、tripling、标量乘法），并研究端射、同源、配对等代数几何性质，最后特化到Cd25519验证实用性。

Result: 建立了Cd曲线族的特征均匀理论，给出了完整的仿射/射影加法公式和完备加法律图集，涵盖了差分加法、Kummer算术、halving、tripling等全套运算过程；在特征2和3下给出了特殊化公式。研究了自同态、分裂多项式、同构类、有限域均值、保模型同源和Vélu构造以及Tate/Weil配对。Cd25519特化表明该模型支持高效的全点与Kummer线算术，且与Curve25519直接兼容。此外将Cd曲线推广到更一般的对称族 (u²+u+a)(v²+v+b)=d。

Conclusion: Cd曲线是一个自洽且计算高效的椭圆曲线模型，在任意特征下都有完整的理论和算法支撑，既可作为椭圆曲线密码学的实用实现平台（如Cd25519），也为椭圆曲线理论提供了一个统一而优美的几何框架。

Abstract: We develop a characteristic-uniform theory of the elliptic-curve family \[
  \mathcal C_d:\qquad (u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d, \] called the Cd curves. The smooth \((2,2)\)-completion, its marked boundary, and its dihedral symmetry provide a common geometric framework in every characteristic. In odd characteristic we give explicit dictionaries with Edwards, Montgomery, and Weierstrass models, whereas in characteristic two the same equation retains its intrinsic Artin--Schreier structure.
  Working directly on \(\mathcal C_d\), we derive affine and projective addition and doubling formulas, complete addition-law atlases, differential addition, Kummer arithmetic, halving, tripling, and scalar-multiplication procedures, including specialized formulas in characteristics two and three. We also investigate endomorphisms, division polynomials, isomorphism classes, finite-field mean values, model-preserving isogenies, Vélu-type constructions, and Tate and Weil pairings. A detailed specialization to Cd25519 shows how the model supports efficient full-point and Kummer-line arithmetic while remaining directly compatible with Curve25519. Finally, we place \(\mathcal C_d\) in the broader symmetric family \[
  (u^2+u+a)(v^2+v+b)=d. \] These results exhibit Cd curves as a self-contained and computationally effective model for elliptic-curve theory and applications.

</details>


### [176] [Factorial residues modulo a prime: beyond the square-root bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01781)
*Xiyu Hu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究素数 p 下阶乘余数集合 A_p 的基数下界，利用代数恒等式构造分式线性映射族的关联结构，结合 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式与 Stevens–de Zeeuw 笛卡尔积点线关联定理，将原下界从 (√2−o(1))p^{1/2} 改进为 |A_p| ≫ p^{8/15}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶乘模素数余数集合的分布是数论与组合学交叉领域的基础问题，此前已知的通用下界 (√2−o(1))p^{1/2} 较为粗糙，作者希望借助现代关联计数技术获得显著改进的指数下界。

Method: 核心方法是利用恒等式 (n+2)! = (n+1)! + ((n+1)!)²/n! 在有限域 F_p 中建立大量分式线性映射关联；经 Cauchy–Schwarz 后，族内映射间的转换映射退化为重数至多 2 的仿射线，最终调用 Stevens–de Zeeuw 的笛卡尔积点线关联定理推导指数 8/15。

Result: 证明了 |A_p| ≫ p^{8/15}，将此前的最佳指数下界 1/2 提升至 8/15 ≈ 0.5333。

Conclusion: 通过将阶乘余数问题转化为有限域上分式线性映射族的关联计数问题，并借助先进点线关联定理，成功显著改进了 A_p 的基数下界，展示了代数恒等式与组合几何工具结合的效力。

Abstract: For a prime \(p\), let \(A_p=\{k!\pmod p:1\leq k<p\}\). We prove \(|A_p|\gg p^{8/15}\), improving the general lower bound \((\sqrt{2}-o(1))p^{1/2}\). The proof begins with the identity \((n+2)!=(n+1)!+((n+1)!)^2/n!\) in \(\mathbb{F}_p\), which produces many incidences for a family of fractional-linear maps. After Cauchy--Schwarz, the transition maps between two members of this family become affine lines, with multiplicity at most two. The Cartesian-product point-line incidence theorem of Stevens and de Zeeuw then yields the exponent \(8/15\).

</details>


### [177] [Integral Representations and Asymptotics for a Family of Areal Mahler Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01951)
*Quanyu Tang,Shu Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文解答了Matilde Lalín提出的关于多元多项式族P_n的扩域Mahler测度问题。通过概率论重述，作者推导出卷积和一维Fourier积分表示，进而证明了序列前向差分具有严格交替符号性质，并给出了n→∞时的显式三项渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Matilde Lalín提出的关于特定多元多项式族P_n的扩域Mahler测度问题，该问题涉及解析数论与概率论的交叉领域。

Method: 采用概率论重述（probabilistic reformulation）方法，将Mahler测度问题转化为概率模型，进而推导出卷积表示和一维Fourier积分表示。

Result: 1) 得到了m_D(P_n)的卷积和一维Fourier积分表示；2) 证明了序列所有前向差分具有严格交替符号性质；3) 给出了n→∞时的显式三项渐近展开。

Conclusion: 通过概率方法成功解决了该Mahler测度问题，不仅获得了多种积分表示，还揭示了序列的符号交替性以及渐近行为，深化了对该多项式族Mahler测度的理解。

Abstract: We answer a problem posed by Matilde Lalín concerning the areal Mahler measures of the multivariable polynomial family $$
  P_n(x_1,\ldots,x_n,u)
  =
  \prod_{j=1}^n(1+x_j)
  +
  u\prod_{j=1}^n(1-x_j),
  \qquad n\geq1. $$ Using a probabilistic reformulation, we derive convolution and one-dimensional Fourier integral representations for $\mathrm m_{\mathbb D}(P_n)$. These representations yield strict alternating signs for all forward differences of the sequence $\bigl(\mathrm m_{\mathbb D}(P_n)\bigr)_{n\geq1}$, as well as an explicit three-term asymptotic expansion as $n\to\infty$.

</details>


### [178] [The Local Four-Square Problem over \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01999)
*Heikki Orelma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究四元数环 \(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}\) 上的范数映射 \(N\)，利用同构 \(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}\cong M_2(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k})\) 通过矩阵方法研究四元数。论文给出了范数纤维大小 \(a_{p^k}(m)\) 的显式公式，该值仅依赖于 \(m\) 的 \(p\)-adic 赋值 \(v_p(m)\)。核心结果是完整解决了环 \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) 上的局部四平方和问题，即 \(a_{p^k}(m)\) 恰好等于将任意元素 \(m\) 表示为四个平方和的表示数。证明纯代数化，仅依赖于矩阵论和 Smith 标准形，避免了抽象数论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的四平方和问题（Lagrange）在整数环上已获充分研究，但在模 \(p^k\) 环上的局部版本（local four-square problem）尚未有完整的显式计数公式。作者旨在通过四元数范数与矩阵表示之间的对应关系，给出该问题在 \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) 上的精确解，并提供一种不依赖抽象数论方法的纯代数证明路径。

Method: 作者利用四元数环与 \(2\times 2\) 矩阵环的同构 \(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}\cong M_2(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k})\)，将四元数的范数映射转化为矩阵相关的代数结构。通过引入矩阵方法和 Smith 标准形（Smith normal form），对范数纤维进行完全刻画，按 \(m\) 的 \(p\)-adic 赋值 \(v_p(m)\) 分三种情形（\(t=0\)、\(0<t<k\)、\(t=k\)）推导出纤维大小的闭式公式。经过同构对应，四平方和的表示数恰与范数纤维计数一致。

Result: 论文给出了纤维大小的完整显式公式：
\[
a_{p^k}(m)=
\begin{cases}
p^{3k-2}(p^2-1), & t=0,\\
p^{3k-2-t}(p+1)(p^{t+1}-1), & 0<t<k,\\
p^{2k-1}(p^{k+1}+p^k-1), & t=k,
\end{cases}
\]
其中 \(t=v_p(m)\)（约定 \(v_p(0)=k\)）。该结果揭示了纤维大小仅依赖于 \(m\) 的 \(p\)-adic 赋值，并完整解决了 \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) 上的局部四平方和问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过矩阵论和 Smith 标准形这一纯代数途径，完整给出了 \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) 上任意元素作为四个平方和的表示数公式。该结果是局部四平方和问题的完备解答，其证明不使用抽象数论工具，方法具有自足性和可推广性。

Abstract: The norm map \(N:\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}\to \mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) is studied on the quaternion ring over \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\), where \(p\) is an odd prime and $k\ge 1$ an integer. By means of the isomorphism \(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}\cong M_2(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k})\), quaternions are investigated using matrix methods. It is shown that the fibre size \[ a_{p^k}(m)=|\{q\in \mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}}:N(q)=m\}| \] depends only on the \(p\)-adic valuation \(v_p(m)\) of \(m\). Explicit formulas for the fibre sizes are derived for every \(m\in\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\): \[ a_{p^k}(m)= \begin{cases} p^{3k-2}(p^2-1), & t=0,\\[6pt] p^{3k-2-t}(p+1)(p^{t+1}-1), & 0<t<k,\\[6pt] p^{2k-1}(p^{k+1}+p^k-1), & t=k, \end{cases} \] where \(t=v_p(m)\) (with the convention \(v_p(0)=k\)).
  The main result of the paper is a complete solution to the \emph{local four-square problem} over the ring \(\mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\): the number \(a_{p^k}(m)\) gives the exact number of representations of an arbitrary element \(m\in \mathbb{Z}_{p^k}\) as a sum of four squares, \[ x_1^2+x_2^2+x_3^2+x_4^2=m. \] The proof is purely algebraic; it relies only on matrix theory and Smith normal form, thus avoiding the abstract machinery of number theory.
  This preprint has not undergone peer review (when applicable) or any post-submission improvements or corrections

</details>


### [179] [Every quasiperfect number has at least eight distinct prime factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02066)
*Akira Toyohara,Ye Tao,Siqiong Yao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出三个初等代数引理（判别式准则、二次剩余筛、多重线性求解器），消除了准完全数搜索中阻碍枚举终止的"深层叶"障碍，从而证明若准完全数存在则其不同素因子个数ω(n)≥8，这是自1982年Hagis-Cohen以来对该下界的首次推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准完全数（满足σ(n)=2n+1的数）是否存在尚属未知，已知其不同素因子个数有下界。Hagis-Cohen提出的ω≥7自1982年以来一直未被突破，原因是搜索中存在一类"深层叶"，纯枚举无法终止（中间素数的扫描界达到8×10^8，指数维度无界）。

Method: 本文通过三个中学代数级别的引理清除障碍：判别式准则消除最后一个素数q，二次剩余筛消除中间素数p，多重线性求解器消除指数维度，从而将非终止搜索转化为有限判定。在此基础上消除"3|n且ω=7"的全部381个茎及其79,751,212个深层叶，"3∤n且ω=7"则坍缩为单一茎并直接消除。计算由三种算法架构（CPU和GPU）的七个独立闭合账本复现，引理层在Lean中形式化验证（259个定理，零sorry）。

Result: 证明任何准完全数（若存在）满足ω(n)≥8，首次推进了自1982年以来的下界。所有381个茎和79,751,212个深层叶均被消除，账本精确闭合，零解。论文还审查了Zemann 2023年的预印本并发现其公开代码存在35个可行指数的覆盖缺口，本文是第一个完整证明。全计算由七个独立闭合账本跨三种架构复现，引理层在Lean中形式化（259个定理，零sorry）。

Conclusion: 结合机器消除ω≤6的情形，得到主定理：任何准完全数若存在则ω(n)≥8。该证明不依赖任何无法独立复核的已发表定理，代码、账本和Lean源码均公开可得。

Abstract: No quasiperfect number ($σ(n) = 2n + 1$) is known, and its number of distinct prime factors is bounded below; the bound $ω\ge 7$ of Hagis--Cohen has stood since 1982, obstructed by a family of ``deep leaves'' on which pure enumeration cannot terminate (the scan bound for the intermediate prime reaches $8 \times 10^8$, and the exponent dimension is unbounded). This paper clears that obstruction with three lemmas at the level of secondary-school algebra --- a discriminant criterion, a quadratic-residue sieve, and a multilinear resolver --- which eliminate the last prime $q$, the intermediate prime $p$, and the exponent dimension respectively, turning a non-terminating search into a finite decision. On this basis all 381 stems of ``$3 \mid n$ and $ω= 7$'' and their $79{,}751{,}212$ deep leaves are eliminated, with the ledger closing exactly and zero solutions throughout; the complementary case ``$3 \nmid n$ and $ω= 7$'' collapses to a single stem, which is eliminated directly, so that the proof does not rest on any theorem whose published record we could not independently re-verify. Together with the machine elimination of $ω\le 6$ (Theorem B4), this yields the main theorem: \emph{any quasiperfect number, if one exists, satisfies $ω(n) \ge 8$} --- the first advance of this bound since Hagis--Cohen 1982. The full computation has been reproduced by seven separately closed ledgers across three algorithmic architectures (CPU and GPU), all with zero solutions and exact ledger closure, and the lemma layer is formalized in Lean (259 theorems, zero \texttt{sorry}). A 2023 preprint of Zemann reported the same bound by a different computation; our audit of its public code found a coverage gap of 35 feasible exponents, so the elimination given here is, to our knowledge, the first complete proof. Code, ledgers, and Lean sources are available from the authors.

</details>


### [180] [Multiple Zeta Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02230)
*Henrik Bachmann*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文是名古屋大学三门课程的讲义笔记，为初学者提供多重zeta值及其变体的入门介绍，包括有限与对称多重zeta值、q-模拟以及多重Eisenstein级数，未来将持续更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为初学者提供一个友好、系统的入门材料，介绍多重zeta值这一数学研究领域及其多个重要变体。

Method: 以课程讲义的形式，系统性地组织内容，涵盖多重zeta值的基础理论以及有限和对称多重zeta值、q-模拟、多重Eisenstein级数等变体的介绍。

Result: 形成了一套面向初学者的讲义笔记，覆盖多重zeta值及其主要变体的基础知识。

Conclusion: 该讲义为初学者提供了进入多重zeta值研究领域的入门途径，且作为活文档将持续更新和完善。

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on three courses given at Nagoya University. Their purpose is to give a beginner-friendly introduction to multiple zeta values and several of their variants, such as finite and symmetric multiple zeta values, q-analogues of multiple zeta values, and multiple Eisenstein series. These notes will be updated in the future.

</details>


### [181] [On the $L^6$-norm of holomorphic Hecke eigenforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02234)
*Chengliang Guo,Liangxun Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意Hecke特征形式f的平方归一化形式F（在权数k趋于无穷时），其L^6模至少以(\log\log k)^{1/2}的量级发散，从而证否了Hecke特征形式L^6模在权数趋于无穷时一致收敛的猜想，并给出了关于6次联合质量的若干结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全纯尖点形式空间中Hecke特征形式的L^6范数在权数趋于无穷时的渐近行为，特别是验证L^6范数是否一致收敛。该问题的研究有助于理解高权Hecke特征形式的分布与质量集中现象。

Method: 对权数为k的全纯尖点形式的L^2归一化Hecke基H_k，构造F(z)=(Im z)^{k/2}f(z)，利用解析数论和自守形式的工具估计L^6范数的下界，并结合Hecke特征形式的算术结构推导其发散速率。

Result: 证明了max_{f∈H_k}||F||_6的阶至少为(\log\log k)^{1/2}，说明当权数趋于无穷时Hecke特征形式的L^6范数不能一致收敛（即存在范数趋于无穷的特征形式）。此外还给出了度数6的联合质量的相关结果。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在高权情形下，Hecke特征形式的L^6范数存在超出有界性的发散行为，否定了L^6范数一致收敛的可能性，深化了对模形式质量分布（mass distribution）及其L^p范数行为的理解。

Abstract: Let $H_k$ be an $L^2$-normalized Hecke basis for the space of all holomorphic cusp forms of weight $k$. We show that $\max_{f\in H_k}\Vert F\Vert_6\gg (\log\log k)^{\frac{1}{2}}$ where $F(z)=(\Im z)^{\frac{k}{2}}f(z).$ This confirms that the $L^6$-norm of Hecke eigenforms does not converge uniformly as the weight goes to infinity. We also give some results on the joint mass of degree $6$.

</details>


### [182] [Mahler measures at interior CM points: proofs of two conjectures of Samart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02255)
*Huimin Zheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Samart关于Mahler度量的两个猜想，涉及7次模形式g_7的L-函数导数与Mahler度量之间的精确等式。第一个是k=1情形（参数在临界轨迹边界），第二个是一对二次CM共轭条目，两处参数均位于或处于关键轨迹内部/边界，作者通过微分比较延拓、Fricke对合映射和精确CM格和计算填补了此前从修正Mahler度量到真实Mahler度量的空白，并用区间算术和41-60位数值验证确保了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Samart提出了两个关于Mahler度量的猜想，但此前只在修正（全纯）Mahler度量层面得到条件性验证（Samart计算L-值侧，Fei处理实部层面）。问题的难点在于参数落在临界轨迹的内部或边界上，使得从修正度量到真实Mahler度量的过渡这一关键gap一直未解决。

Method: 对于第一个定理，作者通过微分比较（differential-comparison）方法沿一条显式验证的路径延拓Mahler微分，再用内部点的连续性论证来闭合gap。对于第二个定理，无需延拓——参数在模参数化下的第二原像是Samart CM点的Fricke对合伴侣，落在已证区域，之后通过精确CM格和计算完成求值。未在此证明的解析与数论输入显式引用；延拓论证中使用的有限数值不等式由区间算术认证，所有恒等式用41-60位数值精度确认。

Result: 证明了m((x+x^{-1})(y+y^{-1})(z+z^{-1})+1)=4L'(g_7,0)（其中g_7是S_3(Γ_0(7),χ_{-7})的唯一新模形式），以及n_2((47±45√-7)/2)=(4/7)(54L'(g_7,0)+L'(χ_{-7},-1))两个恒等式，将Samart的两个猜想提升为真实Mahler度量的精确等式。

Conclusion: 通过结合微分几何的延拓技术与模形式的CM理论，成功将两个原本仅在修正Mahler度量层面成立的L-值公式推广到真实Mahler度量，解决了参数位于临界轨迹处时从修正度量到真实度量的关键困难，所有结论均以可验证的方式（区间算术和41-60位数值）得到确认。

Abstract: We prove two conjectures of Samart as identities of genuine Mahler measures. The first is the case $k=1$ of his evaluations for the family $(x+x^{-1})(y+y^{-1})(z+z^{-1})+k^{1/2}$:
  $m((x+x^{-1})(y+y^{-1})(z+z^{-1})+1)=4L'(g_7,0)$, where $g_7(τ)=η(τ)^3η(7τ)^3$ is the unique newform of $S_3(Γ_0(7),χ_{-7})$ (LMFDB label 7.3.b.a). The second is the
  conjugate pair of quadratic CM entries of his 2015 table: $n_2((47\pm 45\sqrt{-7})/2)=(4/7)(54L'(g_7,0)+L'(χ_{-7},-1))$, where $n_2(s):=2m((x+x^{-1})(y+y^{-1})(z+z^{-1})+\sqrt{s})$. In both
  cases the parameter lies inside (or on the boundary of) the critical locus, so the passage from the holomorphic (modified) Mahler measure---where Samart computed the $L$-value side
  conditionally, and Fei the real-part level---to the true Mahler measure was open. For the first theorem we close the gap by a differential-comparison continuation of the Mahler differential
  along an explicitly certified path, followed by a continuity argument at the interior point. For the second theorem no continuation is needed: the second preimage of the parameter under the
  modular parametrization is the Fricke partner of Samart's CM point and lies in his proved region, and the evaluation is an exact CM lattice-sum computation. The analytic and arithmetic inputs
  not proved here are stated explicitly with references; the finite numerical inequalities used in the continuation argument are certified by interval arithmetic, and all identities are
  confirmed numerically to 41--60 digits.

</details>


### [183] [Samart's conjecture n_4(81)=40M_7: the exact CM evaluation and the two obstructions. A status report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02265)
*Huimin Zheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文档记录了Samart关于Table-6的猜想n_4(81)=40M_7（其中M_7是模形式g_7的L-值）的研究现状。作者完成了L-值侧(P1)的完整证明，即Eisenstein-Kronecker表达式在CM点精确等于40M_7；但通过两点定量障碍和20位精度的直接环面积分，数值上反驳了该猜想（差值约+0.05867，比积分误差高五个数量级），并指出真值n_4(81)的闭式解仍需regulator/monodromy机器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Samart的Table-6猜想是开放内点条目中最简洁的一个（判别式-7、类数1、单一L-值），此前配套论文证明了n_2族相关猜想但放弃将其作为定理目标。本文旨在记录已证明的部分、精确指出方法失败的位点，并对猜想本身做出数值判定。

Method: 本文采用三部分方法：(1)利用CM点τ_2=(7+√-7)/4处的Eisenstein-Kronecker表达式，通过Q(√-7)整数环上的格点求和及主理想(ϖ)的计算，精确证明EK_4(τ_2)=40M_7，并展示了寄生ζ_K(2)项的精确相消；(2)给出两个定量障碍：n_4族的临界像是一个包含参数c=3于内部的二维asteroid盘（对比n_2族的[0,64]一维狭缝），以及Samart的U-级数在上半平面收敛但在Im τ<1/√2区域偏离holomorphic Mahler测度的几何层；(3)进行20位精度的直接环面数值积分判定。

Result: L-值侧的等式(P1)得到了完整证明。但数值结果表明n_4(81)-40M_7≈+0.0586706795972872，比积分误差底限高出五个数量级，因此该猜想的字面形式被反驳。真值n_4(81)的闭式解仍然开放。

Conclusion: Samart的Table-6猜想n_4(81)=40M_7作为字面恒等式不成立；作者确定了L-值侧可证明、Mahler测度侧的继续性方法不可行的精确原因，并呼吁引入regulator/monodromy机制来寻找真值的闭式表达。

Abstract: This note archives the status of Samart's Table-6 conjecture $n_4(81)=40M_7$, $M_7:=L'(g_7,0)$, where $g_7(τ)=η(τ)^3η(7τ)^3$ is the newform of $S_3(Γ_0(7),χ_{-7})$ and
  $n_4(s):=4m(x^4+y^4+z^4+1+s^{1/4}xyz)$. The conjecture is the cleanest of Samart's open interior-point entries (discriminant $-7$, class number $1$, a single $L$-value), and it was dropped as
  a theorem target in the companion paper, where the two $n_2$-family conjectures were proved. We record what is proved and precisely where those methods fail. First, a complete proof of the
  $L$-value side (P1): at the CM point $τ_2=(7+\sqrt{-7})/4$ the Eisenstein--Kronecker expression underlying Samart's formula evaluates exactly to $\mathrm{EK}_4(τ_2)=40M_7$, via lattice
  sums over the ring of integers of $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-7})$ and the principal ideal $(\bar\varpi)$, with an exact cancellation of the parasitic $ζ_K(2)$-terms. Second, two quantitative
  obstructions to the remaining half $n_4(81)=\mathrm{EK}_4(τ_2)$: the critical image of the $n_4$-family is a two-dimensional astroid disc containing the parameter $c=3$ in its interior (in
  contrast to the one-dimensional slit $[0,64]$ of the $n_2$-family), so no continuation path can approach the CM point; and Samart's $U$-series converges on all of the upper half-plane but
  leaves the geometric sheet of the holomorphic Mahler measure everywhere below $\mathrm{Im}\,τ=1/\sqrt{2}$, so the premise of the differential-comparison continuation fails. A 20-digit
  direct torus integration then decides the conjecture numerically: $n_4(81)-40M_7=+0.0586706795972872...$, five orders of magnitude above the integration error floor, so the identity as
  literally stated is refuted; a closed form for the true value $n_4(81)$ remains open and appears to require regulator/monodromy machinery.

</details>


### [184] [An intermediate conjecture between Goldbach and Dubner: every even number is the sum of a prime and a twin prime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02381)
*Tushar Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究介于哥德巴赫猜想和Dubner猜想之间的一个"介于"表述(S)：每个偶数n≥6可写为素数p与孪生成员t之和。作者证明了(S)蕴含哥德巴赫猜想和孪生素数猜想，证明了一个方向刚性定理（n≢0 mod 3时所有孪生成员方向一致），并在n≤10^12范围内彻底验证了(S)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将哥德巴赫猜想和孪生素数猜想统一为单个初等陈述(S)，为同时攻克两个经典数论猜想提供新的研究路径，并通过大规模计算验证以及方向刚性定理揭示结构规律。

Method: 定义孪生成员为t-2或t+2为素数的素数t；提出陈述(S)并证明其蕴含哥德巴赫猜想与孪生素数猜想；通过模3分析证明方向刚性定理（依赖n mod 3确定孪生成员的方向）；利用穷举计算在n≤10^12范围内验证(S)，并统计最小孪生见证t_min(n)与孪生接触哥德巴赫分割的密度。

Result: 对n≤10^12的所有偶数彻底验证(S)；反向验证Dubner猜想与A007534的33项异常列表（仅至10^11）；最大最小孪生见证t_min为14549（在n=571714791706处），小于log^3 n；孪生接触哥德巴赫分割密度约c(n)/log n衰减，常数c(n)中位数4.93-5.01与Hardy-Littlewood预测的4.97-4.98吻合（均值为8C_2≈5.28）。

Conclusion: 陈述(S)作为连接哥德巴赫猜想与Dubner猜想的桥梁，计算验证到10^12，方向刚性定理揭示了模3结构，经验数据与Hardy-Littlewood理论预测高度一致，为最终证明这些猜想提供了有力证据和结构洞见。

Abstract: Call a prime $t$ a twin member if $t-2$ or $t+2$ is prime, and let $\mathcal{T}$ denote the set of twin members. We study the statement (S): every even $n \ge 6$ can be written as $n = p + t$ with $p$ prime and $t \in \mathcal{T}$. Statement (S) sits between the Goldbach conjecture and Dubner's conjecture (every even $n > 4208$ is a sum of two twin members), and we observe that (S) is a single elementary statement implying both the Goldbach conjecture and the twin prime conjecture. We prove an orientation-rigidity theorem: for $n \not\equiv 0 \pmod 3$, all twin members $t$ appearing in Goldbach partitions of $n$ (representations $n = p + q$ with $p, q$ prime) have the same orientation. According to $n \bmod 3$, either every such $t$ has $t + 2$ prime, or every such $t$ has $t - 2$ prime, with degenerate exceptions confined to the primes $3$ and $5$. We verify (S) exhaustively for all even $n \le 10^{12}$. In the course of the earlier run to $10^{11}$ we also re-derive the $33$ terms $\ge 6$ of the Dubner exception list A007534 and re-verify Dubner's conjecture; those two by-products are established for $n \le 10^{11}$ only, the extension to $10^{12}$ covering (S) alone. We compute the least twin witness $t_{\min}(n) = \min\{t \in \mathcal{T} : n - t \text{ prime}\}$, whose maximum is $14549$ (at $n = 571714791706$), below $\log^3 n$, and the density of twin-touching Goldbach partitions (those containing a twin member), which decays like $c(n)/\log n$ and matches the Hardy-Littlewood prediction for the constant $c(n)$: measured median values $4.93$-$5.01$ against a predicted median of $4.97$-$4.98$ (the mean is $8C_2 \approx 5.28$, with $C_2$ the twin-prime constant).

</details>


### [185] [Isogeny graphs of elliptic curves in characteristic zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02494)
*Alexander J. Barrios,Enrique González-Jiménez,Ivan Novak*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对特征0域K上满足End_K(E)≅Z的椭圆曲线E，完成了其同源图G(E/K)的完全分类。主要结果是：同源图可分解为各p-primary同源图的弱笛卡尔积，且每个p-primary同源图（除H_{2^k}^0, k≥2外）都同构于显式边加权图族H_{p^k}^r和H_{p^{∞,+}}^r中的某个成员。证明基于对E的p-adic Galois表示的深入研究，将每个图与GL₂(Z_p)的子群对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线的同源图在计算数论、密码学（如同源密码学）和算术几何中具有基础性意义。此前对同源图的分类仅局限于有限域或特殊情形（如有理数域上的椭圆曲线），对于一般的特征0域上无复杂乘法的椭圆曲线，同源图的结构和分类一直缺乏系统的刻画。作者旨在给出一个完全的、显式的分类，并建立同源图与p-adic Galois表示之间的深层联系。

Method: 主要技术手段包括：（1）将同源图按素数p分解为p-primary子图的弱笛卡尔积，将问题归约为p-primary情形；（2）通过对E的p-adic Galois表示ρ_p: Gal(K)→GL₂(Z_p)进行精细分析，将每个p-primary同源图与GL₂(Z_p)中特定子群建立对应；（3）引入p-adic自适应（p-blooming）不变量I_p(E/K)，作为一个同源类不变量来确定图族参数r的值；（4）对每个可能的同源图识别GL₂(Ẑ)的相应子群并描述其对应的模曲线。

Result: 给出了完整分类：除H_{2^k}^0(k≥2)之外的所有图族成员均可实现为p-primary同源图。引入了p-blooming不变量，并证明具有实嵌入域上的椭圆曲线取得该不变量的最小可能值。作为应用，刻画了具有潜在复乘的椭圆曲线的同源图，给出了从adelic Galois表示确定同源图的算法，恢复了有理同源图的分类，并在GRH假设下将分类推广到某些数域。

Conclusion: 本文在一般的特征0域上完整地分类了无复乘椭圆曲线的同源图，建立了同源图理论与p-adic Galois表示理论之间的深刻联系，并完成了亏格0情形下同源图经由参数化同源族椭圆曲线的显式参数化。

Abstract: For an elliptic curve $E$ defined over a field $K$ of characteristic $0$ with $\operatorname{End}_K \! E \cong \mathbb{Z}$, we classify which isogeny graphs $\mathcal{G}(E/K)$ can occur. We first show that $\mathcal{G}(E/K)$ decomposes as a weak Cartesian product of its $p$-primary isogeny graphs, one for each prime $p$, thereby reducing the problem to classifying $p$-primary isogeny graphs. We then show that each such graph is isomorphic, as an edge-weighted graph, to a member of an explicit family of edge-weighted graphs $\mathcal{H}_{p^k}^r$ and $\mathcal{H}_{p^{\infty,+}}^r$, every member of which occurs as a $p$-primary isogeny graph except for $\mathcal{H}_{2^k}^0$ for $k\ge 2$. The proof relies on a detailed study of the $p$-adic Galois representation attached to $E$, through which we identify each graph with a subgroup of $\operatorname*{GL}\nolimits_{2}(\mathbb{Z}_{p})$.
  More generally, we identify subgroups of $\operatorname*{GL}\nolimits_{2}(\widehat{\mathbb{Z}})$ for each possible isogeny graph and describe their corresponding modular curves, completing, in the genus $0$ case, the explicit parameterization of isogeny graphs via parameterized isogenous families of elliptic curves. We also introduce the $p$-blooming invariant $\mathfrak{I}_p(E/K)$, an isogeny class invariant determining the value of $r$ in the $p$-primary isogeny graph, and show that elliptic curves over fields with a real embedding attain the smallest possible value. As applications, we characterize the isogeny graphs of elliptic curves with potential complex multiplication; give an algorithm for determining the isogeny graph from the adelic Galois representation; recover the classification of rational isogeny graphs; and, under GRH, classify the isogeny graphs occurring over certain number fields.

</details>


### [186] [Additive decompositions of multiplicative subgroups in prime fields: a self-contained approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02568)
*Chi Hoi Yip,Semin Yoo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了素数域中真乘法子群的所有加法分解的一个新的自包含证明，将该分类问题（唯一非平凡例子为4阶子群）的论证进行了精简，整合并优化了Kalmynin以及Rudnev和Tyrrell的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sárközy猜测足够大的素数的非零二次剩余没有非平凡的加法分解。Hanson和Petridis证明了对几乎所有素数成立，Kalmynin完成了证明并发展了乘法子群加法分解的一般框架。这一框架展示了该分类问题的核心价值，值得给出更简洁、自包含的证明。

Method: 论文提供一个新的自包含证明，系统性地精简了Kalmynin建立的加法分解一般框架与Rudnev和Tyrrell的分类论证，以更直接的方式证明了素数域中真乘法子群的加法分解分类定理。

Result: 以简洁统一的方式重新证明了素数域中真乘法子群的所有加法分解均可分类，且唯一非平凡的例子是4阶子群，从而完成了对Sárközy猜想的补充性分类。

Conclusion: 该工作通过精简的论证为Sárközy猜想相关的乘法子群加法分解分类提供了一致且自包含的证明，展示了该领域方法的本质所在。

Abstract: Sárközy conjectured that the nonzero quadratic residues modulo a sufficiently large prime have no nontrivial additive decomposition. Hanson and Petridis proved the conjecture for almost all primes, and Kalmynin completed the proof. Kalmynin also developed a general framework for additive decompositions of multiplicative subgroups. More recently, Rudnev and Tyrrell used this framework to classify all additive decompositions of proper multiplicative subgroups of prime fields, showing that the only nontrivial example is the subgroup of order $4$. We give a new self-contained proof of this classification that streamlines the arguments of Kalmynin and of Rudnev and Tyrrell.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Topological model for derived category associated to sphere with four binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00163)
*Jianmin Chen,Li Fan,Yu Qiu,Yiting Zheng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构建了加权射影线CP1_ω（型(2,2,2,2)）导出范畴的几何模型，将其实现为带四个二重点的梯度球面，并建立了不可分解刚对象与特定弧之间的双射，使得Hom-空间维数由有向交点数计算。该结果进一步推广到聚类范畴，通过带标记弧实现，并验证了与自同构群及映射类群作用的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权射影线的导出范畴和聚类范畴的几何化是表示论与几何表示理论中的核心问题。已有工作（如Ishii-Ueda、Qiu等）对带三点或一点奇点的情形构造了几何模型，但针对型(2,2,2,2)的多奇点加权射影线尚缺乏完整的几何描述。本文旨在填补这一空白，为该类导出范畴提供直观的曲面几何模型。

Method: 作者构造了一个带四个二重点的梯度球面（graded sphere with four binaries）作为加权射影线CP1_ω(2,2,2,2)导出范畴的几何模型。具体方法包括：(1) 在球面上定义适当的弧系统，建立不可分解刚对象与特定弧之间的双射；(2)利用有向交点数（oriented intersection numbers）计算Hom-空间的维数；(3)将该构造推广到聚类范畴C(CP1_ω)，通过带标记弧（tagged arcs）实现不可分解刚对象的几何表示；(4)在加权点为(0,1,∞,1/2)时验证构造与自同构群和映射类群自然作用的相容性。

Result: 成功建立了型(2,2,2,2)加权射影线导出范畴的球面几何模型，给出了不可分解刚对象与弧的显式双射，并以有向交点数精确刻画了Hom-空间维数。同时将结果推广至聚类范畴，利用四个穿刺球面上的带标记弧实现了不可分解刚对象的几何化，且证明了在特殊加权点构型下与自同构群及映射类群作用是相容的。

Conclusion: 本文为多奇点加权射影线（型(2,2,2,2)）提供了完整的曲面几何模型，统一了导出范畴与聚类范畴的几何描述，并揭示了Hom-空间结构与曲面交点数之间的深层联系。该工作为更一般加权射影线的几何表示论研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct a geometric model for the derived category of the weighted projective line $\mathbb{CP}^1_ω$ of type $(2,2,2,2)$, given by a graded sphere with four binaries. Precisely, we give a bijection between the set of indecomposable rigid objects and the set of certain arcs, such that the dimensions of $\operatorname{Hom}$-spaces are computed by oriented intersection numbers. As applications, we provide a geometric realization of the indecomposable rigid objects in the cluster category $\mathcal{C}(\mathbb{CP}^1_ω)$ via tagged arcs on the sphere with four punctures, where $\operatorname{Hom}$-space dimensions are expressed in terms of tagged intersection numbers. When the weighted points of $\mathbb{CP}^1_ω$ are $(0,1,\infty,\frac{1}{2})$, both the correspondences are compatible with the natural actions of the automorphism groups and mapping class groups.

</details>


### [188] [On bricks of one-point extension algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00395)
*Qi Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究单点扩张代数 Λ=B[E] 的混合砖（mixed bricks）性质。利用Λ-模的标准三元组描述，通过单射条件和标量稳定化条件刻画混合砖；在B是E-可见有限的假设下，将混合砖解释为格拉斯曼流形中的轨道，并推导出Λ砖有限性的充要判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数表示论中砖（brick）以及砖有限性这一重要性质，特别是针对单点扩张代数这类由基代数B和扩张模E构造的代数，需要给出混合砖的显式刻画以及砖有限性的判定准则。

Method: 采用标准三元组（standard triple）描述Λ-模，通过单射条件和标量稳定化条件（scalar-stabilizer condition）刻画混合砖；在B为E-可见有限（E-visible finite）的假设下，将混合砖视为格拉斯曼流形（Grassmannian）中的轨道，从而利用几何方法进行建模和分析。

Result: 得到了混合砖的两种等价刻画条件（单射条件与标量稳定化条件），并在E-可见有限假设下将混合砖等同于格拉斯曼流形中的轨道，进一步给出了Λ砖有限性的必要且充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了单点扩张代数混合砖的结构性刻画，并提供了判定其砖有限性的完整准则，为理解更一般代数的砖有限性问题提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Let $Λ=B[E]$ be the one-point extension algebra of $B$ by the extension module $E$. Using the standard triple description of $Λ$-modules, we characterize mixed bricks through an injectivity condition and a scalar-stabilizer condition. Under the assumption that $B$ is $E$-visible finite, we interpret mixed bricks as orbits in Grassmannians and derive a necessary and sufficient criterion for the brick-finiteness of $Λ$.

</details>


### [189] [Affine vertex algebras and an affine analog of Barbasch-Vogan's construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00428)
*Peng Shan,Wenbin Yan,Qixian Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文是一篇基于作者合作工作的综述性论文，旨在解释关于单仿射顶点代数$L_k(\mathfrak{g})$的伴随簇与单模的两个猜想的陈述及其背后的思想，其中$\mathfrak{g}$为单且单连通型的李代数，$k$为高于临界水平的整数层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望通过这篇综述论文，向读者清晰阐明关于单仿射顶点代数伴随簇与单模的两个猜想的具体内容与核心思路。由于这些猜想涉及较深的理论背景，作者意在用易于理解的方式呈现其陈述和背后的动机。

Method: 本文为综述型（expository）论文，基于作者们的联合工作，系统性地阐述两个猜想的数学陈述、关键概念（如伴随簇、单模、简单仿射顶点代数）、以及猜想所蕴含的思想与直觉。

Result: 论文本身并非提出新的定理，而是对已有合作成果中两个猜想进行梳理与解释，帮助读者理解这些猜想的数学内涵及它们在表示论中的意义。

Conclusion: 这篇论文起到桥梁作用，将前沿猜想的抽象表述转化为可理解的叙述，为后续研究或证明这些猜想提供概念基础与思想铺垫。

Abstract: This is an expository paper based on the authors' joint works. The goal is to explain the statements and the ideas behind two conjectures on associated varieties and simple modules of simple affine vertex algebras $L_k(\mathfrak{g})$ for a simple and simply-laced Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ and a integer level $k$ above the critical level.

</details>


### [190] [Zelevinsky Segments and Hyperspecial Branching Laws for All Depth-Zero Representations of GL_N(F)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00941)
*Runze Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文确定了非阿基米德局部域上一般线性群的所有不可约深度零表示在超特殊极大紧子群的pro-单幂根固定的向量空间的完全分解，该分解由Zelevinsky参数显式描述：不可约成分由Kostka数和Zelevinsky分解数控制，并自然地界于由表示的Zelevinsky段及其Aubert对偶的两个分拆之间。一般情形完全由Kostka数给出。对一般深度零Bernstein块，重数是相应单块重数的乘积。此外，论文证明对任意非分歧连通约化群，取pro-单幂固定向量的函子将p-adic侧的Zelevinsky-Aubert对偶与有限侧的Alvis-Curtis对偶交织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是为一般线性群上所有不可约深度零表示提供完全且有效的超特殊分支律。虽然深度零表示的结构已由Zelevinsky分类给出（以多重段参数化），但其在超特殊极大紧子群上的分支行为尚未被完整刻画。作者旨在揭示该分解与Kostka数、Zelevinsky分解数之间的精确联系，并建立p-adic侧对偶与有限侧对偶之间的桥梁。

Method: 论文采用多工具综合的方法：首先对任意非分歧连通约化群证明pro-单幂固定向量函子交织Zelevinsky-Aubert对偶与Alvis-Curtis对偶。对一般线性群的证明综合运用了Bushnell-Kutzko类型理论、有限Harish-Chandra级数、Iwahori-Hecke代数以及Zelevinsky分类。通过将表示分解问题转化为段参数和分拆之间的组合问题，利用Kostka数和Zelevinsky分解数给出显式分解描述，并证明了成分自然地界于表示本身的Zelevinsky段与Aubert对偶段诱导的两个分拆之间。

Result: 论文取得了以下主要结果：(1) 对所有不可约深度零表示，给出了超特殊分支的完全显式分解，由Kostka数和Zelevinsky分解数控制；(2) 分解的两个界由表示的Zelevinsky段及其Aubert对偶给出；(3) 在一般（generic）情形下，分解完全由Kostka数描述；(4) 对任意深度零Bernstein块，重数为对应单块重数的乘积；(5) 建立了任意非分歧连通约化群上pro-单幂固定向量函子与p-adic/有限侧对偶之间的交织关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为GL_n上所有不可约深度零表示提供了完整且有效的超特殊分支律，将分析问题完全转化为组合学问题。其核心结论——对偶函子的交织性质——揭示了p-adic表示理论（Zelevinsky-Aubert对偶）与有限群表示理论（Alvis-Curtis对偶）之间的深层联系，为更一般群的超特殊分支研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: For every irreducible depth-zero representation of the general linear group over a non-Archimedean local field, parameterized by multisets of segments in the sense of Zelevinsky, we determine the complete decomposition of the space of vectors fixed by the pro-unipotent radical of a hyperspecial maximal compact subgroup. The decomposition is described explicitly in terms of the Zelevinsky parameters: the irreducible constituents are governed by Kostka numbers and Zelevinsky decomposition numbers, and they are naturally bounded between two partitions arising from the Zelevinsky segments of the representation and of its Aubert dual. In the generic case the result is given entirely by Kostka numbers. For general depth-zero Bernstein blocks, the multiplicities are products of the corresponding single-block multiplicities.
  We first prove, for an arbitrary unramified connected reductive group, that the functor of taking pro-unipotent fixed vectors intertwines Zelevinsky--Aubert duality on the p-adic side with Alvis--Curtis duality on the finite side. The proof for general linear groups combines Bushnell--Kutzko types, finite Harish--Chandra series, Iwahori--Hecke algebras, and the Zelevinsky classification to provide a complete and effective hyperspecial branching law for all irreducible depth-zero representations.

</details>


### [191] [Cluster Algebras for Bosonic Plethysm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00963)
*Yelin Fan,Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对特征零代数闭域上由对称方与向量空间的张量积对称代数构造的环 R_{ℓ,m}，通过限制和折叠旗标 m-箭 Kronecker 箭图的行列式种子，构造了显式的斜对称化种子 Σ_{ℓ,m}，证明了该环等于该种子的上簇代数（具有多项式冻结系数），并验证了红化序列的存在性。文章进一步研究了 theta 基越过冻结边界的扩张条件（由有理多面体锥 C_{ℓ,m} 刻画），建立了权重纤维与多重级最高权重重数之间的对应，并通过 Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式将对称方 plethysm 系数表达为这些重数的有限交错和，最后通过优化冻结变量给出了该锥的显式有限不等式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为一类源自表示论与组合学交叉的簇代数 R_{ℓ,m} 提供显式种子构造和分类刻画，特别是将其与斜对称化簇代数框架联系起来，从而利用簇代数的 theta 基、红化序列等工具深入了解该环的代数结构、多重权重分解及对称方 plethysm 系数的组合性质。

Method: 作者将旗标 m-箭 Kronecker 箭图的行列式种子通过限制（restricting）与折叠（folding）操作，构造了斜对称化（skew-symmetrizable）种子 Σ_{ℓ,m}。利用簇代数的标准理论证明 R_{ℓ,m} 同构于 Σ_{ℓ,m} 的上簇代数，冻结系数为多项式。通过分析 theta 基跨越冻结边界的扩张，将其与有理多面体锥 C_{ℓ,m} 中的参数对应起来，并利用优化冻结（optimized frozens）方法推导该锥的有限不等式系统。最后通过 Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式建立对称方 plethysm 系数与多重级最高权重重数之间的联系。

Result: 对任意 ℓ,m ≥ 2，构造了显式斜对称化种子 Σ_{ℓ,m}，证明 R_{ℓ,m} 等于其上簇代数，系数为多项式冻结变量，且种子存在红化序列。theta 基越过冻结边界恰好对 C_{ℓ,m} 锥中的参数扩张；权重纤维计数 R_{ℓ,m} 的多重级最高权重重数；Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式将偶次对称幂 plethysm 系数表示为这些计数的有限交错和；优化冻结给出了 C_{ℓ,m} 的显式有限不等式系统。

Conclusion: 这项工作为 R_{ℓ,m} 提供了完整的簇代数结构描述，给出了其 theta 基扩张的精确参数区域（有理多面体锥 C_{ℓ,m}），并将对称方 plethysm 系数的计算归结为多重级最高权重重数的交错和，建立了表示论、簇代数与组合学之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Let $\Bbbk$ be an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero, let $V=\Bbbk^\ell$ and $W=\Bbbk^m$, and set \[ \mathcal R_{\ell,m}=\operatorname{Sym}(\operatorname{Sym}^2V\otimes W)^{U_V}. \] We construct an explicit skew-symmetrizable seed $Σ_{\ell,m}$ by restricting and folding the determinantal seed for the flagged $m$-arrow Kronecker quiver. For every $\ell,m\ge2$, we have \[ \mathcal R_{\ell,m}=\mathcal U(Σ_{\ell,m}), \] with polynomial frozen coefficients, and $Σ_{\ell,m}$ admits a reddening sequence. The theta basis extends across the frozen boundary exactly for parameters in a rational polyhedral cone $\mathscr C_{\ell,m}$. Its weight fibers count the multigraded highest-weight multiplicities of $\mathcal R_{\ell,m}$, and the Jacobi--Trudi identity expresses symmetric-square plethysm coefficients as finite alternating sums of these counts. Optimized frozens give an explicit finite system of inequalities for $\mathscr C_{\ell,m}$.

</details>


### [192] [The double super Yangians in type A for arbitrary $0^m 1^n$-sequences and their bosonic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01031)
*Pengfa Xu,Hongda Lin,Honglian Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入了与任意固定的0^m1^n-序列s相关联的双重超Yangian DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)和DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)，建立了Drinfeld与R-矩阵两种表示的显式同构，推广了量子Berezinian的概念，并给出了level-1玻色子表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yangian及其超版本在数学物理中具有重要地位，而带有符号序列的双重超Yangian推广了这一经典结构。作者的目标是系统性地建立这类新的代数结构的不同表示（Drinfeld表示与R-矩阵表示）之间的同构关系，并探索其量子行列式（量子Berezinian）和表示理论。

Method: (1) 引入与0^m1^n-序列s相关联的双重超Yangian DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)和DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)；(2) 建立其Drinfeld表示与R-矩阵表示之间的显式同构；(3) 将量子Berezinian的概念推广到该代数，并利用它构造DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)的R-矩阵表示；(4) 证明该新表示与Drinfeld表示同构；(5) 利用Drinfeld流生成元给出level-1玻色子表示。

Result: 成功建立了双重超Yangian的Drinfeld与R-矩阵表示的显式同构；通过推广的量子Berezinian构造了特殊线性版的R-矩阵表示并证明其与Drinfeld表示同构；给出了DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)和DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)的level-1玻色子表示。

Conclusion: 这项工作完整地建立了带符号序列的双重超Yangian的代数和表示理论框架，包括两种经典表示的等价性以及具体的表示实现，为后续研究这类代数的表示论和量子可积系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the double super Yangian $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ associated with any fixed $0^{m}1^{n}$--sequence $\mathfrak{s}$. First, we establish an explicit isomorphism between the Drinfeld and R--matrix presentations of $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}^{\mathfrak{s}}_{m|n})$. We then generalize the notion of the quantum Berezinian to $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$, and employ it to construct the R--matrix presentation of $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and prove that it is isomorphic to the Drinfeld presentation. As an application, we present level--1 bosonic representations for $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ in terms of their Drinfeld current generators.

</details>


### [193] [Residue construction of quantized Coulomb branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01379)
*Daniil Klyuev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文描述了量化BFN Coulomb分支在局部化（阿贝尔化）映射下的像，该描述适用于任意规范群G和N，并通过类似Ginzburg-Kapranov-Vasserot构造的根与留数条件给出答案。作为推论，对任意量化锥形Coulomb分支（零或微小味参数情形），提供了Gaiotto和Okazaki引入的球面迹的数学构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化BFN Coulomb分支是数学物理中重要的对象，其局部化（阿贝尔化）映射的像长期以来缺乏完整描述。作者旨在填补这一空白，为任意G,N的情形给出统一刻画，并将结果应用于球面迹的构造，为Gaiotto和Okazaki的物理推测提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 利用局部化（阿贝尔化）映射，将量化Coulomb分支约化到环面（torus）情形。答案以类似于Ginzburg-Kapranov-Vasserot在DAHA(arXiv:alg-geom/9512017)中的“根与留数条件”（roots and residue condition）形式给出。对于大多数情形（如无环的quiver规范理论），该描述对所有味参数均成立；在特殊情形下需选取一般位置或形式味参数。

Result: 对任意G,N给出了量化BFN Coulomb分支在阿贝尔化映射下的完整像的描述（以根与留数条件刻画）；并作为推论，在零或微小味参数情形下，为Gaiotto和Okazaki提出的球面迹(arXiv:1911.11126)提供了数学构造。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了先前关于量化Coulomb分支局部化映射像的结果，建立了与DAHA理论的深层联系，并首次提供了球面迹的严格数学构造，从而架起了物理与数学之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We describe the image of a quantized BFN Coulomb branch $\mathcal{A}_{G,N}^{\hbar=1}$ under localization (abelianization) map for any $G,N$. In most cases, such as quiver gauge theories without loops, this description works for any flavors, sometimes we need to take generic or formal flavor parameters. The answer is given by a roots and residue condition similar to Ginzburg---Kapranov---Vasserot construction of DAHA arXiv:alg-geom/9512017. As a corollary, for any quantized conical Coulomb branch (and zero or small flavors) we provide a mathematical construction of the sphere trace introduced by Gaiotto and Okazaki arXiv:1911.11126.

</details>


### [194] [A uniform decomposition theorem for invariant differential operators on imprimitive complex reflection groups G(r,p,n)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01474)
*Jean Kaboré,Ibrahim Nonkané*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在判别式处局部化的多项式环，作为不变式环上微分算子环的模的模块结构，描述了其简单分量并用Ariki–Terasoma–Yamada的高阶Specht多项式给出显式生成元。证明基于计算G(r,p,n)判别式的Jacobi引理和双重中心化子论证。作为特例（r=2, p=2或p=1），恢复了实反射群W(D_n)和W(B_n)的已知分解定理并大幅简化；还显式处理了G(r,r,n)和G(r,1,n)，附有例子（D_2, D_3, B_2）及其中心幂等元。最后，首次将Nonkané的Galois下降范畴等价应用于G(r,p,n)，给出了简单直和项的第二种、无需生成元的描述——作为扭曲不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复数反射群G(r,p,n)的不变式环上微分算子模的结构，推广先前针对实反射群W(D_n)和W(B_n)的分解定理，提供更统一、更简洁的证明框架，并借助Galois下降范畴等价获得更自然的代数描述。

Method: 采用两条主要技术路线：(1) 建立Jacobi引理以精确计算G(r,p,n)的判别式，结合双重中心化子（double-centralizer）论证来刻画局部化多项式环的模块分解；(2) 首次将Nonkané的Galois下降等价范畴应用于G(r,p,n)，利用扭曲不变量给出简单直和项的第二种描述。高阶Specht多项式（Ariki–Terasoma–Yamada）被用作简单分量的显式生成元。

Result: 完整描述了G(r,p,n)在判别式处局部化多项式环的模块结构，给出了所有简单分量及其显式生成元和中心幂等元。特例中恢复了W(D_n)和W(B_n)的已知分解定理并显著缩短了证明，同时显式处理了G(r,r,n)与G(r,1,n)，并提供了D_2、D_3、B_2的详细例子。

Conclusion: 本文为复数反射群G(r,p,n)建立了统一的模块分解理论，既有基于多项式的显式构造（生成元描述），又有基于Galois下降的范畴论描述（扭曲不变量），两种视角互补，推广并简化了既有结果。

Abstract: {We study the module structure of the polynomial ring, localized at the discriminant, over the ring of differential operators on the ring of invariants of the imprimitive complex reflection group $G(r,p,n)$, describing its simple components with explicit generators given by the higher Specht polynomials of Ariki--Terasoma--Yamada. The proof rests on a Jacobian lemma computing the discriminant of $G(r,p,n)$, combined with a double-centralizer argument. As particular cases ($r=2$, $p=2$ or $p=1$) we recover, and considerably shorten, the known decomposition theorems for the real reflection groups $W(D_n)$ and $W(B_n)$; we also treat $G(r,r,n)$ and $G(r,1,n)$ explicitly, with worked examples ($D_2$, $D_3$, $B_2$) and their central idempotents. Finally, applying the Galois descent equivalence of categories of Nonkané to $G(r,p,n)$ for the first time gives a second, generator-free description of the simple summands as twisted invariants.}

</details>


### [195] [Intertwining Operators for Siegel Parabolics over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01485)
*Nir Elber,Hahn Lheem*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上经典子群G（GL_{2n}的子群）中由Siegel抛物子群P诱导的退化主级数表示Ind_P^Gχ。主要结果包括：(1)该表示总是无重数的，且对一般性特征χ是不可约的；(2)一个特定的交织算子I产生了具有良好特征值的可对角化反三角矩阵族；(3)将I作用于特殊向量可导出多种矩阵Gauss和，其求值隐含了对称及交错可逆矩阵的迹和行列式的显式等分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化主级数表示是表示论中的重要对象，但其在有限域上的结构性质（如不可约性、无重数性）尚不完全清楚。此外，交织算子与组合对象（如矩阵Gauss和）之间的深层联系有待揭示，这不仅能深化对表示结构的理解，还能为对称/交错矩阵的算术性质提供新的等分布结果。

Method: 作者考虑GL_{2n}的经典子群G，以及其Siegel抛物子群P，研究由特征χ诱导的退化主级数表示。通过分析交织算子I的代数性质（产生可对角化反三角矩阵）和组合特性，并将I作用于特殊向量以构造矩阵Gauss和，利用这些Gauss和的求值建立等分布结果。

Result: (1)证明了退化主级数表示Ind_P^Gχ对一般性特征χ是无重数且不可约的；(2)发现交织算子I生成具有良好（规整）特征值的可对角化反三角矩阵族；(3)将I应用于特殊向量后得到多种矩阵Gauss和，其精确求值导致了对称和交替可逆矩阵的迹与行列式的显式等分布定理。

Conclusion: 本文建立了表示论（退化主级数表示、交织算子）与算术组合学（矩阵Gauss和、等分布）之间的桥梁，既给出了有限域上经典群表示的结构性结论，又提供了对称/交替矩阵轨跡与行列式的精确算术分布信息。

Abstract: We consider degenerate principal series representations $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ over finite fields, where $G$ is a classical subgroup of $\operatorname{GL}_{2n}$, and $P$ is the Siegel parabolic subgroup. For example, we show that this representation is always multiplicity-free and irreducible for generic characters $χ$. We then discuss a particular intertwining operator $I$ on $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ and its related combinatorics. Firstly, this operator $I$ produces families of diagonalizable antitriangular matrices with well-behaved eigenvalues. Secondly, applying $I$ to a special vector in $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ leads us to various matrix Gauss sums, whose evaluations imply an explicit equidistribution result of the trace and determinant of symmetric and alternating invertible matrices.

</details>


### [196] [Chevalley-Eilenberg cohomology of linearly reductive Lie algebras in the Verlinde category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01576)
*Pavel Etingof,Serina Hu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征p≥5的代数闭域k上，Verlinde融合范畴Ver_p^+（即Rep_k(Z/p)的半单化）中线性约化李代数g的Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调。证明了g是无不变量的，并给出了上同调代数的显式公式：H^•_CE(g)≅∧^• E_g^*，其中E_g是奇次简单对象的Frobenius扭曲的直和。该结果推广了Borel-Chevalley关于复数半单李代数上同调的经典定理及其在足够大正特征情形的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Verlinde融合范畴Ver_p^+(即Rep_k(Z/p)的半单化)中研究线性约化李代数的上同调理论。经典的李代数上同调理论（如Borel-Chevalley定理）适用于复数半单李代数，而有兴趣在正特征、特别是在Verlinde范畴这一新兴框架下建立类比结果。此外，通过考虑Z/p用主幂幺元素作用于简单李代数的半单化，可以构造线性约化李代数的一类基本例子，需要深入理解其结构性质（如无不变性）与上同调。

Method: 作者首先证明了Ver_p^+中的线性约化李代数g是无不变量的（即单位对象不是g的直和因子）。然后对奇整数m（3≤m≤p-2），定义g_m:=Hom_{Ver_p^+}(L_m,g)并构造E_g:=⊕ g_m^{(1)}[m]（其中(1)表示Frobenius扭曲，[m]表示移位）。主要结果是建立了分级代数同构H^•_CE(g)≅∧^• E_g^*。进一步将此代数与群概形G=exp(g)的de Rham上同调等同，并验证了诱导的Hopf代数结构与外代数上的标准结构一致。最后对非平凡作用的简单g-模V证明H^•_CE(g,V)=0，从而对任意有限维g-模V得到分解公式，并指出这恢复并推广了Borel-Chevalley定理及其正特征类比。

Result: 主要结果：(1) Ver_p^+中线性约化李代数g的Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调同构于外代数：H^•_CE(g)≅∧^• E_g^*；(2) 该上同调与群概形G=exp(g)的de Rham上同调H^•_dR(G)一致，且Hopf代数结构相容；(3) 对于非平凡作用的简单模V，H^•_CE(g,V)=0，从而对任意有限维模V有H^•_CE(g,V)≅∧^• E_g^*⊗V^g。该结果恢复了Borel-Chevalley定理（复数情形）及其在充分大正特征下的类比。

Conclusion: 本文在Verlinde融合范畴框架下建立了线性约化李代数上同调的完整描述，揭示了其Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调具有纯外代数结构，并统一了经典复李代数上同调理论与正特征情形。这一结果不仅推进了对Ver_p^+中李代数结构的理解，还为Verlinde范畴中表示论与上同调理论的发展奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Let $k$ be an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p\geq 5$, and let $\mathrm{Ver}_p^+$ be the even part of the Verlinde fusion category $\mathrm{Ver}_p$, the semisimplification of $\mathrm{Rep}_k(\mathbb Z/p)$. Let $\mathfrak g$ be a linearly reductive Lie algebra in $\mathrm{Ver}_p^+$, i.e., one whose finite-dimensional representations are semisimple. A basic class of examples is obtained by semisimplifying a simple Lie algebra over $k$ equipped with the action of $\mathbb Z/p$ by a principal unipotent element, when $p$ exceeds its Coxeter number. We prove that $\mathfrak g$ is invariantless, i.e., that the unit object is not a summand of $\mathfrak g$. For odd $m$ with $3\leq m\leq p-2$, set $\mathfrak{g}_m:=\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathrm{Ver}_p^+}(L_m,\mathfrak g)$ and $E_{\mathfrak g}:=\bigoplus_{3\leq m\leq p-2,\ m\ {\rm odd}}\mathfrak g_m^{(1)}[m]$, where $(1)$ denotes Frobenius twist. Our main result is an isomorphism of graded algebras $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g)\cong\bigwedge^\bullet E_{\mathfrak g}^*$. We also identify this algebra with the de Rham cohomology $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{dR}}(G)$ of the group scheme $G=\exp(\mathfrak g)$ and show that the induced graded Hopf algebra structure agrees with the standard one on the exterior algebra. Moreover, if $V$ is a simple $\mathfrak g$-module on which $\mathfrak g$ acts nontrivially, then $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g,V)=0$. Hence for every finite-dimensional $\mathfrak g$-module $V$ one has $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g,V)\cong\bigwedge^\bullet E_{\mathfrak g}^*\otimes V^{\mathfrak g}$. This recovers the theorem of Borel and Chevalley on the cohomology of complex semisimple Lie algebras and its analogue in sufficiently large positive characteristic.

</details>


### [197] [Hecke algebra representations from the Katz-Long-Moody construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01727)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Katz-Long-Moody (KLM)构造，精确分类了所得辫群表示何时可分解通过Hecke代数，并给出了何时分解通过Temperley-Lieb代数的精确准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解KLM构造得到的辫群表示何时能通过Hecke代数或Temperley-Lieb代数分解，是连接几何构造与代数表示理论的关键问题，对辫群表示论和对偶性理论具有重要意义。

Method: 对KLM构造产生的辫群表示进行系统分类，分析自由群部分特征值的条件，并针对两股辫的特殊一族进行单独处理，同时推导分解通过Temperley-Lieb代数的精确判定准则。

Result: 除了一族例外的两股辫情形外，表示能否通过Hecke代数分解仅取决于自由群部分的特征值，与构造参数无关；同时给出了分解通过Temperley-Lieb代数的精确准则。

Conclusion: KLM构造中辫群表示分解通过Hecke代数的性质在很大程度上由自由群部分的特征值决定且独立于构造参数，仅存在一例两股辫的例外情形；Temperley-Lieb分解也有明确的判定条件。

Abstract: We study the Katz-Long-Moody (KLM) construction and classify exactly when the resulting braid group representations factor through Hecke algebras. We show that, except for one exceptional two-strand family, this property depends only on the eigenvalues of the free-group part and is independent of the construction parameter. We also give an exact criterion for when the resulting representations factor through Temperley-Lieb algebras.

</details>


### [198] [Some remarks on realizations defining diagrammatic Hecke categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02187)
*Amit Hazi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了图式Hecke范畴的构建中，Demazure满射性和抛物性质这两个常见假设实际上是不必要的。同时给出了非平衡实现中旋转标量的非归纳式描述，以及一个更简单的平衡性判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图式Hecke范畴由实现（realization）构造，实现对Coxeter群的反射表示进行了推广。为了确保构造出的范畴性质良好，通常需要对实现施加Demazure满射性和抛物性质等假设（后者用于反球商）。作者想探究这些假设是否真的必要，并简化相关判定和描述。

Method: 本文通过分析图式Hecke范畴的构造过程，证明Demazure满射性和抛物性质对于保证范畴的良好行为并非必要条件。此外，作者为非平衡实现中的旋转标量（rotational scalars）给出了非归纳式的描述，并提出了一个更简化的平衡性（balancedness）判定准则。

Result: 证明了Demazure满射性和抛物性质这两个常见假设是多余的。给出了旋转标量的非归纳式刻画方法，并提出了更简便的平衡性检验准则。

Conclusion: 图式Hecke范畴在更一般的实现条件下依然能保持良好的性质，本文的简化结果降低了应用这些范畴时对实现的限制要求。

Abstract: Every diagrammatic Hecke category is constructed from a realization, which generalizes the notion of a reflection representation of a Coxeter group. Common assumptions on realizations to ensure that the resulting categories are well behaved include Demazure surjectivity and the parabolic property (for anti-spherical quotients). We show that these assumptions are in fact unnecessary. We also give a non-inductive description of the rotational scalars for unbalanced realizations, as well as a simpler criterion to check for balancedness.

</details>


### [199] [On a question by G. Navarro on the character field generation of odd-degree characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02266)
*Ulrich Thiel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对Navarro提出的一个公开问题给出反例：有限群的奇次数特征的字符域不一定能由单个特征值生成，该反例同时适用于不可解群和可解群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navarro在2023年的一篇文章中提出如下问题：有限群的奇次数（odd degree）特征，其字符域（character field）是否总能由该特征的某个单值（single character value）生成？这个问题的答案在此之前是未知的，本文旨在检验该猜想是否成立。

Method: 通过构造具体的显式反例（explicit examples），分别针对不可解群（non-solvable groups）和可解群（solvable groups）两类有限群进行验证，展示Navarro问题的答案为否定。

Result: 作者给出了具体的反例，表明存在有限群（既包括不可解群也包括可解群），其中存在奇次数特征，但其字符域无法由该特征的任何一个单独特征值生成，从而否定了Navarro的猜想。

Conclusion: Navarro提出的"奇次数特征的字符域由单个特征值生成"这一猜想在一般情况下不成立，无论对于不可解群还是可解群都可通过显式反例予以反驳。

Abstract: In [Publ. Mat. 67 (2023), no. 1, 173-198], G. Navarro asked whether the character field of an odd-degree character of a finite group is generated by a single character value. We show by explicit examples that the answer is negative in general, for non-solvable as well as solvable groups.

</details>


### [200] [Associated graded modules for modular representations of quaternion algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28748)
*Jiusi Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限非分歧Q_p扩张K上的非分裂四元数代数D，计算D^×在Jacquet–Langlands对应中预测表示的対合（dual）的伴随分次模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacquet–Langlands对应给出了GL_n与四元数代数D^×之间表示的一种对应关系，但对于预测表示的具体结构（尤其是分次模结构）尚缺乏显式计算。本文旨在填补这一空白，为理解该对应中表示的双侧结构提供代数工具。

Method: 设K为Q_p的有限非分歧扩张，D为K上的非分裂四元数代数。作者通过代数与表示论方法，具体构造并计算了D^×预测表示的對合所对应的伴随分次模，利用Jacquet–Langlands对应的预测性质进行推导。

Result: 给出了这些预测表示的對合之伴随分次模的完整计算方法与显式实现。

Conclusion: 该计算结果为Jacquet–Langlands对应中预测表示的结构提供了更精细的代数描述，有助于深入理解四元数代数表示与GL_n表示之间的对应关系。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a finite unramified extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ and $D$ the non-split quaternion algebra over $K$. We compute the associated graded modules of the duals of the predicted representations of $D^\times$ occurring in the Jacquet--Langlands correspondence.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Exact Algorithms for Minimum Steiner Point Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00795)
*Eungyu Woo,Donghoon Shin*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带最小斯坦纳点数和边长界的斯坦纳树问题（L_p度量，p∈Q≥1∪{∞}），提出了一种与k无关的确定性精确算法，可在n^{O(n)}时间内计算最优隐式表示（包含完整斯坦纳拓扑、精确分支坐标及每条拓扑边的段数），细分需额外Θ(n+k)时间。可行段数向量是O(n)维凸投影的整数点，通过连续松弛将整数最优限制在2n-3个连续值内，结合半代数例程和晶格坐标平坦度递归求解，最终给出min{n^{O(n)}, k^{O(k)}n^{O(1)}}的价值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统斯坦纳树问题要求最小化总长度，而本文关注的是在边长有界（每条边长度不超过R）的条件下最小化斯坦纳点数。该问题的解k不被n所界定，即使仅有两个终端（二终端情形）也可能需要任意多的斯坦纳点，这使得标准算法框架（依赖于k的指数复杂度）在实际中可能失效，需要设计独立于k的算法。

Method: 算法采用确定性精确方法，核心思路为：(1) 枚举所有完整斯坦纳拓扑（full Steiner topology）；(2) 对每个拓扑，将可行段数向量建模为O(n)维空间中一个凸投影的整数点集合；(3) 通过连续松弛证明整数最优落在2n-3个连续值区间内；(4) 利用精确半代数例程和晶格坐标系统中的平坦度递归（flatness recursion）判定这些候选值；(5) 与Bandyapadhyay等人的参数化瓶颈算法结合，导出统一的价值上界。

Result: 算法在n^{O(n)}时间内输出最优隐式表示（独立于k），包含完整斯坦纳拓扑、精确分支坐标及各拓扑边的段数；实际细分所需的额外时间为Θ(n+k)。对于本文考虑的所有固定度量（L_p，p∈Q≥1∪{∞}），问题价值上界为min{n^{O(n)}, k^{O(k)}n^{O(1)}}。

Conclusion: 本文为带最小斯坦纳点数和边长界的斯坦纳树问题提供了首个与k无关的确定性精确算法，将问题的计算瓶颈从k转移到n。通过凸投影几何、连续松弛和晶格平坦度递归的组合，在多项式维数空间实现了有效刻画，并为该问题建立了双参数（n和k）视角下的复杂度上界。

Abstract: Given distinct terminals $P\subset R^2$ and $R>0$, the Steiner tree problem with minimum number of Steiner points and bounded edge length asks for a straight line tree spanning $P$, with every edge of length at most $R$, that minimizes the number of Steiner points. Length is measured in a fixed $L_p$ metric with $p\in Q_{\ge 1}\cup\{\infty\}$. The optimum $k$ is not bounded by $n$, even in two-terminal case.
  We give a deterministic exact algorithm that computes an optimal implicit representation in $n^{O(n)}$ time, independent of $k$, in the computation model of Section~\ref{subseccomputation}. The representation consists of a full Steiner topology, exact branch coordinates, and a segment count for each topology edge. Subdivision requires additional time $Θ(n+k)$.
  For each full Steiner topology, the feasible segment count vectors are the integer points of a convex projection in $O(n)$ dimensions. A continuous relaxation restricts the integer optimum to $2n-3$ consecutive values. Exact semialgebraic routines and a flatness recursion in integral lattice coordinates decide these values. Together with the parameterized bottleneck algorithm of Bandyapadhyay et al., this gives the value bound $\min\{n^{O(n)}, k^{O(k)}n^{O(1)}\}$ for every fixed metric considered here.

</details>


### [202] [Algorithms for Connectivity Maintenance and Barrier Coverage on a Closed Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01307)
*Shimin Li*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了闭环上无线网络连通性维护问题：给定n个分布在闭环上的点，通过沿环移动点使得任意相邻点对在双向路径上均直接连通，同时最小化所有点的最大移动距离。提出了线性时间最优算法，并进一步改进为字典序最优解，时间复杂度不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于移动无线网络的实际应用，包括沿闭合轨道运行的传感器、车辆和卫星，以及边界/屏障覆盖问题中传感器沿闭合边界重新部署的场景。如何在闭环上以最小最大移动代价达成网络的连通性是一个具有理论和实践价值的问题。

Method: 论文设计了一个线性时间最优算法来解决该问题，即在闭环上移动点使得相邻点对在环的两个方向上均直接连通（距离不超过r），并最小化所有点的最大移动距离。在此基础上，作者进一步精化算法，在不增加时间复杂度的前提下获得字典序最优解。

Result: 提出了一个线性时间（O(n)）的最优算法，并通过对算法的精化，在保持相同时间复杂度的条件下获得了字典序最优解。

Conclusion: 该问题存在线性时间最优算法，且可以在不增加时间复杂度的前提下得到字典序最优解，为该类闭环网络连通维护问题提供了高效且优越的解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of maintaining connectivity of a wireless network on a closed cycle is studied in this paper. In the initial input, we have $n$ points located on a closed cycle. The points can move along the cycle, and if the distance between two points is at most a given value $r$, we say these two points are connected. The goal of the problem is to move the points along the cycle such that any adjacent pair of points is directly connected--i.e., there exist two paths between them in opposite directions along the cycle--while minimizing the maximum movement over all points. This problem is motivated by applications in mobile wireless networks, including sensors, vehicles, and satellites operating on closed orbits. It is also applicable to barrier or border coverage problems, where sensors are deployed along a closed boundary and coverage is achieved through repositioning along the cycle. We present a linear time optimal algorithm for this problem. Then we refine the algorithm to obtain a lexicographically optimal solution without increasing the time complexity.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Truncated Differentiation for Inverse Potential MFGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00217)
*Siting Liu,Yat Tin Chow,Samy Wu Fung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究逆势平均场博弈（MFGs），从观测到的种群密度推断未知的空间逆代价（迁移率）映射。提出Jacobian-free反向传播方法（JFB-r），仅记录预热分离后最后r次迭代，同时保留完整前向求解。通过证明精确近端对偶外推PDHG映射是最大单调KKT算子的度量预解，表明JFB-r精确微分有限轨迹代理，并收敛到隐式梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆平均场博弈中的参数推断问题具有重要应用价值，但传统全展开反向传播需要存储所有前向迭代历史，导致内存和计算开销巨大。需要一种既能精确反演代价映射又节省计算资源的方法。

Method: 采用预处理原-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法求解前向MFG，提出JFB-r方法：从分离的热启动出发，仅记录最后r次迭代用于反向传播，但保留完整前向求解。理论分析证明精确近端对偶外推PDHG映射是最大单调KKT算子的度量预解，从而揭示JFB-r的截断微分行为的理论性质。

Result: 在多个逆MFG设置中，数值实验表明JFB-r在适度跟踪深度下能达到与全展开相当的重建精度，同时大幅降低内存占用和运行时间。

Conclusion: JFB-r为逆平均场博弈中的代价映射推断提供了一种高效的计算方案，兼顾精度与资源效率，其理论分析为截断微分方法提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We study inverse potential mean-field games (MFGs), in which an unknown spatial inverse-cost (mobility) map is inferred from observed population densities. We solve the forward MFG with a preconditioned primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method and develop Jacobian-free backpropagation (JFB-$r$), which records only the final $r$ iterations from a detached warm start while retaining the full forward solve. To analyze this truncated differentiation method, we show that the exact-proximal dual-extrapolated PDHG map is a metric resolvent of the maximal monotone KKT operator. This resolvent view shows that JFB-$r$ exactly differentiates a finite-trajectory surrogate and, under a locally fixed active set at an exact equilibrium detach point, converges to the implicit gradient as the tracked depth increases. Across several inverse-MFG settings, numerical experiments show that JFB-r at moderate tracked depths can achieve recovery accuracy comparable to full unrolling while reducing memory and runtime.

</details>


### [204] [Residual Centering and Error Bounds for Convex Approximation of Mixed-Integer Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00245)
*Alban Kryeziu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两阶段随机规划中混合整数补偿函数的凸近似问题，提出了基于坐标切片中心化的确定性误差界，并通过交换坐标投影算子处理精确中心化与凸性不兼容的情况，得到缺陷调整后的误差证书与决策质量保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段随机规划中混合整数补偿函数非凸，使得问题求解困难。需要构造凸近似并量化其误差，以同时保证近似精度（误差证书）和首阶段决策质量（决策质量保证）。

Method: 在有界不确定集上识别残差条件（核心为坐标切片中心化）以获得符号期望残差的确定性界；通过分部积分得到各向异性全变差界和混合导数界（全坐标情形用Vitali变差表达）；针对精确中心化与凸性不兼容的问题，利用交换坐标投影算子将任意残差分解为中心化分量和显式切片均值缺陷；推导缺陷调整界，并发展张量积与几何扩展框架，以及最大仿射凸拟合和审计方法。

Result: 建立了可选择的误差证书和统一的初始阶段决策质量保证，给出了尖锐常数，将混合导数估计推广到非光滑密度情形，并提出了最大仿射凸拟合与审计方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为混合整数补偿函数的凸近似提供了理论严谨、常数尖锐且可实际部署的误差控制与质量保证方法，即使精确中心化不可行也能通过缺陷调整获得有效保证。

Abstract: We study convex approximations of mixed-integer recourse functions in two-stage stochastic programming. For first-stage decisions, the relevant approximation error is the signed expected residual between the convex approximation and the integer-recourse value function. On bounded uncertainty boxes, we identify residual conditions that yield deterministic bounds for this expectation error. The central condition is coordinate slice-centering, under which integration by parts gives an anisotropic total-variation bound and a mixed-derivative bound whose all-coordinate case is expressed through Vitali variation of the density. Exact centering, however, may be incompatible with convexity, even for totally unimodular ceiling recourse. We therefore use commuting coordinate projectors to decompose an arbitrary residual into a centered component and an explicit slice-mean defect. The resulting defect-adjusted bounds provide selectable error certificates and uniform first-stage decision-quality guarantees. We further establish sharp constants, extend the mixed-derivative estimate to nonsmooth densities, and develop tensor-product and geometric extensions of the framework, alongside a max-affine convex fitting and audit methodology.

</details>


### [205] [Persistent Mean Tracking in Client--Server Open Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00327)
*Amit Dutta,Fat-Hy Omar Rajab,Marcos M. Vasconcelos,Olugbenga M. Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究开放客户端-服务器网络中持久性智能体的识别与均值跟踪问题。网络包含高度规律的持久性智能体和偶尔出现的瞬态/非持久性智能体。由于服务器仅能观测二元活动指示和标量更新，朴素平均无法恢复活跃持久性均值。作者提出两阶段估计框架：第一阶段通过两层窗口识别程序以高概率区分持久性与瞬态智能体，第二阶段利用结构估计通过平滑滤波器跟踪活跃持久性均值，并证明跟踪误差几何收敛到由目标漂移和滤波器增益决定的邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的开放客户端-服务器网络中，智能体参与具有间歇性和异质性，持久性智能体（规律参与）与瞬态智能体（零星出现）混杂在一起。服务器只能观测二元活动指示和来自活跃智能体的标量更新，这导致朴素平均无法恢复活跃持久性均值：瞬态智能体的更新会污染聚合结果，异质性参与又会引入偏差。因此，需要一种能够区分两类智能体并可靠跟踪持久性均值的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段估计框架。Phase I开发了一种两层基于窗口的识别程序：第一层形成单窗口活动决策，第二层跨窗口聚合这些决策以建立持久性与瞬态智能体之间的高概率分离。在异构伯努利参与假设下，推导了显式的有限窗口界，指定服务器需要等待多少个窗口才能以高概率实现该分离。Phase II利用得到的结构估计，通过平滑滤波器跟踪活跃持久性均值，并证明跟踪误差以几何速率收敛到由目标漂移和滤波器增益决定的一个邻域。

Result: 论文推导了Phase I中窗口数目的显式有限窗口界，保证了以高概率实现持久性与瞬态智能体的分离；Phase II中证明了跟踪误差几何收敛到由目标漂移和滤波器增益决定的一个邻域。数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段框架（窗口式识别+平滑滤波跟踪），能够在开放客户端-服务器网络中有效区分持久性与瞬态智能体，并以可保证的几何收敛速率跟踪活跃持久性均值，为间歇性参与的异构网络均值估计问题提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: We study persistent-agent identification and mean tracking in an open client-server network where agents participate intermittently. The network consists of a core of highly regular persistent agents and a subset of transient/non-persistent agents that appear only sporadically. Since the server observes only binary activity indicators and scalar updates from active agents, naive averaging cannot recover the active persistent mean because transient-agent updates contaminate the aggregate, and heterogeneous participation introduces bias. To address this, we propose a two-phase estimation framework. In Phase I, we develop a two-layer window-based identification procedure: the first layer forms single-window activity decisions, while the second aggregates these decisions across windows to create a high-probability separation between persistent and transient agents. Under heterogeneous Bernoulli participation, we derive explicit finite-window bounds that specify how many windows the server must wait for this separation to occur with high probability. In Phase II, we use the resulting structural estimate to track the active persistent mean via a smoothing filter, and show that the tracking error contracts geometrically to a neighborhood determined by the target drift and the filter gain. We provide numerical simulations to illustrate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [206] [Source Reliability Weighted Observer Design for Open Client Server Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00342)
*Amit Dutta,Marcos M. Vasconcelos,Olugbenga M. Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放客户端-服务器网络中的状态估计问题，其中活跃观测源随时间变化，且不一定是有效传感器。提出了一种基于源可靠性加权的观测器，通过重复创新一致性学习每个源的可信度得分，在有限学习期后能够准确追踪潜在状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放客户端-服务器网络中，活跃观测源集合随时间动态变化，且活跃源可能并非潜在状态的有效传感器（即存在干扰源），这使得传统状态估计方法面临挑战。服务器无法预先知道哪些代理提供有效状态测量，因此需要一种能够自动识别可靠源并据此进行状态估计的方法。

Method: 提出源可靠性加权观测器（source-reliability-weighted observer）。状态估计采用标准的固定先验加权最小二乘更新，但每个活跃观测所分配的信息量由源级别的可靠性得分决定，该得分通过重复创新一致性（repeated innovation consistency）学习得到。

Result: 数值结果表明，当考虑所有活跃源时，标准观测器存在有限偏差；而所提出的观测器先学习各源的可靠性，经过有限学习阶段后能够跟踪期望的潜在状态。

Conclusion: 通过源可靠性得分的学习与加权，该观测器能够在开放网络中区分状态一致性传感器与干扰源，从而在有限学习期后实现对潜在状态的准确估计。

Abstract: State estimation in open client server networks is challenging because the set of active observation sources changes over time, and active sources need not be valid sensors of the latent state. We study a client-server estimation problem in which a fixed but unknown state-consistent sensing class measures the latent state when active, while nuisance sources may arrive in the active set and generate observations from a different signal class. The server observes active sources, but it does not know which agents observe valid state measurements. We propose a source-reliability-weighted observer. The state estimate is a standard fixed-prior weighted least-squares update, but the information assigned to each active observation is determined by a source-level reliability score learned from repeated innovation consistency. Numerical results show that the observer, when all active sources are considered, has a finite bias, while the proposed observer initially learns source reliability and then tracks the desired latent state after a finite learning period.

</details>


### [207] [Dynamic Traffic Allocation for Revenue Maximization on Creator Economy Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02293)
*Zhengli Wang,Franklin Lin Feng,Zhixi Wan*

Main category: math.OC
