Moreover, an explicit convergence rate is also derived for the resulting high-order multi-scale solutions. Furthermore, an efficient multi-scale algorithm consisting of off-line and on-line computation stages is developed for high-accuracy simulation of nonlinear thermal radiation behavior in composite structures, and corresponding convergence analysis is also obtained. Two- and three-dimensional numerical examples are presented to validate the competitive advantages of the proposed multi-scale approach, not only exceptional numerical accuracy, but also reduced computational cost in both storage requirements and computational time.

</details>


### [245] [Generalized Modulo Hysteresis Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01270)
*Matthias Beckmann,Jürgen Jeschke*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义模数编码器，通过引入通用转移函数并允许折叠时间依赖于折叠转换的非理想性，灵活设计信号折叠机制，并给出了带限信号由广义模数样本唯一确定的条件及有理论保证的恢复算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模数滞回（modulo hysteresis）编码方案存在不足，限制了高动态范围信号的编码效率与恢复性能，因此需要更灵活、更具普适性的模数编码框架。

Method: 引入广义模数编码器，设计时将通用转移函数纳入折叠过程，并允许折叠时间随折叠转换的非理想性而变化；对相应算子进行严格的数学性质分析，推导带限信号唯一可恢复的条件，并基于此设计有理论保证的信号恢复算法。

Result: 给出了广义模数采样下带限信号唯一确定的充分条件，提出了具有数学保证的恢复算法，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性与算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 广义模数编码器克服了模数滞回模型的局限性，在理论上保证了信号可恢复性，并在实践中展现了良好的性能，为高动态范围信号采样提供了更通用的编码与恢复方案。

Abstract: Unlimited sensing and its extension via modulo hysteresis provide an efficient encoding scheme for high dynamic range signals by folding the signal's amplitude into the range of the analog-to-digital converter prior to sampling. Motivated by shortcomings of the modulo hysteresis model, in this work we introduce generalized modulo encoders that flexibly design the folding by incorporating a general transition function and allowing the folding times to depend on the nonideality of the folding transition. We rigorously study mathematical properties of the respective operators and derive conditions under which a bandlimited signal is uniquely determined by its generalized modulo samples. Finally, we propose a recovery algorithm backed by mathematical guarantees and illustrate our theoretical results through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [246] [On a Class of Block-Regularized Preconditioned Iterative Methods for Indefinite Least-Squares Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01273)
*Pinki Khatun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的块正则化分裂（BRS）框架，用于高效求解不定最小二乘（ILS）问题，并在此基础上发展了BRS迭代方法及BRS预处理器。为提升性能，进一步引入松弛BRS（RBRS）预处理器，加速Krylov子空间方法的收敛，大量数值实验验证了其在迭代次数、计算时间和整体效率上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定最小二乘（ILS）问题在科学计算和工程应用中广泛存在，但现有的求解方法在效率和稳健性方面存在不足，尤其是在大规模问题的处理上。因此需要开发更高效、更稳健的数值求解框架。

Method: 提出块正则化分裂（BRS）框架，将块状正则化策略融入矩阵分裂方案中，构造BRS迭代方法及BRS预处理器；并进一步引入松弛BRS（RBRS）预处理器以改善谱性质，加速Krylov子空间方法的收敛。对迭代方法的收敛性进行了严格分析，并建立了预处理矩阵的谱界。

Result: 在稠密和稀疏两类测试问题上进行了大量数值实验，结果表明所提出的BRS和RBRS预处理器在迭代次数、计算时间和整体效率方面均一致优于现有方法，证明了该框架在求解大规模ILS问题上的有效性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的块正则化分裂（BRS）框架及其衍生预处理器（BRS和RBRS）为大规模不定最小二乘问题提供了一种高效、稳健的求解方案，具有重要的理论价值和应用前景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel block-regularized splitting (BRS) framework for the efficient solution of indefinite least-squares (ILS) problems. Based on a block-wise regularization strategy incorporated into a matrix splitting scheme, we develop a BRS iterative method together with an effective BRS preconditioner. The convergence of the proposed iterative method is rigorously analyzed, and spectral bounds for the BRS-preconditioned matrix are established. To further enhance computational performance, we introduce a relaxed BRS (RBRS) preconditioner, which provides improved spectral properties and significantly accelerates the convergence of Krylov subspace methods. Extensive numerical experiments on both dense and sparse test problems demonstrate that the proposed BRS and RBRS preconditioners consistently outperform existing approaches in terms of iteration count, computational time, and overall efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed block-regularized splitting framework for solving large-scale ILS problems.

</details>


### [247] [On calculating polar solvation energy of nonrigid proteins in the Poisson-Boltzmann theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02525)
*Matthias Dogbatsey,Yuanzhen Shao,Emil Alexov,Shan Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一个广义的Poisson-Boltzmann（PB）框架，能够处理蛋白质从真空到水相转变时的非刚性构象变化，并避免自能伪影。通过解析消去正则化PB模型中的自能项，并针对非正则化求解器（如APBS和DelPhi）提出基于热力学循环的Coulombic校正，该框架在扰动双原子系统和多种蛋白质上得到验证，表现出高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PB理论是隐式溶剂模型的核心，在计算极性溶剂化能时通常假设蛋白质结构固定不变。然而，蛋白质从真空到水相的转变过程中会发生构象变化，简单的刚性假设会导致计算误差。作者旨在开发一个能够容纳非刚性构象变化的广义PB框架，同时避免自能伪影问题。

Method: 作者提出两种策略：对于正则化PB模型（电荷奇异性由Green函数捕捉），通过解析方式在真空和水相中消除自能项；对于非正则化PB求解器（如APBS和DelPhi），提出了一个简单热力学循环，在真空态添加Coulombic校正项来补偿构象变化带来的能量差异。该方法不依赖于特定的尖锐界面或扩散界面PB模型选择。

Result: 该方法在扰动双原子系统和一组在真空与水相中具有不同结构的蛋白质上得到验证，结果表明无论采用sharp-interface还是diffuse-interface PB模型，也无论使用何种数值求解器，该广义PB理论均表现出高精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了在PB框架下正确处理蛋白质的非刚性构象变化对极性溶剂化能的影响，消除了自能伪影，并兼容多种PB模型和求解器，为更真实的生物分子静电分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The Poisson-Boltzmann (PB) theory is a cornerstone of implicit solvent models for electrostatic analysis, and has found a great success in various biomolecular applications. However, in calculating polar solvation energy, one should consider that the structure of the protein changes upon transition from vacuum to water phases. To address this, here we report for the first time a generalized PB framework capable of accommodating nonrigid conformational changes without suffering from self-energy artifacts. For regularized PB models, in which the charge singularities are captured by the Green's functions, self-energies in the water and vacuum states will be analytically canceled. For non-regularized PB solvers, such as APBS and DelPhi, a simple thermodynamic cycle is proposed for nonrigid proteins by adding a Coulombic correction in vacuum. The generalized PB theory is validated using a perturbed two-atom system and a diverse set of proteins with different structures in vacuum and water, demonstrating its accuracy and robustness, regardless of the choice of sharp-interface and diffuse-interface PB models and different numerical solvers.

</details>


### [248] [High-order WENO-based semi-implicit Newton-type fast sweeping methods for static Hamilton-Jacobi equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01304)
*Yuan Liu,Jianliang Qian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高阶WENO的半隐式Newton型Gauss-Seidel Lax-Friedrichs快速扫描方法，用于求解流体移动中波传播产生的广义程函方程，通过将高阶WENO近似引入数值Hamiltonian，将局部求解器扩展到五阶、七阶和九阶精度，并在二维和三维空间中验证了其效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义程函方程在运动流体中的波传播问题（如地震成像、声学等）中具有重要应用，但现有方法的精度有限（通常为低阶），需要发展高阶精度且高效的数值求解算法来更好地刻画复杂传播特性。

Method: 在Li和Qian(2020)的Newton型框架基础上（利用Newton法逐行更新解，并利用三对角且严格对角占优的Jacobian矩阵），通过将空间导数的高阶WENO近似引入数值Hamiltonian，将局部求解器扩展到五阶、七阶和九阶精度。在Gauss-Seidel迭代框架内考虑了三种交替扫描策略：按列扫描、按行扫描、列行交替扫描。

Result: 二维和三维空间中的数值算例展示了所提出方案的高效率和高精度，验证了高阶WENO近似结合半隐式Newton型快速扫描方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶WENO半隐式Newton型Gauss-Seidel Lax-Friedrichs快速扫描方法能够高效且高精度地求解广义程函方程，且多种交替扫描策略均能有效工作。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose high-order weighted essentially non-oscillatory (WENO)-based semi-implicit Newton-type Gauss-Seidel Lax-Friedrichs fast sweeping methods for solving the generalized Eikonal equation arising in wave propagation through a moving fluid. Building upon the Newton-type framework of Li and Qian (2020), which updates the solution line-wise using Newton's method with a tridiagonal and strictly diagonally dominant Jacobian, we extend the local solver to fifth-, seventh-, and ninth-order accuracy by incorporating high-order WENO approximations of the spatial derivatives into the numerical Hamiltonian. Three alternating sweeping strategies, namely column-wise, row-wise, and column-row-wise, are considered within the Gauss-Seidel iteration framework. Numerical examples in both two and three spatial dimensions demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [249] [Control variates with neural surrogates for uncertainty quantification in kinetic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01360)
*Wei Chen,Giacomo Dimarco,Lorenzo Pareschi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带有随机输入的动力学方程（如Boltzmann、Landau方程）中的不确定性量化难题，提出并分析了用神经代理替代经典数值格式的低保真模型的多保真控制变量方法。证明了该估计器保持无偏性，且最优方差变化受神经近似误差控制；结合Fokker-Planck和BGK代理的残差稳定性估计，并将其推广到多个控制变量和含Euler极限观测量的渐进保持（AP）层级。流体极限下，层级最优方差收敛到Euler控制的方差。微-宏神经代理的数值试验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学方程的高维模拟成本高昂，使得随机输入下的不确定性量化计算难以承受。多保真控制变量方法通过将少量高保真模拟与大量低保真模型评估相结合来降低计算负担，但传统低保真模型仍需数值求解。本文动机是在低保真层引入神经代理，以进一步降低计算成本，同时保证估计器的统计性质。神经代理相比经典数值方案在速度上有优势，但其近似误差对方差的影响需要严格的理论分析。此外，在从动力学模型过渡到流体极限时，控制变量结构的渐进行为也亟需研究。

Method: 1) 将多保真控制变量框架中的低保真模型替换为神经代理，构建新的估计器并证明其无偏性。2) 推导最优方差偏差的上界，该上界由精确低保真观测量与神经近似之间的误差控制。3) 对非齐次Fokker-Planck和BGK方程建立残差稳定性估计，用于量化神经代理误差。4) 将分析扩展至多个控制变量的情形。5) 构建含Euler极限观测量的AP层级，分析流体极限下各层级控制变量的贡献和最优方差收敛行为。6) 基于微-宏神经代理进行数值试验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论结果显示：使用神经代理替换低保真模型后，估计器仍是无偏的；最优方差的偏差由神经近似误差所界定，并与残差稳定性估计相结合给出可操作的误差上界。在AP层级中，当物理参数趋向流体极限时，层级最优方差收敛到仅使用Euler控制变量的方差，而中间动力学修正项的贡献趋于零。基于微-宏神经代理的数值试验验证了方差稳定性和AP层级的预期行为。

Conclusion: 本文为在动力学方程不确定性量化中利用神经代理作为低保真模型的多保真控制变量方法提供了严格的理论保证。研究揭示了神经近似误差如何影响最优方差，并确立了AP层级中从动力学到流体极限的方差收敛行为，为高效、可靠的不确定性量化在实际高维动力学模拟中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Efficient uncertainty quantification for kinetic equations with random inputs is challenging because it requires repeated simulations of high-dimensional models, such as the Boltzmann, Landau, and related collisional equations, whose computational cost can quickly become prohibitive. Multifidelity control variates address this difficulty by coupling a small number of high-fidelity simulations with many evaluations of lower-complexity reduced models. In this work, we analyze the case in which the reduced model is replaced by a neural surrogate rather than evaluated through a classical numerical scheme. We show that the resulting estimator remains unbiased and that the change in the optimal variance induced by the neural approximation is controlled by the error between the exact low-fidelity observable and its neural approximation. This estimate is then combined with residual stability estimates for inhomogeneous Fokker--Planck and Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook surrogates. We also extend the analysis to several control variates and to an asymptotic-preserving (AP) hierarchy containing the limiting Euler observable. In the fluid limit, the optimal hierarchical variance converges to the variance associated with the limiting Euler control, while the contribution of the intermediate kinetic correction vanishes. Numerical tests based on micro--macro neural surrogates illustrate the predicted variance stability and the behavior of the AP hierarchy.

</details>


### [250] [Results, challenges and new steps on RBF approximation and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01362)
*Stefano De Marchi,Nadaniela Egidi,Josephin Giacomini,Pierluigi Maponi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对基于径向基函数（RBF）技术的逼近方法进行了最新的综述，涵盖了该领域的最新进展、相关的计算挑战及稳定性问题，特别是在高性能或并行计算场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向基函数（RBF）技术近年来在各类计算工具和应用领域受到广泛关注，但缺乏一个统一的、最新的综述来系统梳理相关成果，尤其是对其计算挑战和稳定性问题的归纳。

Method: 这是一篇综述性研究，系统性地回顾和整理了基于RBF技术的逼近方法，重点总结相关研究成果，并讨论了在高性能或并行计算条件下必须应对的计算挑战和稳定性问题。

Result: 呈现了RBF技术的最新进展概览，阐明了在高性能/并行计算场景下所面临的关键计算挑战和稳定性问题。

Conclusion: RBF逼近方法在不同应用领域有广泛前景，但其计算效率和数值稳定性仍需进一步研究，特别是在高性能与并行计算环境下。

Abstract: We present an up-to-date overview of approximation methods based on Radial Basis Function (RBF) techniques, which have recently attracted attention across various computational tools and application fields. This review study presents relevant results on RBF techniques, highlighting the associated computational challenges and stability issues that must be addressed when high-performance or parallel computation is required.

</details>


### [251] [An analytical-numerical approach to the Emden-Fowler equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01465)
*Mihai Halic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究源自天体物理的Emden-Fowler方程u''=-x^r u^p, u(0)=1, u'(0)=0，对其解关于参数(r,p)的定性和定量依赖性进行了分析与数值模拟，重点估计第一个零点x_0，并利用结果解决两个新问题：从x_0反向求解该方程，以及将其转化为超定边值问题并判定可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emden-Fowler方程在天体物理中具有重要应用背景，其解的性质（特别是第一个零点）对物理模型至关重要。作者旨在深入理解解对参数的依赖关系，并探索两个此前未被研究的新问题：反向求解和超定边值问题的可解性。

Method: 采用解析分析与数值模拟相结合的方法。解析部分研究解对参数(r,p)的定性和定量依赖，特别关注第一个零点x_0关于r和p的估计；数值仿真并行验证解析结果。在此基础上，将方程从x_0处反向求解，并将其转化为超定边值问题进行可解性判定。

Result: 给出了第一个零点x_0关于参数r、p的估计结果，并成功解决了两个新问题：从x_0反向求解Emden-Fowler方程，以及判定转化后的超定边值问题何时可解。

Conclusion: 本文深化了对Emden-Fowler方程参数依赖性的理解，为零点的估计提供了新结果，并拓展了该方程在反向求解和超定边值问题方面的研究，具有理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate the Emden-Fowler equation $u''=-x^ru^p, u(0)=1, u'(0)=0$, with roots in astrophysics, and study the qualitative and quantitative dependence of its solution on the parameters; the analytical work is paralleled by numerical simulations. A special attention is given to estimating the first zero $x_0$ of $u$ in terms of $r, p$. The results are used to address two apparently new issues: first, we solve EF backwards starting from $x_0$; second, we transform it into an overdetermined boundary value problem and decide when is this solvable.

</details>


### [252] [On a boundary value problem related to radiative heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01472)
*Mihai Halic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类与辐射热传递和化学反应相关的边值问题 $y''=κ^2y^n, y(0)=1, y(1)=0$，利用上/下包络方法分析解对参数的定性和定量依赖关系，并将其转化为易于求解的初值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该边值问题在辐射热传递、化学反应等物理领域具有广泛应用，但直接求解存在困难，需要系统地研究解对参数的依赖性，并寻找更简便的数值求解途径。

Method: 采用上包络和下包络（upper/lower envelopes）技术来分析该非线性二阶边值问题解对参数κ和n的定性与定量依赖性，并通过变换将其转化为可解的初值问题。

Result: 成功建立了解对参数的依赖关系，并将原始的边值问题转化为更简单、易于求解的初值问题。

Conclusion: 上/下包络方法为该类非线性边值问题提供了有效的分析工具，同时初值问题的转化大大降低了数值求解的难度。

Abstract: We consider the boundary value problem $y''=κ^2y^n, y(0)=1, y(1)=0,$ relevant to radiative heat transfer, chemical reactions, etc. We use upper/lower envelopes to study the qualitative and quantitative dependence of its solution on parameters, and transform it into an easily solvable initial value problem.

</details>


### [253] [Logarithmic Resonance at Mixed Boundary Junctions in Gradient-Dependent Semilinear Equationsv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01790)
*Marin Mišur*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆偏微分方程在混合Dirichlet-Neumann边界连接点处的解正则性问题。当引入随|∇u|线性增长的梯度惩罚时，会产生O(r^{-1/2})的局部源项，与主齐次微分算子的半整数谱产生非正交共振，导致标准的可分离多项式假设在O(r^{3/2})阶失效。作者建立了广义共振定理，证明了对数异常项r^{3/2}ln(r)的出现，推导了精确的对数系数与角偏移，并验证了ℓ₁、ℓ₂、ℓ∞及任意ℓ_q范数惩罚的普适性。此外，作者构造了相应的富集连续Galerkin空间（XFEM），证明其拟最优性，并通过有限元实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆偏微分方程在混合Dirichlet-Neumann边界连接点处的解正则性会严重退化，经典已知的主导奇异函数为O(r^{1/2})。虽然线性行为已被充分研究，但引入依赖梯度的半线性扰动后，局部渐近行为如何改变尚不清楚。理解这一机制对于发展有效的数值方法至关重要。

Method: 作者采用理论分析与数值验证相结合的方法：(1) 建立广义共振定理，分析梯度惩罚项与算子半整数谱之间的非正交共振机制；(2) 推导精确的对数异常项r^{3/2}ln(r)的解析形式及余项的Sobolev正则性界；(3) 对多种ℓ_q范数惩罚（ℓ₁、ℓ₂、ℓ∞及任意ℓ_q）计算精确对数系数与角偏移；(4) 构造富集连续Galerkin空间（XFEM），证明其拟最优性；(5) 在无曲率的平坦连接点上进行有限元实验验证。

Result: 成功证明了梯度惩罚导致O(r^{-1/2})源项与半整数谱的共振，迫使r^{3/2}ln(r)对数异常的出现；给出了精确的对数系数和角偏移；验证了该阻碍在不同ℓ_q范数惩罚下具有普适性；XFEM富集空间被证明是拟最优的；有限元实验确认了局部污染、恢复了ℓ₁/ℓ₂/ℓ∞惩罚下的预测对数系数，并表明消解连接点可恢复标准有限元求解器的最优自由度效率。

Conclusion: 梯度依赖的半线性扰动在混合边界连接点处引发了新的共振机制，产生超越经典O(r^{1/2})奇异性的对数异常项。这一结果不仅深化了对半线性椭圆问题边界奇异性结构的理解，也为设计针对性的富集数值方法（XFEM）和软件架构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The regularity of solutions to elliptic partial differential equations degrades severely at mixed Dirichlet-Neumann boundary junctions, characterized classically by an $\mathcal{O}(r^{1/2})$ leading singular function. While this linear behavior is well documented, the introduction of gradient-dependent semilinear perturbations alters the local asymptotic profile. This article proves that gradient penalties scaling linearly with $|\nabla u|$ induce a highly localized $\mathcal{O}(r^{-1/2})$ source term that resonates with the half-integer spectrum of the principal homogeneous differential operator. This non-orthogonal resonance causes standard separable polynomial assumptions to fail at order $\mathcal{O}(r^{3/2})$. We establish a generalized resonance theorem that forces the emergence of a logarithmic anomaly, providing the exact analytical formulation of the resulting $r^{3/2} \ln(r)$ profile alongside rigorous local Sobolev regularity bounds for the remainder. By calculating the exact logarithmic coefficients and angular offsets for $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$, $\ell_\infty$, and arbitrary $\ell_q$-norm penalties, we demonstrate the universality of this obstruction. Finally, we formalize the corresponding enriched continuous Galerkin space (XFEM), establish its quasi-optimality, and present finite element experiments that confirm the predicted localized pollution, recover the predicted logarithmic coefficients across the $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$, and $\ell_\infty$ penalties on a curvature-free flat junction, and show that resolving the junction recovers optimal degree-of-freedom efficiency in standard finite element solvers; we close by outlining the targeted software architectures required for a fully enriched implementation.

</details>


### [254] [Numerical approximation of fractional diffusion equations on metric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01932)
*David Bolin,Lenin Riera-Segura,Alexandre B. Simas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致度量图上的分数阶扩散方程，其中非局部动力学由移位Kirchhoff-Laplacian的分数幂控制。作者建立了严格的数学框架，提出基于后向欧拉时间步进和有限元空间离散的全离散格式，并采用有理逼近近似分数算子，将问题转化为一系列稀疏椭圆求解。文章导出了时间、空间和有理逼近三个层面的误差估计，并通过数值实验验证了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶扩散方程在度量图上缺乏系统的数值分析和求解框架，现有工作对移位Kirchhoff-Laplacian分数幂算子的离散化研究不足，需要建立严格的理论基础和高效的数值方案。

Method: 采用后向Euler时间离散与有限元空间离散相结合的全离散格式；利用有理逼近近似分数算子作用，将非局部问题约化为一系列稀疏椭圆问题以提升计算效率；分别推导时间、空间和有理逼近三个离散层的误差估计。

Result: 建立了度量图上分数阶扩散方程的全离散数值格式，给出了时间离散、空间离散和有理逼近的误差估计，并通过数值实验证实了各层离散的收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于后向Euler-有限元-有理逼近的全离散方案在理论上具有收敛保证，数值上高效可行，为度量图上的分数阶扩散问题提供了可靠的数值求解工具。

Abstract: We study fractional diffusion equations on compact metric graphs, where the nonlocal dynamics is governed by fractional powers of the shifted Kirchhoff-Laplacian. Building on recent advances in the analysis of fractional operators on metric graphs, we establish a rigorous mathematical framework and propose a fully discrete scheme based on backward Euler time-stepping and finite element discretization. To approximate the action of the fractional operator, we employ rational approximations, reducing the problem to a sequence of sparse elliptic solves for efficient implementation. We derive error estimates for the temporal, spatial, and rational discretizations, and confirm convergence through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [255] [An $H^{-1}$ least-squares UnCut FEM on domains defined by a level set function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02015)
*Jiashun Hu,Buyang Li,Han Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的UnCut有限元方法，用于在由水平集函数表示的曲边界域上求解Poisson和Stokes方程。该方法通过最小化$H^{-1}$残差的最小二乘公式避免了对切割子区域的数值积分，同时不需要用户调节的罚参数，保证了数值稳定性。作者严格证明了该方法在$H^1$范数下对两类方程均具有最优阶收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的切割有限元方法（如φ-FEM）在处理由水平集函数表示的曲边界时需要进行切割子区域的数值积分，并依赖于用户选择的罚参数来保证稳定性，这降低了算法的鲁棒性并增加了使用难度。本文旨在克服这些限制，提出一个无需用户调节参数且避免切割区域积分的有限元方法。

Method: 该方法的创新核心在于采用最小二乘公式，通过最小化控制方程的$H^{-1}$残差来实现稳定性，而非依赖较大的稳定性/罚参数。与φ-FEM类似，它避免了对边界元素的切割子区域进行数值积分，从而简化了实现流程。

Result: 理论上，作者严格证明了所提出的UnCut FEM在$H^1$范数下对于Poisson和Stokes方程均达到最优阶收敛；数值实验进一步支持并验证了这一理论结论。

Conclusion: UnCut FEM提供了一种无需用户调节罚参数、无需切割区域积分的稳健有限元方法，在曲边界域上求解Poisson和Stokes方程时保持了最优收敛阶，具有较高的理论价值和应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a novel UnCut finite element method (FEM) for the Poisson and Stokes equations on domains with curved boundaries represented by a level set function. Like the $φ$-FEM, the method avoids numerical integration over cut subregions of boundary elements, but introduces a novel least-squares formulation that minimizes an $H^{-1}$ residual of the governing equations. This formulation ensures stability without requiring large stabilization parameters, thereby eliminating the need for user-tuned penalty parameters and improving the robustness of the computation. Optimal-order convergence of the UnCut FEM solutions is rigorously established in the $H^1$ norm for both the Poisson and Stokes equations, and numerical experiments are presented to support the theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [256] [A posteriori error analysis for the second-order BDF method for the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02021)
*Stefan Karch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于BDF(2)时间离散和任意多项式次数有限元空间离散的切平面格式(TPS)求解Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG)方程的严格后验误差估计，为LLG方程的全自适应算法奠定了数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切平面格式(TPS)是Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG)方程的成熟离散化方法，但长期以来缺乏严格的后验误差估计，这阻碍了自适应算法的设计与理论保证。

Method: 采用二阶后向微分公式(BDF(2))进行时间离散，空间上使用任意多项式次数的有限元方法；在自适应网格和变时间步长的设定下，推导出可计算的时空离散误差上界估计子。

Result: 得到了严格的后验误差估计，能够为自适应网格上的时间离散误差和空间离散误差提供可计算的上界。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了LLG方程全自适应算法的数学基础，填补了TPS后验误差估计的理论空白。

Abstract: The tangent plane scheme (TPS) is a well-established discretization of the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation. However, rigorous a posteriori error estimates have not been established. In this work, we derive a rigorous a posteriori error estimate for the TPS based on the second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF(2)) in time and finite elements of arbitrary polynomial degree in space. The proposed estimators provide computable upper bounds for the temporal and spatial discretization errors on adaptive meshes with variable time-step sizes. This result establishes the mathematical foundation for fully adaptive algorithms for the LLG equation.

</details>


### [257] [Finite element discretization of Yang--Mills connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02108)
*Geir Bogfjellmo,Charles Curry,Andreas Myklebust*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非平凡主丛（阿贝尔结构群）上Yang-Mills联络的有限元方法。局部联络形式满足由丛的转移函数诱导的内部跳跃条件，作者在破碎有限元外微分空间中对这些形式进行离散，并通过拉格朗日乘子弱施加跳跃条件，证明了鞍点问题的适定性和先验收敛估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang-Mills联络是规范场论的核心对象，但在非平凡主丛上进行数值逼近具有挑战性，因为局部联络形式之间存在由转移函数决定的内部跳跃条件。已有方法大多局限于平凡丛，需要一种能够在非平凡阿贝尔主丛上求解Yang-Mills联络的数值方法。

Method: 采用破碎有限元外微分空间（broken finite element exterior calculus）离散局部联络形式；利用拉格朗日乘子在弱形式下强制跳跃条件；将问题转化为鞍点系统，分析其适定性与收敛性。

Result: 证明了鞍点问题的适定性，导出了先验收敛估计；针对Hopf纤维化的数值实验表明，当球面用分段线性网格逼近时，方法呈现线性收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够正确处理非平凡阿贝尔主丛上Yang-Mills联络的跳跃条件，理论分析与数值实验均验证了方法的有效性与收敛性，为规范场论中的数值计算提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We propose a finite element method for Yang--Mills connections on nontrivial principal bundles with abelian structure group. Local connection forms satisfy internal jump conditions induced by the transition functions of the bundle. We discretize these forms in broken finite element exterior calculus spaces and enforce the jump conditions weakly by Lagrange multipliers. We prove well-posedness of the resulting saddle-point problem and derive an a priori convergence estimate. A numerical experiment for the Hopf fibration illustrates the method and exhibits linear convergence when the sphere is approximated by a piecewise linear mesh.

</details>


### [258] [Localized pointwise a posteriori error estimates for nonconforming finite element methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02132)
*Yongxing Guo,Yuwen Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为非协调有限元离散（Crouzeix–Raviart方法求解Poisson方程，Morley方法求解双调和方程）建立了局部化的逐点后验误差估计。关键创新在于设计了两个新型权函数，用于对双调和方程正则化Green函数导数的L¹范数进行尖锐估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验误差估计是自适应有限元方法的核心工具，但已有的非协调有限元后验误差估计大多是全局或非局部化的。本文旨在建立局部化（逐点意义下）的后验误差界，以支持更精细的自适应网格细化策略。

Method: 针对Poisson方程，推导了Crouzeix–Raviart方法的函数值与间断梯度误差的局部化估计；针对双调和方程，推导了Morley离散的局部化后验估计以控制局部Hessian误差。核心技术手段是构造两个新型权函数，实现对双调和方程正则化Green函数导数的L¹范数的尖锐估计。

Result: 成功建立了Crouzeix–Raviart方法在Poisson方程上的函数值与broken梯度误差的局部化后验估计，以及Morley方法在双调和方程上的局部化Hessian误差后验估计，且这些估计依赖于新设计的权函数实现了尖锐的常数控制。

Conclusion: 本文为非协调有限元方法提供了理论上的局部化逐点后验误差界，填补了该领域在双调和方程非协调离散局部化误差分析方面的空白，所提出的权函数构造方法具有潜在的推广价值。

Abstract: This paper establishes localized pointwise a posteriori error estimates for nonconforming finite element discretizations of the Poisson and biharmonic equations. For the Poisson problem, we derive localized estimates for the function-value and broken gradient errors of the Crouzeix--Raviart method. For the Morley discretization of the biharmonic equation, we derive a localized a posteriori estimate that controls the local Hessian error. The key ingredient is the design of two novel weight functions that facilitate sharp estimates of the \(L^1\) norms of derivatives of a regularized Green's function for the biharmonic equation.

</details>


### [259] [Discontinuous Galerkin Semidiscretization of the Information Geometric Regularized Compressible Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02223)
*Brook Eyob,Jesus Arias,Spencer H. Bryngelson,Florian Schäfer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息几何正则化（IGR）框架的不连续伽辽金（DG）离散化方法，用于可压缩欧拉方程中的激波稳定。该方法通过在PDE层面引入熵压Σ（由辅助椭圆方程获得）来正则化激波，仅通过增广压力P+Σ进入欧拉通量，保持格式的守恒结构。数值实验表明，该方法无需限制器或人工粘性即可稳定激波，随着多项式阶数提高能解析更细尺度的流场特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度数值方法处理可压缩欧拉流中的激波稳定仍是一个核心挑战。现有的激波捕捉方法（如限制器、人工粘性、重构类方法）在鲁棒性、精度、细尺度流场特征保持和计算复杂度之间存在权衡，需要一种无需这些传统手段即可实现激波稳定的新方法。

Method: 将Cao和Schäfer提出的信息几何正则化（IGR）框架应用于压可缩欧拉方程，采用DG离散化。方法通过辅助椭圆方程求解熵压Σ，将该正则化仅通过增广压力P+Σ引入欧拉通量中，保持离散化的守恒结构；超双曲方程和椭圆方程使用共同的逼近空间。

Result: 一维和二维基准问题的数值实验表明：该格式无需激波捕捉限制器或人工粘性即可稳定激波；与特征化TVB限制DG格式相比，IGR-DG方法在增加多项式阶数时能解析越来越细尺度的流场特征，同时保持稳定的激波分辨率；熵压仅集中于强压缩区域，在光滑区域中激活最小，提供选择性PDE级正则化。

Conclusion: IGR-DG方法提供了一种在PDE层面选择性正则化激波的有效途径，在保持守恒结构的同时避免了传统激波捕捉方法的权衡。尽管在密度或压力趋近零时仍可能需要正性保持方法，但该方法作为无需限制器实现高精度激波稳定的方案具有显著优势。

Abstract: Shock stabilization in compressible Euler flows remains a central challenge for high-order numerical methods. Existing shock-capturing approaches, including limiters, artificial viscosity, and reconstruction-based methods, involve tradeoffs between robustness, accuracy, preservation of fine-scale flow features, and computational complexity. In this work, we develop a discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretization of the information geometric regularization (IGR) framework introduced by Cao and Schäfer for the compressible Euler equations. The method regularizes shocks at the PDE level by augmenting the Euler equations with the entropic pressure $Σ$, obtained from an auxiliary elliptic equation. Within the DG formulation, the regularization enters only through the augmented pressure $P+Σ$ in the Euler fluxes, preserving the conservative structure of the discretization while using a common approximation space for both the hyperbolic and elliptic equations. Numerical experiments spanning one and two-dimensional benchmark problems show the proposed formulation stabilizes shocks without shock-capturing limiters or artificial viscosity, although positivity-preserving methods may still be required when the density or pressure approaches zero. Compared with a characteristic TVB-limited DG formulation, the IGR-DG method resolves increasingly finer-scale flow features as the polynomial order is increased while maintaining stable shock resolution. The entropic pressure remains localized to regions of strong compression with minimal activation in smooth regions of the flow, providing selective PDE-level regularization while preserving the underlying solution elsewhere.

</details>


### [260] [A Least-Squares Weak Galerkin Method for the Biharmonic Cauchy Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02317)
*Chunmei Wang,Shangyou Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于双调和方程Cauchy问题的最小二乘弱Galerkin（LS-WG）有限元方法，通过将四阶方程重构为两个二阶方程的耦合系统进行离散，得到对称正定的线性系统，无需inf-sup条件或全局C1相容有限元空间，并适用于一般多边形网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和方程的Cauchy问题是一个不适定问题，传统混合有限元方法需要满足离散inf-sup条件，而全局C1相容有限元空间的构造困难且复杂。因此需要一种无需这些约束、同时能灵活处理一般多边形网格的数值方法。

Method: 将四阶双调和方程重写为两个二阶方程的耦合系统；使用离散弱Laplacian算子在弱有限元空间上进行离散；采用最小二乘公式构造线性系统，使其对称正定；在连续Cauchy问题解唯一的假设下推导离散解的唯一性和最优阶误差估计。

Result: 建立了离散LS-WG解的唯一性，导出了离散能量范数下的最优阶误差估计；数值实验验证了理论收敛阶，并展示了方法的准确性、鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: LS-WG方法为双调和方程Cauchy问题提供了一种无需inf-sup条件和C1全局相容性要求的有效数值方案，且天然支持一般多边形网格，具有显著的计算灵活性和实用性。

Abstract: We develop a least-squares weak Galerkin (LS-WG) finite element method for the Cauchy problem of the biharmonic equation. The proposed approach reformulates the fourth-order equation as a coupled system of two second-order equations, which are discretized using discrete weak Laplacian operators on weak finite element spaces. The resulting least-squares formulation yields a symmetric positive definite linear system, thereby eliminating the discrete inf-sup condition required by mixed finite element methods while avoiding the construction of globally $C^1$-conforming finite element spaces. Furthermore, the weak Galerkin framework naturally accommodates general polygonal meshes, offering considerable flexibility in mesh generation and approximation. Under the assumption that the continuous biharmonic Cauchy problem admits a unique solution, we establish the uniqueness of the discrete LS-WG solution and derive optimal-order error estimates in a discrete energy norm. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the accuracy, robustness, and effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [261] [A Unified Kullback--Leibler Divergence Analysis of Generative Diffusion Models via Entropy Production Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02406)
*Han Wu,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于正向-反向扩散过程对的熵产生率的统一框架，用于生成模型的误差分析。该框架将终端KL散度分解为初始化误差、分数近似误差和时间离散化误差之和，并在边际分布层面分析熵产生，从而为Euler-Maruyama采样器获得了O(h²)的锐利收敛率（优于Girsanov路径空间分析得到的O(h)）。该框架还通过改变扩散系数统一了基于分数的SDE、概率流ODE和随机插值器的分析，揭示了确定性与随机采样之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型误差分析主要依赖Girsanov路径空间方法，通常只能获得O(h)的时间离散化误差率，且不同生成模型（score-based SDE、probability-flow ODE、stochastic interpolants）缺乏统一的误差分析框架。本文旨在构建一个统一的、基于边际分布层面的熵产生率分析框架，以获得更锐利的收敛率并统一各类生成模型的误差分析。

Method: 本文提出基于前向-反向连续性方程流对的熵产生率分析框架。核心贡献在于：1) 推导出熵产生率的闭合速度形式恒等式，其时间积分可将终端KL散度分解为初始化误差、分数近似误差和时间离散化误差三项之和；2) 区别于传统的路径空间分析，该方法在边际分布层面分析熵产生；3) 通过改变扩散系数，将score-based SDE、probability-flow ODE和stochastic interpolants统一纳入单一不等式框架中。

Result: 该框架为Euler-Maruyama采样器获得了O(h²)的时间离散化收敛率，显著改善了Girsanov路径空间分析中典型的O(h)率。数值实验验证了关于步长和终端时间的预测缩放行为。此外，该框架成功统一了多种生成模型的误差分析，并揭示了确定性采样与随机采样之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过熵产生率的边际分布分析，提供了更锐利且更统一的生成模型误差分析框架，为理解不同采样策略的误差特性及其权衡提供了理论基础，并给出了与理论预测一致的数值验证。

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for the error analysis of generative models based on the entropy production rate of the forward-reverse diffusion process pair. For a pair of continuity equation flows, the rate admits a closed velocity form identity whose time integral decomposes the terminal Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence into the sum of an initialization error, a score approximation error, and a time-discretization error. By analyzing the entropy production at the level of marginal distributions, rather than in path space, our framework yields a sharp convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(h^2)$
  for the Euler-Maruyama sampler, where $h$
  is the step size. This improves upon the $\mathcal{O}(h)$ rates typically obtained from Girsanov's path-space analyses. Furthermore, our framework unifies the analysis of score-based SDEs, probability-flow ODEs, and stochastic interpolants by varying diffusion coefficients within a single inequality, revealing the trade-off between deterministic and stochastic sampling. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted scaling with step size and terminal time.

</details>


### [262] [Generalised projective integration scheme in equation-free multiscale modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02419)
*Tanay Kumar Karmakar,Durga Charan Dalal*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义投影积分（GPI）格式，用于应对系统频谱随时间显著变化时固定时间步长和突发长度失效的问题。该方案统一并扩展了多种多尺度方法，通过自适应地选择微观、介观和宏观时间步长及突发长度来适应动态演化的尺度分离。文章进行了包含稳定性区域分裂在内的全面稳定性分析，并通过三个具有不同频谱演化类型的问题验证了该方案在多个性能指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当系统频谱随时间显著变化时，固定的宏观、介观和微观时间步长以及突发长度不再适用，需要自适应且局部信息驱动的策略来解决这一多尺度模拟中的关键挑战。

Method: 提出广义投影积分（GPI）格式，统一并扩展多种现有多尺度方法；开展包含稳定性区域分裂的全面稳定性分析；发展针对微观、介观、宏观时间步长及突发长度的问题依赖选择策略，并通过数值实验系统验证。

Result: 通过三个具有不同类型频谱演化的代表性问题，从微观时间步数、精度、计算时间、内存使用和微观模拟比例五个方面进行评估。结果表明GPI方案在性能上持续优于现有投影积分方法及常用刚性求解器。

Conclusion: GPIGPI方案为时间依赖的频谱变化和动态尺度分离提供了一种更通用、更灵活且性能优越的多尺度计算范式。

Abstract: When the spectrum of a system varies significantly over time, fixed choices of macro-, meso-, and micro-time steps, as well as burst lengths, become inadequate, necessitating adaptive and locally informed strategies. To address these challenges, this article proposes a novel and flexible generalised projective integration (GPI) scheme, designed to accommodate time-dependent spectral variation and dynamically evolving scale separation. The proposed framework unifies and extends several existing multiscale methodologies, thereby offering a more general and adaptable computational paradigm. A comprehensive stability analysis of the GPI scheme is carried out, including a detailed investigation of the splitting of the stability region, which forms a central component of this work. Furthermore, problem-dependent strategies for selecting the micro-, meso-, and macro-time steps, as well as the burst length, are developed and their impacts are systematically validated through numerical experiments. To assess the effectiveness of the proposed scheme, three representative problems with distinct types of spectral evolution are considered. The performance of the proposed GPI scheme is evaluated and compared with several existing projective integration methods as well as some widely used stiff solvers, based on (i) number of micro time steps, (ii) accuracy, (iii) computational time, (iv) memory usage and (v) proportion of micro-scale simulations. The results demonstrate that the GPI scheme consistently outperforms the existing methods in terms of performance.

</details>
