### [263] [Rhombohedral Graphene: A Tale of Many Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00167)
*Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Filippo Gaggioli,Daniele Guerci,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于非相互作用能带色散提出一个简单指标，用于识别菱面体石墨烯相图中的强关联区域，并结合神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法与Hartree-Fock理论求解相互作用基态，发现了包括Wigner晶体、自掺杂Wigner晶体及"反晶体"在内的多种无经典类比电子晶体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体石墨烯展现出从手性超导体到电子晶体等极其丰富的关联量子相，但如何系统识别和预测这些强关联区域仍是一个挑战，需要有效的理论工具来描绘相图中的关联特征。

Method: 提出一个从非相互作用能带色散导出的简单指示器来标记强关联区域；发展神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法并与Hartree-Fock理论结合，求解相互作用基态。

Result: 计算揭示了一系列无经典类比的电子晶体相，随载流子密度增加依次为：Wigner晶体、自掺杂Wigner晶体，以及"反晶体"（电子液体中的空穴格子），并讨论了它们的实验表现及与超导的可能关联。

Conclusion: 这些新型电子晶体相的发现扩展了对菱面体石墨烯关联现象的理解，为探索其与超导性的联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Experiments on rhombohedral graphene have uncovered an extraordinary wealth of correlated quantum phases - from chiral superconductors to electronic crystals - all within a single family of atomically thin materials. Here, we introduce a simple indicator, derived from the noninteracting band dispersion, that identifies strongly correlated regions in the phase diagram of rhombohedral graphene as a function of carrier density and displacement field. We develop a neural-network variational Monte Carlo method, combined with Hartree-Fock theory, to solve the interacting ground states. Our calculation reveals a variety of electron crystals with no classical analog. These include, at increasing density: Wigner crystal, self-doped Wiger crystal, as well as ''anticrystal'', a lattice of holes in an electron liquid. We discuss their experimental manifestations and possible connection to superconductivity.

</details>


### [264] [Shape of Wigner Crystals and Hole Self-Doping in a Mexican-Hat Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00170)
*Minho Luke Kim,Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究墨西哥帽色散（ε_k=c_2k^2+c_4k^4）环状费米面在强库仑相互作用下诱导的维格纳晶体（WC）。通过设计轨道形状最小化WC能量，发现低基态能量需要k=0附近电子耗尽的轨道形状。作者引入Jastrow因子及电子-掺杂空穴关联因子，利用变分蒙特卡洛计算标定能带参数，复现了实验观测的从自旋-谷极化费米液体（盘状费米面）→环状费米面→维格纳晶体的两次相变。结果表明，即使在优化轨道形状下，引入电子-空穴关联后，WC过渡附近具有k=0空穴自掺杂的WC仍可能量占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强库仑相互作用下墨西哥帽色散系统中环状费米面诱导的维格纳晶体形成机制，并解释实验中观察到的从费米液体到维格纳晶体的两次密度驱动的相变。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）方法：设计优化轨道形状以最小化WC能量；引入Jastrow因子描述库仑关联，并加入电子与掺杂空穴之间的关联因子；标定有效能带参数c_2和c_4以复现实验相变。

Result: 1) 低基态能量需要k=0处电子耗尽的轨道形状；2) 即使在优化轨道形状下，加入电子-空穴关联后，具有k=0空穴自掺杂的WC在相变点附近仍可能量占优；3) 估算了掺杂空穴的色散关系。

Conclusion: 电子-空穴关联对WC在相变点附近的稳定至关重要，k=0空穴自掺杂现象可在环状费米面系统中出现，这为理解相关实验现象提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study Wigner crystals (WCs) induced by a strong Coulomb interaction from the ring-like Fermi surface of a Mexican-hat dispersion $ε_k= c_2k^2+c_4k^4$. We design orbital shape in order to minimize the energy of the WC, and find that a low ground-state energy requires an orbital shape with a depletion of electrons near $k=0$. To capture the Coulomb-induced correlations, we include a Jastrow factor as well as a factor describing the correlation between electrons and doped vacancies. Using variational Monte Carlo calculations, we calibrate the effective band parameters $c_2$ and $c_4$ to reproduce the two transitions observed experimentally as the electron density is lowered: from a spin-valley-polarized Fermi liquid with a disk-like Fermi surface, to one with a ring-like Fermi surface, and finally to a WC. We find that, even with an optimized orbital shape that depletes electrons near $k=0$, a WC with hole self-doping near $k=0$ can still be energetically favorable near the WC transition, provided that the electron-vacancy correlation is included. We also estimate the dispersion of the doped hole.

</details>


### [265] [Chiral Magnon Mixing by Symmetry-Breaking in Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00210)
*Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,S. X. M. Riberolles,Bing Li,Tianxiong Han,D. M. Pajerowski,D. L. Abernathy,Barry Winn,Melissa Graves-Brook,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er)亚铁磁材料中具有相反动力学手性的左右手性磁振子能带，发现在基平面内有限动量处发生能带交叉，且该交叉的性质（节线或能隙）取决于亚铁磁序的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁体中的磁振子具有由右手进动定义的自旋角动量，而反铁磁体中左右手性磁振子在无外场时简并。亚铁磁体具有未补偿的磁子晶格，理论上应天然存在由内部分子场劈裂能量的左右手性磁振子，但此前缺乏实验/理论验证，本文旨在探索亚铁磁体中手性磁振子的行为。

Method: 以RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er)亚铁磁材料为研究对象，通过分析其磁性亚晶格结构，研究左右手性磁振子能带在基平面内有限动量处的交叉行为，并探讨如何通过改变稀土磁各向异性或施加外场来调控亚铁磁序的对称性，从而影响能带交叉的结构（保持节线或被能隙打开）。

Result: 发现RMn6Sn6亚铁磁体确实具有左右手性磁振子能带，且它们在基平面内有限动量处发生交叉，定义了具有相反动力学手性的模式。该能带交叉可保持为节线或被打成能隙，取决于亚铁磁序的对称性。在能隙打开的模式中含有杂化的手性激发，其手性随动量k变化。

Conclusion: 亚铁磁体中存在天然分立的左右手性磁振子能带，其交叉点的拓扑性质可通过磁对称性调控，为手性磁振子的调控和应用提供了新的平台。

Abstract: Magnons in ferromagnets possess spin angular momentum defined by right-handed precession of the moment around the magnetization direction. In antiferromagnets with no net magnetization, left-and right-handed magnons are degenerate in the absence of an applied field. Ferrimagnets possess uncompensated magnetic sublattices, which should natively possess right-and left-handed magnons where their energy is split by the internal molecular field. Here, we show that RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er) ferrimagnets possess right and left-handed magnon bands that cross at finite momentum (k) within the basal plane, defining modes with opposite dynamical chirality. Depending on the symmetry of the ferrimagnetic order, which may be manipulated by varying the rare-earth magnetic anisotropy or with applied field, the band crossing may remain a nodal line or may be gapped. The gapped modes contain hybridized chiral excitations whose chirality becomes k-dependent.

</details>


### [266] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splitting and correlations: a ghost rotationally invariant slave boson treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00254)
*Xue Sun,Walber Hugo Brito,Andreas Gleis,Ran Adler,Tsung-Han Lee,Gabriel Kotliar,Corey Peters*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用最近提出的鬼旋转不变滑移玻色子（GRISB）方法，系统地研究了t2g Hubbard-Kanamori模型中自旋轨道耦合、晶体场劈裂与电子关联之间的相互作用，以Sr2RuO4为具体研究对象，考察了GRISB方案向动力学平均场理论（DMFT）及实验结果的收敛性，并揭示了U增强自旋轨道耦合而J抑制自旋轨道耦合的关键结论，同时研究了应变驱动的Lifshitz相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋轨道耦合（SOC）与电子关联效应如何在多轨道强关联材料中相互竞争是凝聚态物理的核心问题。已有方法（如DMFT）在低温宽参数范围内计算成本高昂，而GRISB作为一种近似于DMFT的低成本方法，其收敛性和适用性尚未被系统验证。作者希望通过系统研究GRISB方法的收敛行为，并澄清Sr2RuO4中SOC与关联强度的相互作用机制。

Method: 采用鬼旋转不变滑移玻色子（GRISB）方法求解t2g Hubbard-Kanamori模型，以Sr2RuO4的紧束缚模型为基础，模型参数取自DFT和线性化准粒子自洽GW（LQSGW）计算。通过逐步增加"鬼态"数量来检查GRISB向DMFT和实验结果的收敛过程；在低温下对Hubbard U和Hund耦合J的宽参数范围进行扫描，考察SOC驱动的静态与动态可观测量，并借助不同方法比较费米面形状及应变诱导的Lifshitz相变。

Result: GRISB方法在大多数可观测量上收敛迅速。核心发现为：Hubbard相互作用U增强自旋轨道耦合效应，而Hund耦合J则抑制自旋轨道耦合效应。此外，通过不同方法的比较研究了费米面形状，并分析了Sr2RuO4在应变作用下发生的Lifshitz相变。

Conclusion: GRISB方法能够在低温宽参数范围内高效且准确地描述含SOC的多轨道强关联体系，并快速收敛到DMFT结果。研究明确揭示了U与J对SOC的相反调控作用，为理解Sr2RuO4中自旋轨道耦合与电子关联的复杂竞争关系提供了清晰的理论图景，同时为应变调控费米面拓扑（Lifshitz相变）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splittings, and electronic correlations in the $t_{2g}$ Hubbard-Kanamori model within the recently formulated ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson method (GRISB). In particular, we study a tight binding model of Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ with parameters extracted from density functional theory and linearized quasiparticle self-consistent GW (LQSGW) calculations; we study the behavior of different physical quantities as the number of ghosts increases to examine the convergence of GRISB to dynamical mean field theory (DMFT) and experimental results; and we leverage the ability of GRISB to investigate the model over a wide range of parameters at low temperature. In particular, we examine both static and dynamical observables driven by the spin orbit coupling (SOC) and study how they vary as a function of the Hubbard $U$ and Hund's coupling $J$. GRISB converges quickly for most of these observables, and the calculations reveal the following: $U$ enhances the spin-orbit coupling while $J$ suppresses it. We also study the shape of the Fermi surface within different methodologies, and examine the Lifshitz transition which takes place as a function of strain in this material.

</details>


### [267] [Breakdown of Universal Whirling Order in a Heisenberg Tsai-Type Approximant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00467)
*Farid Labib,Guillaume Beutier,Alessandro Bombardi,Marc de Boissieu,Ryuji Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过X射线共振磁散射实验，发现非共面旋转磁序（whirling magnetic order）在Heisenberg型Tsai准晶近似物中并非普适，其成立需要自旋各向异性而非仅仅依赖Tsai簇几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明非共面旋转磁序是非Heisenberg型Tsai准晶近似物中的普适磁态，但该普适性是否适用于Heisenberg极限尚未得到验证。该研究旨在检验其在Heisenberg体系中的普适性，以厘清旋转磁序的真正起源（几何 vs 自旋各向异性）。

Method: 利用X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）技术，对Heisenberg型Au-Al-Gd和non-Heisenberg型Au-Al-Tb两个1/1近似物进行系统测量，对比两者的方位角依赖模式，并将实验结果与既有的旋转磁序模型进行拟合与对照分析。

Result: non-Heisenberg的Au-Al-Tb体系与已知的旋转磁结构吻合良好；而Heisenberg的Au-Al-Gd体系展现出截然不同的方位角依赖，与普适旋转磁序模型不相容。两个体系虽然晶体结构几乎相同、磁能标相当，且具有相同的传播矢量k=(1,0,0)，却稳定了不同的反铁磁基态。

Conclusion: 旋转磁序的普适性并非由Tsai簇几何结构单独决定，而是依赖于自旋各向异性。该结果还揭示了在Heisenberg极限下存在一系列近简并的竞争磁态。

Abstract: Noncoplanar whirling magnetic order has recently been proposed as a universal magnetic state in non-Heisenberg Tsai-type quasicrystal approximants. Here, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering measurements on Heisenberg and non-Heisenberg Au-Al-R (R = Gd, Tb) 1/1 approximants reveal a clear breakdown of this universality in the Heisenberg limit. While the non-Heisenberg Au-Al-Tb system is well described by the previously identified whirling magnetic structure, the Heisenberg Au-Al-Gd counterpart exhibits qualitatively different azimuthal-angle dependences that are incompatible with the universal whirling-order model. Despite nearly identical crystal structures and comparable magnetic energy scales, the two systems stabilize distinct antiferromagnetic ground states with the same propagation vector k = (1, 0, 0), demonstrating that the apparent universality of whirling order is not a consequence of Tsai-cluster geometry alone but requires spin anisotropy. These results further reveal the emergence of a competing manifold of nearly degenerate magnetic states in the Heisenberg limit.

</details>


### [268] [Negative thermal expansion, lattice dynamics, and complex magnetism in TbFeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00469)
*Shubham Farswan,Reshma Kumawat,Dipankar Sarkar,Deeksha Singh,Md. Atif Hasan,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Kaushik Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线衍射、直流磁化、拉曼散射和X射线光电子能谱对正交铁酸盐TbFeO3进行了温度依赖性研究，发现负热膨胀、自旋-声子耦合、双磁子激发和低温下出现的局域磁-晶格异常，揭示了晶格动力学、自旋关联与局域磁晶格异常之间的显著相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示正交铁酸盐TbFeO3中晶格动力学、自旋关联与局域磁-晶格异常之间的复杂相互作用，理解该体系中自旋-声子耦合和异常热膨胀行为背后的物理机制。

Method: 采用多种互补实验手段：X射线衍射（5-300 K）探测晶体结构变化，直流磁化测量表征磁性质，拉曼散射研究声子模式和自旋激发，以及室温X射线光电子能谱补充电子结构信息。通过分析拉曼模频率偏离Klemens非谐衰减模型的预言、线形从高斯到高斯-洛伦兹混合的转变、双磁子激发谱及低温新拉曼模的出现来揭示物理机制。

Result: 发现5-300 K范围内存在负热膨胀现象但无结构相变；拉曼声子频率明显偏离Klemens模型预示自旋-声子耦合；Ag和B1g声子模线形随温度从高斯主导过渡到混合高斯-洛伦兹型；高能区域观察到与Fe子晶格相关的双磁子激发但谱权重温度依赖弱；~175 K以下出现类序参量演化的新拉曼模并与声子异常同步，但双磁子响应无明显异常。

Conclusion: TbFeO3被确立为展示晶格动力学、自旋关联和涌现局域磁晶格异常之间显著相互作用的体系，低温下的新拉曼模与声子异常为理解复杂氧化物中磁-晶格耦合提供了重要线索。

Abstract: We report a temperature-dependent investigation of orthoferrite TbFeO$_3$ using x-ray diffraction, DC magnetization, and Raman scattering, complemented by room-temperature x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals negative thermal expansion over 5-300 K, with a small but systematic increase in unit-cell volume upon cooling in the absence of any structural phase transition. Raman scattering measurements identify the Raman-active phonon modes and show clear deviations from the conventional Klemens anharmonic decay model, particularly in phonon frequencies, indicating the presence of spin-phonon coupling. Two modes of $A_g$ and $B_{1g}$ symmetry exhibit a crossover from Gaussian-dominated line shapes at low temperatures to mixed Gaussian-Lorentzian profiles at higher temperatures, reflecting a transition from inhomogeneous broadening to lifetime-driven dynamics. High-energy Raman spectra reveal two-magnon excitations associated with the Fe sublattice, consistent with linear spin-wave theory, whose spectral weight shows only weak temperature dependence. In addition, a broad Raman mode emerging below $\sim 175$ K exhibits an order-parameter-like temperature evolution and coincides with the onset of phonon anomalies, while no corresponding strong anomaly is observed in the two-magnon response. Taken together, these results establish TbFeO$_3$ as a system with pronounced interplay among lattice dynamics, spin correlations, and emergent local magnetic-lattice anomalies.

</details>


### [269] [Spin-orbit-entangled frustrated magnetism in fcc Ba$_2$(Yb,Nd)NbO$_6$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01022)
*S. M. Hossain,Sk. Soyeb Ali,S. Mohanty,R. Kolay,M. P. Saravanan,A. K. Yogi,Y. Tokiwa,R. Nath,S. K. Panda,M. Majumder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种稀土基双钙钛矿材料Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆，其具有面心立方结构。通过磁化率、比热测量和DFT计算，确认了由强自旋轨道耦合和晶体场效应产生的孤立jeff=1/2 Kramers双重态基态。毫开尔文温度下的热力学测量揭示了两种化合物中存在具有中等阻挫的长程磁有序，其形成机制可能符合"序由无序"（order-by-disorder）场景。研究将这些材料确定为有前景的稀土自旋-轨道纠缠磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev材料的搜索主要集中在4d和5d过渡金属化合物上，而稀土4f体系的研究大多局限于蜂窝和三角晶格。作者希望通过研究面心立方结构中的稀土基双钙钛矿材料，拓展Kitaev材料在稀土体系中的候选范围，探索新的自旋-轨道纠缠磁性材料。

Method: 对Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆进行了磁化率测量和比热测量，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算验证电子结构；并在毫开尔文温度范围内进行热力学测量以探测磁有序和阻挫行为。

Result: 两种化合物中均确认了由强SOC和晶体电场效应产生的孤立jeff=1/2 Kramers双重态基态；毫开尔文温度下的测量显示两种材料都表现出具有中等阻挫的长程磁有序。

Conclusion: Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆被确定为有前景的稀土自旋-轨道纠缠磁体，其磁有序可能遵循"序由无序"机制。未来需要更全面的交换张量测定来阐明磁相互作用的微观起源。

Abstract: The search for candidate Kitaev materials has largely focused on 4$d$ and 5$d$ transition-metal compounds with various lattice geometries. In contrast, investigations of rare-earth 4$f$ systems have thus far been restricted mainly to honeycomb and triangular lattices. In this work, we investigate the rare-earth-based double perovskites Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$, which crystallize in a face-centered cubic structure. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements establish isolated ${j_{\rm eff}} = 1/2$ Kramers doublet ground states arising from strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and crystal electric-field effects, which are further supported by density-functional theory calculations. Millikelvin-temperature thermodynamic measurements reveal long-range magnetic order with moderate frustration in both compounds. The emergence of magnetic order may be understood within an order-by-disorder scenario, as theoretically proposed for rare-earth fcc lattices with finite Kitaev interactions. Our results thus identify Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$ as promising rare-earth spin-orbit-entangled magnets and motivate further experimental and theoretical investigations aimed at determining the complete exchange tensor to elucidate the microscopic origin of the underlying magnetic interactions.

</details>


### [270] [Magnetic circular dichroism of THz modes and selection rules of Raman-active optical phonons in the polar altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01062)
*F. Schilberth,K. Vasin,M. Knauft,M. Kondákor,M. Vuckovic,N. Herrmann,K. Penc,M. Minola,B. Keimer,V. A. Martinez,K. Panda,A. A. Sirenko,L. Prodan,V. Tsurkan,A. A. Tsirlin,I. Kézsmárki,J. Deisenhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过温度依赖的拉曼散射和磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱，系统地研究了共线交变磁体候选材料Mn2Mo3O8中的磁性和振动激发，并结合第一性原理计算识别了所有光学声子，包括此前未被观测到的最低拉曼模式。研究发现磁有序态下不存在预期的简并E2声子圆偏振态分裂，但在宽带太赫兹激发上观测到强磁圆二色性，该信号可能源于双磁振子激发，且具有场依赖的双组分精细结构，在4 T自旋翻转跃迁以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnet）作为新兴的磁性材料类别，兼具反铁磁的无净磁化与铁磁的能带分裂特性。Mn2Mo3O8被认为是共线交变磁体候选材料，但其磁振子与声子在磁有序态下的耦合行为、激发模式的选择定则及对称性破缺效应尚未被系统研究。本文旨在通过高精度光谱实验与理论计算相结合，全面表征该材料的声子谱，并探究磁有序引入后振动模式的分裂与磁光响应行为。

Method: 采用温度依赖的拉曼散射光谱和磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱两种互补手段，在宽温区和磁场范围内测量Mn2Mo3O8的光学响应；结合第一性原理计算精确复现振动本征模式的频率，用于声子模式的归属与识别；进一步对比顺磁相和磁有序相中光学活性声子的选择定则，并基于赝角动量守恒分析拉曼选择定则；通过磁圆二色性测量探测太赫兹波段的磁激发。

Result: 成功识别了所有光学声子模式，包括此前拉曼研究中遗漏的最低能A1和E2型拉曼模式。在磁有序相中未观察到顺磁态简并的E2光学声子分裂为圆偏振模式的现象，归因于Mn2+较弱的自旋轨道耦合。然而，在磁有序态中出现宽带太赫兹波段的强磁圆二色性信号，该信号仅具有电偶极活性，可能源于双磁振子激发，且表现出场依赖的双组分精细结构，在4 T自旋翻转跃迁以上该二色性消失。

Conclusion: 本研究完整建立了Mn2Mo3O8的声子谱归属，并揭示了交变磁体中声子-磁子耦合的若干重要特征：由于弱自旋轨道耦合，磁有序并未引起的声子圆偏振分裂；但观察到可能的双磁振子宽带电偶极激发及其精细结构，说明Mn2Mo3O8中磁振子激发具有独特的磁光性质，并为深入理解交变磁体的激发谱提供了实验基础。

Abstract: We investigated the magnetic and vibrational excitations in the collinear altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} by temperature dependent Raman scattering and magneto-optical THz time-domain transmission spectroscopy. By comparison to \textit{ab initio} calculations accurately capturing the eigenfrequencies of the vibrational eigenmodes, we identify all optical phonons, including the lowest-lying Raman modes of $A_1$ and $E_2$ type, which had remained elusive in a previous Raman study. Moreover, we compare the selection rules for optically active phonons in the paramagnetic and the magnetically ordered phases of \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} and analyze the Raman selection rules with respect to pseudo-angular momentum conservation. No evidence of the expected splitting of the degenerate paramagnetic $E_2$ optical phonons into modes with circular polarization upon magnetic ordering could be resolved, likely due to weak spin-orbit coupling typical for Mn$^{2+}$. In contrast, we observe strong magnetic circular dichroism at a broad THz excitation band, emerging in the magnetically ordered state. This band, potentially originating from two-magnon excitations, is only electric-dipole active and features a field-dependent two-component fine structure. Its magnetic circular dichroism vanishes above the spin-flop transition at 4~T.

</details>


### [271] [Pressure-induced self-doping and Fermi surface reconstruction in UAs2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01244)
*Zhenchao Wu,Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用DFT+DMFT方法研究了压力下重费米子化合物UAs2的电子结构，揭示了压力诱导的轨道选择性自掺杂效应（电子从局域5f5/2轨道转移至巡游5f7/2轨道），并发现超导出现在费米面由两块分离片层构成且具有增强嵌套性的压力区间，表明费米面嵌套和电荷涨落可能共同促成了其增强的超导转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAs2是目前报道的铀基关联5f电子超导体中最高Tc的重费米子化合物，但其超导的微观起源尚不明确。作者希望通过第一性原理计算阐明其电子结构，特别是压力如何调控其超导电性。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）方法，对UAs2在常压和压力下的电子结构进行计算，分析5f轨道占据态的变化、费米面拓扑结构及其随压力的演化。

Result: 常压下复现了Kondo晶格电子结构特征（Γ和M点附近的平杂化带），与ARPES一致；压力下电子从局域5f5/2轨道转移至巡游5f7/2轨道，总5f占据数基本不变；超导出现在费米面由两块分离片层构成且具增强嵌套性的压力区间，当片层弯曲合并为三维波纹柱状时超导消失。

Conclusion: UAs2的超导可能由费米面嵌套和电荷涨落共同驱动，这使其区别于传统重费米子超导体，为理解其超导机制提供了电子结构层面的基础。

Abstract: Superconductivity has recently been reported in the heavy-fermion compound UAs2 under pressure, with the highest Tc among uranium-based correlated 5f-electron superconductors. To elucidate its microscopic origin, we investigate its electronic structure using density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). At ambient pressure, our calculations reproduce the characteristic Kondo-lattice electronic structure, with flat hybridization bands near the Fermi energy around the Γ and M points, in good agreement with angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Under pressure, we find a systematic transfer of electrons from the more localized 5f5/2 orbitals to the more itinerant 5f7/2 orbitals, while the total U-5f occupancy remains nearly unchanged. This orbital-selective charge redistribution constitutes a pressure-induced self-doping effect that drives the 5f5/2 electrons from a localized Kondo regime toward a mixed-valence regime with enhanced charge fluctuations, leading to a dramatic reconstruction of the low-energy electronic structure. Remarkably, superconductivity emerges in the pressure range where the Fermi surface consists of two disconnected sheets with enhanced nesting, but disappears when they bend and merge into a corrugated three-dimensional cylinder. Our results provide an electronic-structure basis for understanding superconductivity in UAs2 and suggest that Fermi-surface nesting and charge fluctuations may contribute to the enhanced superconducting Tc, pointing to a possible distinction from conventional heavy-fermion superconductors.

</details>


### [272] [All-Electric Topological Phase Transitions in Proximity-Coupled Bilayer MnBi2Te4 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01312)
*Basavaraja G,Mukul Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需外加磁场的栅极可调机制，通过将MnBi2Te4薄膜与铁磁绝缘体界面耦合，利用磁各向异性的栅极调控动态调节近邻交换偏置，实现层间耦合和能带拓扑的全电切换，从而在超薄MnBi2Te4中实现轴子态与量子反常霍尔态之间的可逆相变，并将磁有序温度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnBi2Te4在二维极限下可通过反铁磁层间耦合实现厚度依赖的轴子态和量子反常霍尔绝缘态，但两者之间的可控转换需要超过9T的极端外磁场，严重限制了实际可调性和器件应用。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算与有效哈密顿量建模，通过在MnBi2Te4与铁磁绝缘体界面引入磁近邻效应；利用栅极电压调节铁磁绝缘体中的磁各向异性，动态调制近邻诱导的交换偏置，实现层间耦合与能带拓扑的全电调控；并通过长程海森堡蒙特卡洛模拟验证磁有序温度的提升。

Result: 实现了无磁场条件下MnBi2Te4中轴子态与量子反常霍尔态的可逆拓扑相变；栅极可调策略消除了对高磁场的依赖，同时包覆的MnBi2Te4薄膜的磁有序温度被大幅提高。

Conclusion: 该栅极可调范式解决了几何拓扑物理中的关键瓶颈（高场依赖与低热稳定性），为可扩展、高温拓扑电子学器件提供了切实可行的路线。

Abstract: The intrinsic magnetic topological insulator MnBi2Te4, in the two-dimensional limit, hosts thickness dependent axion and quantum anomalous Hal (QAH) insulating states governed by antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. However, controlled interconversion between these phases typically requires extreme external magnetic fields exceeding 9 T, limiting practical tunability. Using complementary first-principles calculations and effective Hamiltonian modeling, we demonstrate a field-free, reversible mechanism to engineer topological phase transitions by exploiting magnetic proximity at the interfaces with a ferromagnetic insulator. Gate-tunable magnetic anisotropy within the ferromagnetic insulator dynamically modulates the proximity-induced exchange bias, enabling all-electric switching of interlayer coupling and band topology in ultrathin MnBi2Te4. Crucially, long-range Heisenberg Monte Carlo simulations reveal that the magnetic ordering temperature of the encapculated MnBi2Te4 film is dramatically elevated. By eliminating the high-field requirement and simultaneously improving thermal stability, this gate-tunable paradigm solves a critical bottleneck in topological physics and offers a viable route toward scalable, high-temperature topological electronics.

</details>


### [273] [Quantum geometry and RKKY in flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01363)
*Chang-geun Oh,Makoto Shimizu,Youichi Yanase,Shuichi Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了平带极限下的RKKY相互作用，发现虽然平带中群速度为零，但RKKY相互作用并未消失，而是由Bloch态的量子几何（特别是Brillouin区平均量子度规）所介导。量子度规控制了静态磁化率的长期结构，从而决定磁关联长度和自旋刚度；Wannier函数的有限空间展宽即使在没有单粒子色散的情况下也提供了有效的长程耦合通道。在有限和低维体系中，量子度规还主导了有序温度，绕过了Mermin-Wagner定理的热力学极限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统基于色散（群速度）的RKKY相互作用图景。在平带极限下，群速度消失，传统理论预言RKKY相互作用应被熄灭，但作者旨在证明RKKY相互作用在平带中仍然存在，并由量子几何而非色散所介导。

Method: 从微观RKKY推导出发，将Brillouin区平均量子度规与静态磁化率的长期结构建立联系。利用Wannier函数的有限空间展宽来描述长程耦合通道，并分析有限尺寸与低维体系中量子度规对磁有序温度的影响，从而在有限体系中规避Mermin-Wagner定理的约束。

Result: 证明平带极限下RKKY相互作用由量子度规控制，量子度规决定了磁关联长度和自旋刚度；有限空间展宽的Wannier函数提供了缺失色散时的有效长程耦合；增大量子度规可增强磁刚性并提升有限尺寸体系中的临界温度。

Conclusion: 平带体系中的磁性交换并非被消除，而是由量子几何所支配。这一机制为平带系统（如莫尔超晶格和扁平能带材料）中磁有序的起源提供了新的理论框架，并指出量子度规可作为调控磁性的一种关键参数。

Abstract: Flat conduction bands quench the group velocity and thus challenge conventional, dispersion-driven pictures of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction, where localized moments are coupled via an effective exchange mediated by conduction electrons. Here we show that RKKY interactions in the flat-band limit are not extinguished by the vanishing group velocity but are instead mediated by the quantum geometry of Bloch states. Starting from a microscopic RKKY derivation, we demonstrate that the Brillouin-zone-averaged quantum metric controls the long-wavelength structure of the static susceptibility, thereby determining the magnetic correlation length and the spin stiffness. As a result, the finite spatial spread of Wannier functions provides an effective long-range coupling channel even when single-particle dispersion is absent. Furthermore, we establish the general principle that the ordering temperature is governed by the quantum metric in finite and low-dimensional samples, effectively circumventing the thermodynamic-limit constraint of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Specifically, our theoretical investigation reveals that increasing the quantum metric enhances magnetic rigidity and leads to a corresponding rise in the critical temperature within finite-sized systems.

</details>


### [274] [Emergent modular Luttinger liquid from spin-partitioned entanglement in the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01817)
*Ádám Bácsi,Catalin Pascu Moca,Balázs Dóra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维排斥哈伯德模型中自旋分区的纠缠哈密顿量。通过玻色化与精确对角化相结合，发现对一种自旋分量求迹后产生模卢廷格液体，其性质与物理系统根本不同：模谱完全无色散且具有动量无关的纠缠能隙，但其本征态展现出由单一有效卢廷格参数（电荷与自旋卢廷格参数的几何平均）控制的代数关联。纠缠谱呈现普适的分支层级结构，与精确对角化高度一致，且模基态与无自旋卢廷格液体近乎相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相互作用一维费米子系统中自旋分区纠缠哈密顿量的普适模结构，探索模卢廷格液体态的特性及其与物理系统的关系。

Method: 结合玻色化方法和精确对角化，对一维排斥哈伯德模型进行自旋分区（对一种自旋分量求迹），构造并分析纠缠哈密顿量（模哈密顿量），通过模谱、纠缠能隙、关联函数和纠缠谱的分支结构来刻画其性质。

Result: 模谱完全无色散且具有动量无关的纠缠能隙；本征态由几何平均的卢廷格参数控制代数关联；纠缠谱显示普适的分支层级；模基态与无自旋卢廷格液体几乎一致，全部结果均与精确对角化吻合。

Conclusion: 一维相互作用费米子系统中存在普适的模结构，自旋分区产生的模卢廷格液体代表了一类新的普适类，其性质由单一有效卢廷格参数（电荷与自旋的几何平均）完全刻画。

Abstract: We study the spin-partitioned entanglement Hamiltonian of the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model. By combining bosonization with exact diagonalization, we show that tracing out one spin species produces a modular Luttinger liquid, whose properties fundamentally differ from those of the physical system. While the modular spectrum is fully dispersionless and possesses a momentum-independent entanglement gap, its eigenstates exhibit algebraic correlations governed by a single effective Luttinger parameter equal to the geometric mean of the charge and spin Luttinger parameters. The resulting entanglement spectrum displays a universal branching hierarchy in excellent agreement with exact diagonalization. We further demonstrate that the modular ground state is nearly identical to that of the spinless Luttinger liquid. These results uncover a universal modular structure in interacting one-dimensional fermionic systems.

</details>


### [275] [Topological Defects in Triple-$Q$ Magnetic Orders: A Fixed-Lattice Homotopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01838)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对N=2和N=3时M点三重Q金兹堡-朗道理论中所有七个稳定相的体缺陷进行分类，该理论具有(V4⋊D3)×O(N)对称性。研究发现正交三重Q相具有完整流形O(3)，产生手性壁和阿贝尔Z2框架涡旋而非非阿贝尔二元多面体涡旋；O(2)相中每个连通分量支持整数2π涡旋，而分数绕数须附着在离散畴壁上并受到线性禁闭。平移对称性进一步禁止交叉梯度双线性项，将二次弹性扇区简化为各向同性和M点锁定各向异性两种刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重Q磁序将连续自旋旋转与离散晶格扇区（平移和点群变换）相结合，产生比传统单Q磁体更丰富的缺陷结构。理解这些缺陷的完整分类对于描述三重Q磁性材料中的拓扑激发和物理行为至关重要。

Method: 在具有(V4⋊D3)×O(N)对称性的M点三重Q金兹堡-朗道理论中，将原子晶格视为预定背景（排除位错和向错），三个傅里叶场保持物理M点标记。利用参考态稳定子H的连通分量G0/(H∩G0)而非投影商来分类缺陷，并系统区分自由内部缺陷、晶畴壁和壁束缚复合体。

Result: 七个稳定相中，正交三重Q相具有完整流形O(3)（手性壁+阿贝尔Z2框架涡旋）；O(2)相每个连通分量支持整数2π涡旋，分数绕数仅能附着在离散畴壁上且在线性壁张力下被禁闭；弹性扇区简化为各向同性和各向异性两种刚度。

Conclusion: 该分类清晰区分了三重Q磁体中的自由内部缺陷、晶畴壁和壁束缚复合体，揭示了多重Q系统中缺陷结构的丰富性和独特性，为后续实验和理论研究提供了完整的拓扑框架。

Abstract: Multiple-$Q$ magnetic orders combine continuous spin rotations with discrete crystalline sectors associated with translations and point-group transformations, producing a richer defect structure than conventional single-$Q$ magnets. We classify the bulk defects of all seven stable phases for $N=2$ and $3$ in the $M$-point triple-$Q$ Ginzburg--Landau theory with $(\Vfour\rtimes\Dthree)\times\OO(N)$ symmetry, where $\Vfour$ is the translation-generated Klein four-group. The atomic lattice is treated as a prescribed background, with lattice dislocations and disclinations excluded and the three Fourier fields retaining their physical $M$-point labels. The parent-group transformations continuously connected to the identity form $G_0=\{e\}\times\SO(N)$. For a reference-state stabilizer $H$, the connected component containing the reference state is $G_0/(H\cap G_0)$, not the quotient obtained by projecting $H$ onto spin space. This distinction gives the orthogonal triple-$Q$ phase the full manifold $\OO(3)$, with chirality walls and Abelian $\ZZ_2$ frame vortices rather than non-Abelian binary-polyhedral vortices. Every connected component of the $\OO(2)$ phases supports an integer $2π$ vortex, whereas fractional windings close only when attached to a discrete-domain wall and are linearly confined at nonzero wall tension. Translation symmetry further forbids cross-gradient bilinears, reducing the quadratic elastic sector to an isotropic and an $M$-point-locked anisotropic stiffness. The classification separates free internal defects, crystalline domain walls, and wall-bound composites in triple-$Q$ magnets.

</details>


### [276] [Diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles in fragmented spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01883)
*Anoop Raj,Sumiran Pujari,Ludovic D. C. Jaubert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁性碎裂自旋冰系统中畴壁的理论概念验证，设计了能够控制磁单极子（magnetricity）输运的二极管和电容器器件，并通过百万级自旋模拟验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋冰材料中的准粒子激发（磁单极子）能够传导交流磁电流（即magnetricity），但迄今为止几乎没有人设计器件来控制这种磁单极子输运。本文旨在填补这一空白，为控制magnetricity提供器件层面的设计概念。

Method: 利用具有磁断裂（magnetic fragmentation）性质的系统，其中自旋冰物理与长程反铁磁序共存。关键思想是利用磁序中存在的畴壁：在特定制备条件下，畴壁可充当单极子的非对称滤波器——在某一方向允许正电荷通过而排斥负电荷，反方向则相反，从而实现二极管的整流功能。多个连续畴壁可将正负电荷分离并形成电荷真空区，构成电容器。

Result: 通过超过一百万个自旋的模拟验证了所有微观机制，证明该畴壁设计确实能实现单极子的二极管和电容器功能，充电后的电容器在原理上可用作单极子电池。研究还讨论了在稀土烧绿石氧化物和人工自旋冰中的实验应用前景。

Conclusion: 磁碎裂自旋冰中的畴壁可被视为一个分隔两个由时间反演对称性联系起来的"镜像世界"的涌现边界。这项工作不仅为控制magnetricity提供了理论基础，还为超越自旋冰的领域打开了新方向，例如在手性及向列自旋液体中研究涌现准粒子穿越畴壁的动力学。

Abstract: Spin-ice materials are famous for their quasi-particle excitations that behave like magnetic monopoles. Magnetricity is the concept that these monopoles can conduct an AC magnetic current, in analogy with conduction electrons. While monopole dynamics has been intensively studied and is reasonably well understood, very little has been done to design devices in order to control magnetricity. Here we develop a theoretical proof of concept for the design of diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles. We use the property of systems with magnetic fragmentation, where spin-ice physics co-exists with long-range antiferromagnetic order. The key point is that magnetic order allows for the existence of domain walls. Under certain conditions of preparation, this domain wall is equivalent to an asymmetric filter for monopoles. In a given direction, positive charges can go through while negative ones are repelled; the opposite applies in the opposite direction. This asymmetry effectively functions like a diode for monopole current. Successive domain walls separate positive from negative charges with a vacuum of charge in between, producing a capacitor for monopoles. Once the capacitor is charged, it can in principle be used as a battery for monopoles. All microscopic mechanisms are explained and our proof of concept is validated by simulations of more than a million spins. Application to experiments are discussed for rare-earth pyrochlore oxides and artificial spin ice. Finally, we discuss in general terms how a domain wall in fragmented spin ice can also be seen as an emergent boundary separating two mirror "worlds" separated by time-reversal symmetry. Beyond spin ice, our work opens a promising direction of investigation for the dynamics of emergent quasi-particles crossing domain walls in chiral and nematic spin liquids, which also possess a broken symmetry.

</details>


### [277] [Microscopic magnetic phase evolution in the Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn revealed by $μ^+$SR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01892)
*U. Miniotaite,O. K. Forslund,H. Luetkens,V. Rai,J. Persson,G. D. Morris,D. Zuhair,D. W. Tam,S. Nandi,Y. Sassa,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过μ+SR和体磁化率测量，系统研究了反铁磁Weyl半金属Mn3Sn的磁相变与磁性演化。样品中存在三个特征温度（TN=418K反铁磁转变、Tt≈275K从非公度螺旋态到公度反三角态的转变、Tf=21K疑似自旋玻璃冻结）。研究未发现静态自旋玻璃态，而是观察到铁磁分量增大伴随局域自旋涨落减慢。非公度螺旋相中磁场分布不对称呈非谐性特征，150K以上出现相共存区，公度反三角相中约20%的μ子发生超快退极化，325K以上观察到μ子占据位的温度驱动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn3Sn是一种备受关注的反铁磁Weyl半金属，其磁结构高度复杂，涉及公度反三角（IT）反铁磁相、非公度（IC）螺旋反铁磁相以及可能存在的低温自旋玻璃态。然而这些相之间的边界、相变机制以及低温态的微观磁性本质尚不明确，需要利用对局域磁性敏感的微观探针（如μ+SR）进行深入表征。

Method: 采用零场（ZF）和体磁化率测量相结合的方法。样品为Mn2.99Sn（Mn3Sn）。通过变温μ+SR谱拟合提取内磁场分布、初始不对称度（missing fraction）和μ子占据位等信息，并结合磁化率确定各相变温度。

Result: 确定了三个特征温度：TN=418K、Tt≈275K、Tf=21K。在Tf以下未发现静态自旋玻璃态，而是铁磁分量增大伴随局域自旋涨落减慢。IC螺旋相中内磁场分布不对称表明磁结构存在强烈的非谐性修正。150K以上观察到IC螺旋与IT反铁磁相的宽域共存。IT相中约20%μ子发生超快退极化，325K以上出现μ子占据位的温度驱动迁移。

Conclusion: Mn3Sn展现出高度动态的磁性景观，其复杂磁序往往共存并随温度连续演化。此前提出的低温自旋玻璃态需要修正——低温下实际是铁磁分量的增强和局域自旋涨落的减慢，且两者可能去耦。该研究深化了对Mn3Sn微观磁结构随温度演化机制的理解。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive muon spin relaxation ($μ^+$SR) and bulk magnetization study of the antiferromagnetic (AFM) Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn (composition Mn$_{2.99}$Sn). Mn$_3$Sn is reported to exhibit a commensurate inverse triangular (IT) AFM phase, an incommensurate (IC) helical AFM phase, and a proposed low-temperature spin-glass-like state. In our sample, we establish the respective transition temperatures for these phases to be $T_\mathrm{N} = 418$ K, $T_\mathrm{t} \approx 275$ K, and $T_\mathrm{f} = 21$ K. Investigating the low-temperature regime below $T_\mathrm{f}$, we find no evidence of a static spin-glass state. Instead, the sample exhibits an increasing ferromagnetic (FM) component accompanied by a localized slowing of spin fluctuations, indicating that these phenomena may be decoupled. In the IC helical AFM phase, fitting the zero-field (ZF) spectra reveals an asymmetric internal magnetic-field distribution, indicating that the magnetic structure is heavily modified by anharmonicity. Upon warming above 150 K, a continuous redistribution of muon spectral weight reveals a broad regime of coexisting local magnetic environments associated with the IC helical and IT-AFM phases. In the commensurate IT-AFM phase above $T_\mathrm{t}$, a persistent missing fraction in the initial asymmetry indicates that a subset of implanted muons, corresponding to roughly 20% of the sample-related asymmetry, undergoes unresolved ultrafast depolarization. Finally, we observe temperature-driven shifts in muon site populations above 325 K. Ultimately, our results show a highly dynamic magnetic landscape in Mn$_3$Sn, demonstrating how its complex magnetic orders often coexist and evolve continuously with temperature.

</details>


### [278] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of Electronic and Magnetic Correlations in Bilayer Nickelate La3Ni2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02261)
*Jian Zhou,Rui Song,Haiyan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT、cRPA和DMFT组合方法研究加压双层La3Ni2O7，发现高压下Kondo屏蔽效应压过超交换相互作用，导致有效磁交换耦合Jeff被抑制，从而合理解释了Tc随压力呈穹顶状下降的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加压La3Ni2O7中发现了高温超导电性，但其超导转变温度Tc随压力变化的微观机制尚不清楚，需要从电子和磁关联层面提供理论解释。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、约束随机相位近似（cRPA）和动力学平均场理论（DMFT），系统考察高压下La3Ni2O7的电子结构和磁关联演化，分析轨道分辨的巡游-局域特性变化及有效磁交换耦合。

Result: 发现静水压力虽增强层间跳跃和裸超交换能标（4t2/U），但同时通过降低U/W比值使体系更趋巡游化。轨道选择性演化明显：Ni dx2-y2态愈加巡游，而dz2轨道保持局域特征，两者杂化增强导致Kondo类屏蔽效应显著放大，最终抑制了作为配对胶的Jeff，解释了高压区Tc单调下降。

Conclusion: 高压下La3Ni2O7的Tc穹顶状相图由Kondo屏蔽与超交换相互作用的竞争主导，为高温超导机制提供了自洽的微观图像。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in pressurized bilayer La3Ni2O7 has sparked intense research interest, yet the microscopic mechanism governing its pressure-dependent superconducting transition temperature (Tc) remains elusive. In this work, we investigate the electronic and magnetic correlations of La3Ni2O7 under high pressure using a combination of density-functional theory (DFT), constrained random phase approximation (cRPA), and dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). We find that while hydrostatic pressure enhances the interlayer hopping and the bare superexchange energy scale (4t2/U), it simultaneously drives the system toward a more itinerant regime by reducing the relative correlation strength (U/W). Crucially, our results reveal a distinct orbital-selective evolution: the Ni dx2-y2 states become increasingly itinerant, whereas the Ni dz2 orbitals retain a more localized character. This pressure-induced itinerancy significantly enhances the hybridization between the two, leading to a dramatic amplification of the Kondo-like screening of the local dz2 moments by the itinerant dx2-y2 electrons. Consequently, the effective magnetic exchange coupling (Jeff), which serves as the pairing glue, is suppressed in the high-pressure regime. Our findings suggest that the monotonic decrease of Tc at high pressures is driven by the dominance of Kondo screening over superexchange interactions, providing a coherent microscopic explanation for the dome-shaped superconducting phase diagram in La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [279] [Long and short time linear response of metals: a geometric approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02298)
*Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了量子几何张量在金属中的角色，表明其并非总是亚主导，并计算了金属中依赖时间的量子几何张量，解释了其发散行为，对比了狄拉克和Weyl半金属的奇异几何张量，提出了D/𝒮₁比率作为晶格尺度探针来区分束缚电子与巡游电子，并在kagome金属中验证了该探针的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量对绝缘体、超导体和平带电子性质的描述至关重要，但对金属而言通常被认为在低能描述中是亚主导的。本文作者想要挑战这一传统观点，揭示费米面附近波函数的量子几何在金属中也可能扮演重要角色。

Method: 作者对金属计算了依赖时间的量子几何张量，分析了其发散行为，并与狄拉克和Weyl半金属的奇异几何张量进行了对比。

Result: 识别出D/𝒮₁（Drude权重与总谱权重之比）作为区分束缚电荷与巡游电荷的晶格尺度探针；在kagome金属中验证，发现两个van Hove填充在费米面相同的条件下仍表现出不同的响应。

Conclusion: 量子几何在金属的近费米面描述中具有重要作用，D/𝒮₁比率可作为有效探针用于表征金属中束缚与巡游电子特性。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum geometric tensor, which captures dipole fluctuations of bound electrons, is essential for understanding the electronic properties of insulators, superconductors, and flat bands. It is often considered subleading for low-energy descriptions of metals that are dominated by intra-band processes. Here, we revisit this perspective and highlight scenarios where the quantum geometry of the wavefunctions close to the Fermi surface plays a significant role. We compute the time-dependent quantum geometric tensor for metals, explain its divergence, and contrast it against singular geometric tensors of Dirac and Weyl semi-metals. We identify the ratio of Drude to total spectral weight, $D/\mathcal{S}_1$, as a lattice-scale probe of bound versus itinerant charge, and quantify it in the kagome metal, where the two van Hove fillings respond differently despite identical Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [280] [Optimized Tensor-Network Renormalization for Quantum Dynamics: Resolving the Spectral Function of $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02473)
*Jiahang Hu,Runze Chi,B. Normand,Hai-Jun Liao,T. Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种激发态定制的角转移矩阵重整化群（ET-CTMRG）方法，解决了二维量子系统中无限投影纠缠对态框架下动力学谱函数计算中数值不稳定的问题。该方法将激发张量融入重整化过程，构建更精确的有效哈密顿量矩阵，显著降低了截断误差，并在三角晶格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂的超固态相中与中子散射实验实现了定量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）的动力学谱函数计算方法仅从基态环境构建重整化张量，存在严重的数值不稳定性，限制了其在二维量子系统动力学性质研究中的可靠性。

Method: 提出激发态定制的角转移矩阵重整化群（ET-CTMRG）方法，核心思想是将激发张量纳入重整化过程本身，而非仅依赖基态环境，从而构建更精确的有效哈密顿量矩阵。

Result: 在海森堡反铁磁体中，截断误差降低了数个数量级；在三角晶格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂的超固态相这一复杂情形中，计算结果与非弹性中子散射测量达到了出色的定量一致。

Conclusion: ET-CTMRG为研究强关联量子系统的动力学性质提供了一个稳健且可靠的框架，有望成为二维量子系统激发谱计算的标准工具。

Abstract: Tensor-network methods have opened a powerful route for the study of dynamical spectral functions in two-dimensional quantum systems. However, existing approaches within the framework of infinite projected entangled-pair states construct the required renormalization tensors solely from the ground-state environment and can suffer from severe numerical instability. We identify the origin of this instability and introduce an excitation-tailored corner-transfer-matrix renormalization-group (ET-CTMRG) method to resolve it. By incorporating excitation tensors into the renormalization procedure, the method constructs a substantially more accurate effective Hamiltonian matrix and thereby yields reliable and well-converged excitation spectra. For Heisenberg antiferromagnets, it reduces truncation errors by orders of magnitude and for the particularly complex case of the supersolid phase in the triangular-lattice XXZ magnet $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$, it achieves excellent quantitative agreement with inelastic neutron-scattering measurements. ET-CTMRG therefore provides a robust framework for investigating the dynamical properties of strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Hypergraphs on Riemannian manifolds and their double homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00708)
*Shiquan Ren*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过为Δ-流形的分级子流形构造双重同调，研究了黎曼流形上超（有向）图的同调性质，建立了关于双重同调和微分形式空间的交换图，并探讨了超图基本群（群胚）与曲面辫群之间的关系，进而应用于球填充/覆盖滤过结构的同伦型及正则映射的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超图理论（特别是Δ-流形上的分级子流形）与黎曼流形上的几何和拓扑结构相结合，填补超（有向）图在流形上的同调与同伦性质研究的空白，并探索其与辫群、正则映射等拓扑/几何对象的深层联系。

Method: 构造分级子流形的双重同调理论；利用从有序序列到无序集合的典范投影π建立交换图；通过π诱导的态射研究基本群（群胚）与（纯）辫群的关系；利用超图约束下的球填充/覆盖滤过研究同伦型；借助独立复形与超图的几何实现表征正则映射。

Result: 证明了黎曼流形上超（有向）图双重同调的交换图与微分形式空间的交换图；建立了超图基本群（群胚）与曲面上（纯）辫群之间的同态关系；给出了超图约束下球填充/覆盖滤过的同伦型结果；利用几何实现刻画了黎曼流形上的正则映射。

Conclusion: 该工作将超图代数拓扑与黎曼几何深度融合，为超图约束下的几何结构提供了系统的同调与同伦工具，并揭示了超图拓扑与辫群、正则映射之间新颖且深刻的结构性联系。

Abstract: In this paper, by constructing double homology for graded submanifolds of $Δ$-manifolds, we study the double homology of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds. With the help of the canonical projection $π$ from ordered sequences to their underlying unordered sets, we prove a commutative diagram of the double homology of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds and a commutative diagram of the spaces of differential forms of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds. Besides, we give some relations between the fundamental goup(oid)s of hyper(di)graphs on surfaces and the (pure) braid groups on surfaces by certain homomorphisms induced from $π$. As applications, we study the homotopy types of filtrations for packings and coverings of balls on Riemannian manifolds with hypergraph constraints, and we characterize regular maps on Riemannian manifolds by geometric realizations of the independence complexes and hypergraphs on the manifolds.

</details>


### [282] [Dualizing and canonical complexes on finite posets II: properness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01080)
*Fernando Sancho de Salas*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在有限偏序集的背景下发展了proper空间和proper映射的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拓扑学中proper空间和proper映射的概念推广到有限偏序集这一组合结构中，建立相应的理论框架。

Method: 在有限偏序集的设定下，形式化定义proper空间和proper映射，并建立相应的理论基础。

Result: 在有限偏序集上构造了proper空间与proper映射的完整理论体系。

Conclusion: 成功将proper空间与proper映射的理论从拓扑学推广到有限偏序集的语境中。

Abstract: We develop a theory of proper spaces and proper maps in the context of finite posets.

</details>


### [283] [Sequential $m$-contiguity distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02116)
*Nilay Ekiz Yazici,Nursultan Kuanyshov,Ayse Borat*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入了有限多个单纯映射之间的序列m-邻近距离（sequential m-contiguity distance），作为邻近距离的高维类比，同时推广了高邻近距离和m-邻近距离，并给出了序列m-同伦距离的组合对应物。作者研究了其基本性质（强同伦型不变量、复合、范畴积、重心细分下的表现），并定义了单纯复形的序列m-离散拓扑复杂度。作为应用，作者用序列m-邻近距离刻画了该不变量及m-单纯LS范畴，证明了它们是强同伦型不变量，并建立了m-单纯LS范畴与m-离散序列拓扑复杂度之间的不等式，将拓扑复杂度理论的经典结果推广到单纯和m维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拓扑复杂度理论中的经典概念（如序列同伦距离、离散拓扑复杂度）推广到组合/单纯范畴，并引入m维高维类比，从而在单纯复形上建立统一的框架，扩展经典结果到更一般的设置。

Method: 定义了序列m-邻近距离作为多单纯映射间的组合不变量，推广了已有的邻近距离概念；系统研究了其在强同伦型、复合、范畴积和重心细分下的行为；进一步定义了序列m-离散拓扑复杂度，并利用序列m-邻近距离对其进行刻画。最后通过建立m-单纯LS范畴与m-离散序列拓扑复杂度之间的不等式来验证其理论价值。

Result: 证明了序列m-邻近距离和序列m-离散拓扑复杂度是强同伦型不变量；用序列m-邻近距离刻画了它们以及m-单纯LS范畴；建立了m-单纯LS范畴与m-离散序列拓扑复杂度之间的不等式关系。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将拓扑复杂度的经典理论推广到了单纯和m维框架下，为组合拓扑复杂度理论提供了新的工具和不变量，并建立了一系列重要的不等式和刻画结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the notion of sequential $m$-contiguity distance for finitely many simplicial maps as a higher analogue of contiguity distance. This invariant generalizes both higher contiguity distance and $m$-contiguity distance, and provides a combinatorial counterpart of sequential $m$-homotopic distance. We investigate its fundamental properties, including invariance under strong homotopy type, behaviour under compositions, categorical products, and barycentric subdivision.
  Moreover, we define sequential $m$-discrete topological complexity of simplicial complexes. As applications, we characterise this invariant (along with $m$-simplicial LS category) in terms of sequential $m$-contiguity distance and prove that they are invariants of strong homotopy type. Furthermore, we establish inequalities relating $m$-simplicial LS category and $m$-discrete sequential topological complexity, extending classical results from topological complexity theory to the simplicial and $m$-dimensional setting.

</details>


### [284] [A family completion theorem for tempered cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02390)
*Leonard Tokic*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在定向的P-可除群G（定义在诺特E∞-环R上）的完备化理论中，对有限群G及其子群族F的R(G)G-模族完备化等价于在理想I_G(F)处的代数完备化。对于KU上的μ_{P∞}，这推广了Adams-Haeberly-Jackowski-May的族完备化定理和Atiyah-Segal完备化定理。主要输入是温度特征栈G{BG}上点的支撑理论，推广了Segal对复表示环素谱的分析，并证明了该结果在局部诺特几何基栈上的版本，可应用于真等变拓扑模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Atiyah-Segal完备化定理和Adams-Haeberly-Jackowski-May的族完备化定理统一推广到更一般的定向P-可除群和诺特E∞-环的框架下，建立族完备化与代数完备化之间的等价关系，并发展一般化的支撑理论以理解温度特征栈的几何结构。

Method: (1) 构造温度特征栈G{BG}上的支撑理论，类比Segal对复表示环素谱的分析；(2) 证明温度特征栈上每一点的支撑恰好是G的某个交换子群的一个共轭类；(3) 证明被F内子群支撑的点恰好是仿射化映射下V(I_G(F))的原像；(4) 利用该支撑理论建立族完备化与代数完备化的等价性；(5) 将结果推广到局部诺特几何基栈上的版本，用开子栈替代理想，适用于真等变拓扑模形式。

Result: 得到了族完备化等价于代数完备化的定理；对KU上的μ_{P∞}情形恢复并统一了AHJM族完备化定理和Atiyah-Segal完备化定理；建立了温度特征栈的支撑理论（支撑为单一交换子群共轭类）；证明了基栈版本的结果并应用于真等变拓扑模形式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的完备化定理框架，将经典的表示环完备化结果推广到一般的定向P-可除群与温度特征栈的设定，并通过支撑理论揭示了代数完备化与族完备化之间的深层几何联系，同时为等变拓扑模形式等更一般的场景提供了应用。

Abstract: Let ${\mathbb{G}}$ be an oriented $\mathbb{P}$-divisible group over a noetherian $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$, let $G$ be a finite group, and let $\mathcal{F}$ be a family of subgroups of $G$. We show that completion of $R({\mathbb{G}})_G$-modules at $\mathcal{F}$ agrees with algebraic completion at the ideal $I_{\mathbb{G}}(\mathcal{F})=\bigcap_{H\in\mathcal{F}}\mathrm{ker} (π_0R({\mathbb{G}})^{ G}\to π_0R({\mathbb{G}})^{ H}).$ For ${\mathbb{G}}=μ_{\mathbb{P}^\infty}$ over $\mathrm{KU}$ this recovers the family completion theorem of Adams, Haeberly, Jackowski, and May, and for the trivial family the classical Atiyah-Segal completion theorem. The main input is a theory of support for points of the tempered character stack ${\mathbb{G}}\{\mathbb{B} G\}$, in the spirit of Segal's analysis of the prime spectrum of the complex representation ring: we show that the support of a point is a single conjugacy class of abelian subgroups of $G$, and that the points supported inside $\mathcal{F}$ are exactly the preimage of $V(I_{\mathbb{G}}(\mathcal{F}))$ under the affinization map. We also prove a version over locally noetherian geometric base stacks, in which the ideal is replaced by an open substack of ${\mathbb{G}}({\mathbb{B}} G)$, the analogue over such a base of $\mathrm{Spec}\,R({\mathbb{G}})^{ G}$, and which applies for instance to genuine equivariant topological modular forms.

</details>


### [285] [Colimits in Oriented Category Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02592)
*David Gepner,Hadrian Heine*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种新的"定向余极限"（oriented colimits）理论，用于修正高阶范畴论中lax余极限的缺陷。作者引入与Gray张量积相容的Grothendieck构造版本，证明其在笛卡尔纤维化和$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴预层之间建立了丰富等价，并将定向余极限应用于高维伴随关系、高阶主丛分类以及Quillen定理A和B的高阶推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: lax余极限虽然在代数视角下比通常的（同伦）余极限更强大，但在几何视角下并非正确的胶合概念——它与范畴维数、Gray张量积以及其他基本几何操作不相容。因此需要一种新的余极限概念，既能纠正lax余极限的缺陷，又能在低维情形与lax余极限一致。

Method: 本文引入了与Gray张量积相容的Grothendieck构造新版本，并利用它研究定向余极限。该Grothendieck构造在笛卡尔纤维化与$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴预层之间建立了Gray张量积丰富等价。定向余极限同时推广了lax余极限和Gray张量积，其与lax余极限的差异类似于Gray张量积与笛卡尔积的差异。

Result: 证明了定向余极限是高等范畴论中多种基本构造的正确推广（这些构造不是lax余极限而是定向余极限的实例）。作为应用，分类了高阶范畴主丛，用双笛卡尔纤维化表示高维伴随，并获得了Quillen定理A和B的高阶范畴版本。

Conclusion: 定向余极限在高阶范畴论中比lax余极限具有更强的几何适切性，提供了更自然的胶合框架，并在主丛分类、伴随表示和Quillen定理推广等多个关键应用中展现出优势。

Abstract: In higher category theory, lax colimits are often understood to be a more useful and powerful generalization of usual (homotopy) colimits, which can be recovered from the lax colimit by a suitable localization. However, lax colimits do not provide the correct notion of gluing from the geometric perspective. Indeed, they are incompatible with the notion of categorical dimension, the Gray tensor product, and other basic geometric operations. In this paper, we develop the theory of oriented colimits, which correct the defects of lax colimits, and agree with lax colimits in dimension less than or equal to one.
  In order to study oriented colimits, we introduce a version of the Grothendieck construction which is compatible with enrichment in the Gray tensor product. We prove that the Grothendieck construction induces an equivalence between cartesian fibrations and presheaves of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories, which is enriched in the Gray tensor product of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories. Oriented colimits simultaneously generalize the concept of lax colimits and the Gray tensor product, and differ from lax colimits in much the same way in which the Gray tensor product differs from the cartesian product. We demonstrate the necessity of oriented colimits by showing that various fundamental constructions in higher category theory fail to be lax colimits but are instances of oriented colimits.
  As applications, we classify higher-categorical principal bundles, represent higher dimensional adjunctions by bicartesian fibrations of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories, and obtain higher categorical versions of Quillen's Theorems A and B, which admit very natural formulations in our framework.

</details>

### [286] [Search for a top-philic Z' boson decaying into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair in a final state with jets and an electron or muon in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01251)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）中对top-philic Z'玻色子的搜索。Z'玻色子与顶夸克对（tt̄）联合产生并衰变为tt̄，搜索覆盖0.5-3 TeV质量范围、相对宽度为4%、10%、20%和50%的信号假设。采用机器学习算法识别Lorentz-boosted顶夸克合并形成的大半径喷注，以两个最高横动量顶夸克候选的不变质量分布作为判别变量。结果与标准模型背景预言一致，在95%置信水平上设置了Z'玻色子产生截面的上限，这是目前对该粒子最严格的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多超出标准模型的理论（如复合希格斯模型、大额外维度模型等）预言存在仅与顶夸克耦合的Z'玻色子（top-philic Z'）。这种粒子对理解顶夸克质量层级问题和自然性问题具有重要意义，但目前尚未被实验搜索到，需要在高能对撞机数据中进行针对性搜索。

Method: 利用CMS探测器2016-2018年采集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）。Z'玻色子与tt̄联合产生并衰变为tt̄。由于Z'质量较大，衰变产生的顶夸克是Lorentz-boosted的，其强子衰变产物合并为大半径喷注（large-radius jets）。采用机器学习算法鉴别此类喷注。以两个最高横动量顶夸克候选的不变质量分布作为判别变量，覆盖0.5-3 TeV质量范围和4%、10%、20%、50%四种相对固有宽度。

Result: 搜索结果与标准模型背景预言一致，未发现显著的超出。在95%置信水平上对Z'玻色子的产生截面设置了上限（针对每种衰变宽度分别作为质量函数给出）。这些是迄今对top-philic Z'玻色子存在性最严格的约束。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了对top-philic Z'玻色子的搜索灵敏度和质量覆盖范围，利用机器学习提升了对boosted顶夸克喷注的鉴别能力。尽管未发现新粒子信号，但获得的排除上限显著缩小了该模型可能的参数空间，为未来更高能量的对撞机实验（如HL-LHC）提供了重要的参考基准。

Abstract: A search for a top-philic Z' boson in a final state with jets and an electron or muon is presented. The search is based on a sample of proton-proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC during 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The top-philic Z' boson is produced in association with a top-antitop quark pair ($\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$) and decays into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair, as it couples exclusively to top quarks. The analysis aims to identify a heavy Z' boson that produces Lorentz-boosted top quarks, whose hadronic decay products are merged into large-radius jets. A machine-learning algorithm is employed to identify such jets. The distribution of the invariant mass of the two top quark candidates with the highest transverse momentum is used as the discriminant variable in a Z' boson mass range of 0.5$-$3 TeV, with intrinsic widths of 4, 10, 20, and 50% relative to its mass. The results obtained are found to be in agreement with the standard model background prediction. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the production cross section of the Z' boson, for each of the decay widths as a function of its mass. These results represent the most stringent constraints to date on the existence of a top-philic Z' boson.

</details>


### [287] [Combination of vector boson scattering measurements in leptonic final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01289)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了CMS探测器在质心能量13 TeV下收集的138 fb^-1质子-质子对撞数据中，对矢量玻色子散射测量进行统计组合分析。事件选择要求包含两个矢量玻色子（至少一个轻子衰变）及至少两个高不变质量和较大赝快度间隔的额外喷注。信号率在同号WW、异号WW以及WZ和ZZ通道中提取并与标准模型预言比较，所有结果与标准模型一致。此外，还进行了电荷分离测量以敏感于产生电荷不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过统计组合方式将所有矢量玻色子散射通道纳入统一框架进行全局约束，以提高对标准模型潜在偏离的灵敏度，并利用电荷分离测量来探测W玻色子产生电荷不对称性。

Method: 基于CMS探测器2016-2018年采集的138 fb^-1数据，选择包含两个矢量玻色子（至少一个轻子衰变）且带有至少两个高不变质量和大赝快度间隔喷注的事件；对同号WW、异号WW、WZ和ZZ通道进行信号率提取，采用统计组合方法在相干框架内同时约束所有通道，并进行电荷分离的W玻色子测量。

Result: 统计组合提高了超越各单独输入的灵敏度，所有通道的信号率结果均与标准模型预言一致。电荷分离测量提供了对W玻色子产生电荷不对称性的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该组合分析在相干框架下对矢量玻色子散射提供了全局视角，所有结果与标准模型预言相符，未发现超出标准模型的偏离证据。

Abstract: A statistical combination of vector boson scattering measurements in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV is presented. The analysis is based on a dataset corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, collected with the CMS detector in 2016$-$2018. Events are selected to contain two vector bosons, with at least one decaying leptonically, and at least two additional jets with a high invariant mass and a large pseudorapidity separation. Signal rates are extracted for same-sign WW, opposite-sign WW, as well as for WZ and ZZ channels, and are compared with the standard model predictions. The combination provides a global view of vector boson scattering by simultaneously constraining all channels in a coherent framework, improving sensitivity beyond individual inputs, with all results in agreement with the standard model predictions. In addition, a charge-separated measurement is performed in which W bosons are distinguished according to their electric charge, allowing sensitivity to the production charge asymmetry.

</details>


### [288] [Long-Delayed Afterpulse Measurement of JUNO 20-inch Photomultiplier Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01943)
*Xiaojie Luo,Cailian Jiang,Haojie Dong,Yuduo Guan,Gaosong Li,Zhonghua Qin,Zhenning Qu,Junyu Shao,Liangjian Wen,Zeyuan Yu,Boyi Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对江门中微子实验（JUNO）中高能量宇宙线缪子引发PMT后脉冲干扰延迟物理信号的问题，系统测量了两种20英寸PMT（打拿极型和微通道板型）的长延迟后脉冲特性。通过1.8ms波形窗口和滑窗读出策略将时间研究扩展至20ms，发现两种PMT存在显著不同的长延迟分量，后脉冲产额在10^-3量级且随主光强近似线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在JUNO这类大规模液体闪烁体探测器中，高强度事件（如宇宙线缪子）会诱发PMT后脉冲，这些后脉冲可能与延迟物理信号（如中微子信号）发生干扰。为了准确评估和建模这种仪器本底，需要系统表征不同结构PMT的长延迟后脉冲特性。

Method: 对JUNO使用的两种20英寸PMT（打拿极PMT和MCP-PMT）进行长延迟后脉冲测量：先在1.8ms波形窗口内表征后脉冲时间分布，再利用滑动窗口读出策略将观测时间扩展至20ms，比较两种PMT在不同时间尺度上的后脉冲组分差异。

Result: 两种PMT表现出明显不同的长延迟后脉冲结构：打拿极PMT在约260μs处有一个宽峰；MCP-PMT在约90μs处有显著峰、约550μs处有附加组分，以及一个更小的毫秒级宽分布组分。微秒级后脉冲产额在主光电子10^-3量级，且随主光强近似线性增加。

Conclusion: 高强度事件后的累积延迟活性不可忽略，这些定量结果为PMT响应表征以及高精度中微子实验中延迟关联本底的精确建模提供了关键输入数据。

Abstract: In large-scale liquid scintillator detectors such as the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), high-intensity events like cosmic muons induce photomultiplier tube (PMT) afterpulses that can interfere with the analysis of delayed physics signals. To systematically evaluate this instrumental background, we present a dedicated measurement of long-delayed afterpulses in two types of JUNO 20-inch PMTs: a dynode-based PMT and a microchannel-plate (MCP) PMT. The afterpulse time profiles were first characterized within a direct 1.8~ms waveform window and were further extended to 20~ms using a sliding-window readout strategy. Distinct long-delayed components are observed, revealing a strong dependence on the PMT multiplication structure. The dynode PMT exhibits a broad afterpulse component peaking at approximately 260~$μ$s, whereas the MCP-PMT shows a pronounced peak around 90~$μ$s, an additional component around 550~$μ$s, and a much smaller, broadly distributed millisecond-scale component. For the microsecond-scale components, the afterpulse yield per primary photoelectron is at the $10^{-3}$ level in the selected delayed windows and increases approximately linearly with the primary light intensity. The accumulated delayed activity can therefore become non-negligible following high-intensity events. These quantitative findings provide critical inputs for PMT response characterization and for the accurate modeling of delayed correlated backgrounds in high-precision neutrino experiments.

</details>


### [289] [Construction, commissioning, and performance of the ENUBET demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02164)
*F. Acerbi,I. Angelis,L. Bomben,M. Bonesini,F. Bramati,A. Branca,C. Brizzolari,G. Brunetti,G. T. Burton,S. Capelli,M. Capitani,S. Carturan,M. G. Catanesi,S. Cecchini,N. Charitonidis,F. Cindolo,J. Cogan,G. Cogo,G. Collazuol,D. D'Ago,F. Dal Corso,C. Delogu,G. De Rosa,S. Dolan,A. Falcone,M. Feltre,A. Gola,D. Guffanti,L. Halić,F. Iacob,C. Jollet,A. Kallitsopoulou,B. Kliček,Y. Kudenko,Ch. Lampoudis,M. Laveder,P. Legou,S. Levorato,A. Longhin,L. Ludovici,E. Lutsenko,L. Magaletti,G. Mandrioli,S. Marangoni,A. Margotti,V. Mascagna,M. Mattiazzi,N. Mauri,J. McElwee,L. Meazza,A. Meregaglia,M. Mezzetto,L. Munteanu,M. Pari,T. Papaevangelou,E. G. Parozzi,L. Pasqualini,G. Paternoster,L. Patrizii,M. Perrin-Terrin,M. Pozzato,M. Prest,F. Pupilli,E. Radicioni,A. C. Ruggeri,G. Saibene,D. Sampsonidis,C. Scian,L. Silvestrin,G. Sirri,M. Stipčević,M. Tenti,F. Terranova,M. Torti,S. E. Tzamarias,E. Vallazza,C. Vallée*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了ENUBET仪表化衰变隧道（Demonstrator）最终全尺寸样机的构建与测试结果，该样机基于铁吸收体和闪烁体瓦片（配波长位移光纤和SiPM读出）的纵向采样量能器技术。在2022-2024年CERN东区的束流测试中，验证了该技术的完全可扩展性，评估了通道响应、电子能量分辨率、线性度和粒子鉴别能力（最高5 GeV），并与GEANT4模拟进行了对比，结果满足中微子监测需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENUBET项目需要通过衰变隧道中的轻子鉴别来实现中微子监测，因此需要构建并测试一个全尺寸的仪表化衰变隧道样机（Demonstrator），以验证其技术的可扩展性、性能指标是否满足设计需求，并验证完整模拟的准确性。

Method: 构建了基于铁吸收体和闪烁体瓦片（配波长位移光纤与SiPM读出）的纵向采样量能器全尺寸样机，在CERN东区设施进行了2022-2024年三次束流测试活动，使用最高5 GeV的带电粒子束评估逐通道响应、能量分辨率、线性度与粒子鉴别能力，并将结果与GEANT4模拟预测进行对比。

Result: 测试结果证明该纵向采样量能器技术具有完全可扩展性；电子能量分辨率、线性和粒子鉴别能力满足衰变隧道中通过轻子鉴别进行中微子监测的要求；实验数据与GEANT4模拟预测一致，验证了ENUBET全模拟的正确性。虽然发现了一些局限性，但可在未来开发中轻松解决。

Conclusion: Demonstrator样机验证了ENUBET仪表化衰变隧道技术的可行性，满足中微子监测需求，确认了全模拟的可靠性。发现的局限性不影响当前设计目标，可在后续开发中予以改进。

Abstract: A final full-size prototype of the instrumented decay tunnel of ENUBET, referred to as the Demonstrator, was constructed and its performance was tested during the 2022, 2023 and 2024 beam test campaigns at the CERN East Area facility. We demonstrate the full scalability of this specialized longitudinal sampling calorimeter technology, based on iron absorbers and scintillator tiles readout by wavelength-shifting fibers and SiPMs. In addition, we evaluate the channel-by- channel response and the performance in terms of electron energy resolution, linearity, and particle identification capabilities, using charged particle beams with energies up to 5 GeV. A comparison with the predictions of a GEANT4 simulation of the prototype is also presented. The results meet the requirements for neutrino monitoring through lepton identification in the decay tunnel and validate the ENUBET full simulation. Although some limitations have been identified, they can be readily addressed in future developments.

</details>
